Abstract: Quantum information complexity (QIC), introduced by Touchette [Touchette, STOC 2015], has been shown to be one of the most powerful methods for proving quantum communication complexity and has also been shown to be equal to amortized quantum communication complexity. Unfortunately, QIC is generally hard to analyze because it is a sum of quantum conditional mutual information terms, which are difficult to estimate. In this work, we introduce a new variational approach, the information potential, for analyzing QIC and proving quantum communication lower bounds inspired by the resolvent representation for quantum relative entropy. This approach connects the QIC of individual messages to a quadratic form, making it more amenable and thus enables us to bound the cumulative positive increments of the potential throughout an interactive quantum protocol by its QIC. Lower bounds on the growth of the potential therefore translate into lower bounds on both QIC and quantum communication complexity.
  As an application, we give an optimal $Ω(1/r)$ lower bound on the QIC of the two-bit $\mathsf{AND}$ function as well as an optimal $Ω(n/r)$ lower bound on the quantum communication complexity of $r$-round Set-Disjointness, answering an open problem in~[Braverman, Garg, Ko, Mao, Touchette FOCS 2015]. Moreover, we further prove a direct-sum theorem for bounded-round quantum communication complexity of Set Disjointness. With the tight bound on the QIC of $\mathsf{AND}$ function, we further establish a nearly tight tradeoff for the asymmetric quantum communication complexity of $\mathsf{Set} \mathsf{Disjointness}$: $(q_A+1)(q_B+1)=Ω(n)$, where $q_A$ and $q_B$ denote the total numbers of qubits sent by Alice and Bob, respectively.

</details>


### [93] [Complete product-state contact set and optimality of the canonical three-qubit Shifts witness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39332)
*Bing-Dong Wan,Yu-Chen Guo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了三量子比特Shifts不可扩展积基（UPB）投影算符最小积态期望值 λ=1-3√6/8 的全部等号集，证明其恰好由8条积态射线组成（2条循环不变+2个三元循环轨道），并据此证明该投影算符对应的纠缠见证是展开准则意义下最优的，进而给出白噪声纠缠阈值 p<2-3√6/4≈16.29%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三量子比特Shifts UPB投影算符的最小积态期望值及4个达到该值的积态已知，但完整的等号集（即所有达到最小值的复积态）此前未被确定。等号集的完整刻画决定了相应投影算符是否能作为纠缠见证满足展开准则（spanning criterion）并达到最优性，因此需要完整的等号分类而非仅给出部分极值态。

Method: 证明结合三部分：(1) 精确相位分析；(2) 对全部28个有限实海森堡/实驻点进行Gröbner基分类；(3) 对射影边界（projective boundaries）的完整处理。由此在全部复积态空间上确定等号集。

Result: 等号集恰由8条积态射线构成：2条在三量子比特循环置换下不变，另2个为规模为3的循环轨道。关键之处在于这8条接触射线张成整个三量子比特希尔伯特空间，而先前已知的4条射线不能张成。由此证明相应投影算符纠缠见证满足展开准则，并在“减去任何非零半正定算符仍保持块正性”的意义下最优。作为推论，对白噪声族 ρ(p)=(1-p)ρ_Shifts+p I/8，同一见证在 p<2-3√6/4≈16.29% 时探测到纠缠。

Conclusion: 三量子比特Shifts UPB投影算符的等号集被完整确定，共8条积态射线，其张成性与最优性均成立；该结果优于此前仅有的4条射线结论，并为白噪声模型给出约16.29%的纠缠探测阈值。

Abstract: For the three-qubit Shifts unextendible product basis (UPB), the minimum product-state expectation value $λ_{\mathrm{Shifts}}=1-3\sqrt6/8$ of the UPB projector is known, and four attaining product states were exhibited previously. We determine the complete equality set over all complex product states and prove that it consists of exactly eight product rays: two rays invariant under cyclic permutation of the three qubits and two cyclic orbits of size three. The proof combines exact phase analysis, a Gröbner-basis classification of all 28 finite real stationary points, and a complete treatment of the projective boundaries. Crucially, the eight contact rays span the full three-qubit Hilbert space, whereas the four previously known rays do not. The complete equality classification therefore establishes that the associated projector witness satisfies the spanning criterion and is optimal in the sense that no nonzero positive semidefinite operator can be subtracted while preserving block positivity. As a corollary, for the white-noise family $ρ(p)=(1-p)ρ_{\mathrm{Shifts}}+p\mathbb I/8$, where $ρ_{\mathrm{Shifts}}$ is the complementary-projector Shifts state, the same witness detects entanglement for $p<2-3\sqrt6/4\approx16.29\%$.

</details>


### [94] [Constant-Overhead Injection into Quantum Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39345)
*Louis Golowich,Venkatesan Guruswami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了首个具有常数空间和时间开销的量子纠错码态注入（编码）与弹出（解码）容错方案，可在电路级局部随机噪声下以常数概率保真地工作，并支持单次容错纠错与码态制备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算中，将裸物理量子比特容错地注入（编码）到纠错码块中、以及将码态弹出（解码）回物理比特，是连接量子存储器与计算的关键操作。已有方案在空间或时间开销上往往非最优（非常数），因此需要构造同时具备常数空间开销与常数时间开销的容错注入/弹出机制。

Method: 1) 采用经典 LDPC 码（Spielman 线性时间可编码码的简化版本）的高维超图乘积来构造量子码，得到常数码率的量子纠错码族；2) 由于所得乘积码仅对非均匀概率的物理错误具备韧性，进一步通过与大、小不同规模的内码进行级联（concatenation），获得对均匀噪声的容错性；3) 所有 gadget 均为常数量子线路深度（即单次 single-shot），所需物理量子比特数随逻辑量子比特数线性增长，前提是允许运行多项式规模的无噪声经典旁路电路。

Result: 构造出首个已知的容错态注入方案，可在常数空间与时间开销下将裸物理量子比特容错编码进码块，并可反向将码态弹出为裸物理量子比特；在电路级局部随机噪声下，注入/弹出过程成功且每个量子比特仅以不可避免的小常数概率被破坏。同时给出了在该噪声模型下进行容错纠错与码态制备的方法，所有 gadget 均可单次实现、常数线路深度，物理比特数线性增长。

Conclusion: 通过高维超图乘积构造常数码率量子码并结合级联内码，作者实现了对均匀噪声的容错，给出了首个常数开销的单次容错态注入/弹出与纠错方案，为可扩展容错量子计算提供了新途径。

Abstract: We construct the first known fault-tolerant scheme for injecting states into quantum error-correcting codes with constant space and time overhead. That is, we construct a family of constant-rate quantum error-correcting codes for which a set of bare physical qubits can be injected, i.e. fault-tolerantly encoded, into a code block. Similarly, a code state can be ejected, i.e. fault-tolerantly decoded, back into bare physical qubits. We show that these injection and ejection procedures succeed under circuit-level locally stochastic noise, while incurring just a small constant probability of corrupting each qubit, which is unavoidable for bare physical qubits. We also show how to perform fault-tolerant error correction and code-state preparation under locally stochastic noise. All of our gadgets can be implemented with constant quantum circuit depth (i.e. are single-shot), and with a number of physical qubits growing linearly with the number of logical qubits, assuming the ability to run polynomial-sized noiseless classical circuits on the side.
  We construct our quantum codes by taking a high-dimensional hypergraph product of classical LDPC codes, which in turn are a simplified version of Spielman's linear-time encodable codes (STOC'95). As our resulting product codes are only resilient to physical errors occurring with non-uniform probabilities across qubits, we then show how to concatenate with inner codes of various sizes to obtain fault-tolerance against uniform noise.

</details>


### [95] [Efficient Learning of Fermionic Magic States under Free-Fermion Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39372)
*Jiwon Heo,Myeongjin Shin,Changhun Oh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作证明：在未知粒子数守恒自由费米子演化下，一类费米子魔法态可被高效学习；利用粒子约化密度矩阵（RDM）的谱结构恢复隐藏的块分解，算法仅需单拷贝测量且具有多项式样本与计算复杂度；并证明所需 RDM 阶数是必要的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在未知的粒子数守恒自由费米子演化下，一类费米子魔法态是否能被高效学习，并探讨非高斯块数量很大时高效学习是否仍然可行，同时确定重构所需 RDM 的最小阶数。

Method: 利用粒子约化密度矩阵（RDM）的谱结构，将单个块的贡献与涉及多个块的贡献分离，从而恢复隐藏的块结构；算法采用单拷贝测量，在每块粒子数上界固定时，以多项式样本复杂度和经典计算复杂度重构状态的紧凑描述；重构只需用到不超过该上界的 RDM。

Result: 在固定每块粒子数上界下，算法可在无块分解与演化先验知识的情况下，以指定保真度和高概率恢复状态的紧凑描述，样本与经典计算复杂度均为多项式，测量为单拷贝。进一步证明该 RDM 阶数一般是必要的：家族中存在两个正交态在所有更低阶 RDM 上完全相同。

Conclusion: 大量非高斯块可以与高效状态学习兼容；RDM 阶数在重构中起到了本质作用，且该阶数在一般情况下不可降低。

Abstract: We establish efficient learning for a family of fermionic magic states under unknown number-conserving free-fermion evolution. Each input block has a definite particle number and is a superposition of Fock states, with occupied mode sets disjoint both within and across blocks. The key idea is to exploit the spectral structure of particle reduced density matrices (RDMs) to separate contributions from individual blocks from those involving several blocks, allowing us to reconstruct the hidden block structure. For a fixed upper bound on the particle number per block, our algorithm uses single-copy measurements and polynomial sample and classical computational complexity to recover a compact description of the state with prescribed fidelity and high probability, without prior knowledge of the block decomposition or the evolution. RDMs up to this upper bound suffice for reconstruction. We further show that this RDM order is necessary in general: two orthogonal states in the family can have identical RDMs at every lower order. These results show that an extensive number of non-Gaussian blocks can be compatible with efficient state learning.

</details>


### [96] [Spectrally Selective Charging of an Interacting Quantum Battery via an Anharmonic Mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39393)
*Sujay Mondal,Anumita Mukhopadhyay,Siddhartha Dutta,Abhijit Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一种有限时间量子电池充电架构：驱动二能级工作系统通过弱非简谐三能级中介体，将非平衡资源输运至相互作用双量子比特电池，实现谱选择性充电与循环热力学闭合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限时间条件下，如何将非平衡资源高效、选择性地转移至量子电池，并保持循环级能量闭合，是量子热力学与量子电池研究的关键问题。现有方案通常难以在同一架构中兼顾非平衡资源生成、谱选择性、多能级相干充电与热力学循环闭合。

Method: 构建驱动二能级工作系统—弱非简谐三能级中介体—相互作用双量子比特电池的充电架构；利用有限时间驱动产生相干与ergotropy，通过激发数守恒划分动力学分区；利用中介体跃迁的谱选择性实现单激发近完全转移与交叉共振双激发级联；通过失谐扫描考察谱容忍度；分析全混合态动力学下的能量与ergotropy转移；并以有限时间压缩、热重置和开关功闭合循环能量平衡。

Result: 中介体下跃迁可实现近乎完全的单激发转移，且残余中介体能量可忽略；初始中介体加载可激活交叉共振双激发级联，导向电池双激发态，但因有效三态链集体矩阵元不等，转移效率略有降低。失谐扫描给出谱容忍范围；全混合态动力学显示能量转移与ergotropy转移具有不同特征曲线。有限时间压缩、热重置与开关功使循环级能量平衡闭合至数值精度。

Conclusion: 该方案在单一架构中整合了非平衡资源生成、谱选择性、相干多能级充电与热力学循环闭合，为有限时间量子电池的谱选择性、多激发充电及循环热力学一致性提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We investigate a finite-time quantum-battery charging architecture in which a driven two-level working system transfers nonequilibrium resources to an interacting two-qubit battery through a weakly anharmonic three-level mediator. Finite-time driving generates coherence and ergotropy, while excitation-number conservation organizes the transfer into distinct dynamical sectors. The mediator then provides spectrally selective charging channels: its lower transition enables nearly complete single-excitation transfer with negligible residual mediator energy, whereas initial mediator loading activates a cross-resonant two-excitation cascade toward the doubly excited battery state. The latter exhibits a modest reduction in transfer efficiency due to the unequal collective matrix elements of the effective three-state chain. Detuning scans quantify the spectral tolerance, and the full mixed-state dynamics reveals distinct energy- and ergotropy-transfer profiles. Finite-time compression, thermal reset, and switching work close the cycle-level energy balance to numerical precision. The resulting scheme combines nonequilibrium resource generation, spectral selectivity, coherent multilevel charging, and thermodynamic cycle closure within a single architecture.

</details>


### [97] [Logical Operator Decomposition for Distance Analysis of Bivariate Bicycle Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39395)
*Mohammad Rowshan,Simon Devitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过代数方法研究BB量子码的Z-逻辑商K/S，建立短正合序列并给出逻辑基、维数公式与分量距离恒等式d_Z=min(d_A,d_C)，证明r_A=r_C=k/2，并借助平移锚定簇搜索确定六个标准BB码的最小距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元自行车（BB）量子码是重要的有限长量子LDPC码族，但其最小距离通常只能数值确定，无法直接由定义多项式读出。作者希望建立一种代数框架，从定义多项式出发解析地刻画逻辑结构与距离。

Method: 在F_2[x,y]/(x^ℓ-1,y^m-1)上研究Z-逻辑商K/S，构造一个短正合序列，其核为annihilator商、余核为colon商；利用有限群代数的Frobenius结构分析两个分量的秩；给出下界判据（最小权逻辑算子的任何真子集均有非零syndrome），并据此设计平移锚定簇搜索算法。

Result: 短正合序列给出显式逻辑基、维数公式和分量距离恒等式d_Z=min(d_A,d_C)；证明对所有BB码（含重根情形）均有r_A=r_C=k/2；发现代数分量与最轻代表的支撑形状不同（annihilator类可有更轻的双块代表，colon类可有一侧最小值）；下界性质在colon分量内保持而在annihilator分量内不成立；算法证实长度18至288的六个标准BB码距离分别为4,6,10,10,12,18，并枚举出全部最小权逻辑算子；普查显示[[108,8,10]]是六者中唯一距离仅在单一分量达到的码，其中d_C=10、d_A=12。

Conclusion: 该工作将BB码的最小距离从纯数值问题转化为可由定义多项式刻画的代数问题，给出逻辑基、维数、分量距离的完整结构，并提供可证明距离并枚举最小权逻辑算子的搜索方法，为BB码的距离分析提供了系统工具。

Abstract: Bivariate bicycle (BB) quantum codes are a prominent finite-length family of quantum low-density parity-check codes, but their minimum distance is usually established numerically rather than read from the defining polynomials. We study the $Z$-logical quotient $K/S$ over $\mathbb F_2[x,y]/(x^\ell-1,y^m-1)$ and show that it fits into a short exact sequence with an annihilator quotient as kernel and a colon quotient as cokernel. The sequence gives an explicit logical basis, a dimension formula, and a componentwise distance identity $d_Z=\min(d_{\mathcal A},d_{\mathcal C})$. Using the Frobenius structure of the finite group algebra, we prove $r_{\mathcal A}=r_{\mathcal C}=k/2$ for every BB code, including repeated-root cases. The algebraic component of a logical class is distinct from the support shape of its lightest representatives: an annihilator class can have a lighter two-block representative, and a colon class can have a one-sided minimum. For lower bounds we show that every proper subset of a minimum-weight logical operator has nonzero syndrome, and that this property persists inside the colon component but not inside the annihilator component. A translation-anchored cluster search built on it proves the distances $4,6,10,10,12,18$ of the six standard BB codes of lengths $18$ to $288$ and enumerates every minimum-weight logical operator. The resulting census shows that $[[108,8,10]]$ is the only one of the six whose distance is attained in a single component, with $d_{\mathcal C}=10$ and $d_{\mathcal A}=12$.

</details>


### [98] [Genuine Multipartite Nonlocality Is Fermionic Magic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39411)
*Jamal Slim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 自由费米子无论被多少方共享，其贝尔非局域性都等价于一对纠缠粒子所能承载的量，且从不具有真正的多体非局域性；费米宇称是其根本原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在探索自由费米子可经典模拟、而单个非高斯态即使其具备通用量子计算能力这一界限，是否同样约束量子非局域性，从而揭示非高斯性与多体非局域性之间的深层联系。

Method: 将自由费米子以费米量子比特形式度量，分析其贝尔非局域性；通过费米宇称论证其非局域性的上限；并在IBM处理器的48个量子比特上对真正的多体非局域性进行认证。

Result: 自由费米子携带的贝尔非局域性不超过一对纠缠粒子，且从不具有真正的多体非局域性；真正的多体非局域性在48个IBM量子比特上得到实验认证。

Conclusion: 真正的多体非局域性是通用费米子计算的资源，无法从任何平均场态产生，并已在48个量子比特的IBM处理器上得到认证。

Abstract: A system of free fermions can be simulated on a classical computer, and a single non-Gaussian state makes it a universal quantum computer. We show that the same line bounds quantum nonlocality. Measured as fermionic qubits, free fermions carry the Bell nonlocality of one entangled pair, no more, however many parties share them, and are never genuinely multipartite nonlocal. Fermion parity is the reason. Genuine multipartite nonlocality is thus the resource of universal fermionic computation, cannot arise from any mean-field state, and we certify it on $48$ qubits of IBM processors.

</details>


### [99] [Reservoir- and Measurement-free Microwave Initialization of Semiconductor Spin Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39415)
*Leon C. Camenzind,Ik Kyeong Jin,Akito Noiri,Kenta Takeda,Takashi Nakajima,Takashi Kobayashi,Seigo Tarucha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作展示了在工业制备的Si/SiGe量子点器件中，利用固定序列的微波与基带脉冲，实现无需储库和测量的自旋量子比特对初始化，中位成功率达99.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子处理器需要在大规模阵列中反复初始化量子比特，而半导体自旋量子比特的快速初始化通常依赖局部储库或测量反馈，这类专用基础设施随处理器扩展而难以分布，因此需要无需储库与测量的可扩展初始化方案。

Method: 在工业制备的Si/SiGe量子点器件中，对硅自旋量子比特对施加固定序列的微波与基带脉冲。奇数自旋宇称态弛豫至单态电荷态；被阻塞的偶数自旋宇称态经微波驱动穿越三重态流形，随后通过单-三重态混合与电荷杂化转换为单态。结合微波谱与时域测量，识别暗态限制的单周期转移及后续解阻塞动力学。

Result: 重复循环在采样制备态上产生单态相关电荷结果的中位概率为99.4%；交换谱独立验证了向目标|↑↓⟩操作态的映射。序列约需12 μs微波脉冲与混合驻留时间，并预计在改进器件条件下可实现亚微秒初始化。

Conclusion: 固定序列微波初始化可作为半导体自旋量子比特处理器的可扩展控制原语，其基于单-三重态物理，可适配具有合适泡利阻塞跃迁的平台。

Abstract: Scalable quantum processors require repeated qubit initialization throughout large arrays. In semiconductor spin qubits, fast initialization commonly relies on local reservoir access or measurement-based feedback, requiring dedicated infrastructure that becomes increasingly difficult to distribute as processors scale. Here, we demonstrate reservoir- and measurement-free initialization of a silicon spin-qubit pair in an industrially fabricated Si/SiGe quantum-dot device using a fixed sequence of microwave and baseband pulses. Odd spin-parity states relax to the singlet charge state, whereas blocked even spin-parity states are microwave-driven through the triplet manifold and subsequently converted to the singlet by singlet-triplet mixing and charge hybridization. Repeated cycles produce the singlet-associated charge outcome with a median probability of 99.4% across the sampled preparation states, while exchange spectroscopy independently verifies mapping to the target $|\uparrow\downarrow\rangle$ operational state. Microwave spectroscopy and time-domain measurements identify the dark-state-limited single-cycle transfer and the subsequent blockade-lifting dynamics that set the initialization time scale. The demonstrated pumping sequence uses approximately $12\,μ\mathrm{s}$ of microwave bursts and mixing dwells, while we project sub-microsecond initialization under improved device conditions. These results establish fixed-sequence microwave initialization as a scalable control primitive for semiconductor spin-qubit processors, based on singlet-triplet physics that can be adapted to platforms with suitable Pauli-blockade transitions.

</details>


### [100] [Parallel classical simulation of noisy shallow circuits: no quantum advantage in 1D](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39448)
*Robert Koenig,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对一维带退极化噪声的浅层量子电路，作者给出并行采样算法，并证明其输入/输出行为可被随机 AC⁰ 电路以常数误差再现，从而在一维情形排除了噪声浅层电路的量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维和三维的噪声浅层量子电路已被证明具有无条件量子优势（经典电路需深度 Ω(log n/log log n)）。作者希望考察一维（线）上带噪声的最近邻两比特门电路是否也具有类似优势，即噪声是否真正限制了其计算能力。

Method: 考虑 d 层最近邻两比特门作用于线性排列的 n 个量子比特，每层前每个比特以常数概率独立退极化。核心观察：退极化噪声把一维电路切断为独立的对数宽度片段，每段可精确并行采样。据此设计随机并行算法进行采样，并在常数深度下构造随机 AC⁰（多项式大小、常数深度、无界扇入 AND/OR 加 NOT）电路来再现电路的输入/输出行为。

Result: 采样算法在总变差误差 δ 内的并行运行时间为 2^{O(d)} log log(n/δ)，需 n·2^{O(d)} 次基本实数算术运算；无噪声时相同方法给出并行运行时间 O(log n) 的精确采样器。对常数深度，噪声电路的输入/输出行为在常数误差内可由随机 AC⁰ 电路再现；因此所有一维噪声常数深度量子电路可解的关系问题，也能以基本相同成功率由随机 AC⁰ 电路求解。这排除了二维、三维噪声浅层电路所具有的无条件量子优势在一维的成立。

Conclusion: 一维带退极化噪声的浅层量子电路在计算能力上并不优于随机 AC⁰ 经典电路，噪声使其量子优势消失；这与二维、三维噪声浅层电路存在无条件量子优势形成鲜明对比，凸显维度对噪声量子电路计算能力的关键作用。

Abstract: We consider quantum circuits consisting of $d$ layers of nearest-neighbor two-qubit gates acting on $n$ qubits arranged on a line, where every qubit is independently depolarized with a constant probability before each layer. We describe a randomized parallel algorithm which samples from the output distribution of any such circuit to within total variation error $δ$, with parallel runtime $2^{O(d)}\log\log(n/δ)$ and $n 2^{O(d)}$ elementary real-arithmetic operations. Without noise, the same approach gives an exact sampler with parallel runtime $O(\log n)$. For constant depth, we further show that the input/output behavior of the noisy quantum circuit is reproduced up to a constant error by a randomized $\mathsf{AC}^0$-circuit, that is, a Boolean circuit of polynomial size and constant depth with unbounded fan-in AND and OR gates and NOT gates. Consequently, every relation problem solved by a noisy constant-depth quantum circuit in one dimension is also solved, with essentially the same success probability, by a randomized $\mathsf{AC}^0$-circuit. This rules out, for noisy circuits in one dimension, the unconditional quantum advantage established for noisy shallow circuits in two and three dimensions, where polynomial-size classical circuits over the same gate set require depth $Ω(\log n/\log\log n)$. Our algorithm exploits the fact that depolarizing noise cuts a one-dimensional circuit into independent pieces of logarithmic width, each of which can be sampled exactly in parallel.

</details>


### [101] [From Wavefunction Regularity to Eigenvector Conditioning: Accuracy--Cost Trade-offs of Quantum Algorithms for Non-Hermitian Transcorrelated Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39452)
*Cheng-Lin Hong,Emiel Koridon,Paul K. Faehrmann,Thomas D. Kühne,Stefano Polla,Werner Dobrautz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过可解模型与二次量子化电子结构哈密顿量，研究transcorrelated(TC)方法中电子-电子库仑奇点导致的尖点结构、基组收敛、非正常哈密顿量的特征向量条件数与本征值敏感性，并推导能量误差渐近标度及查询复杂度上界改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-电子库仑奇点使电子波函数产生尖点结构，限制波函数正则性并减慢光滑基组展开收敛，需大基组才能达到目标精度。TC方法将短程行为并入哈密顿量以提升变换后波函数正则性、加速收敛至完备基组极限，但所得哈密顿量非厄米且非正常，限制标准量子算法直接适用；对这类非正常算子，若干近期量子算法复杂度显式依赖特征向量条件数与本征值敏感性，因此需理解这些量以评估算法复杂度与量子资源需求。

Method: 分两阶段：首先研究一个可解模型，在受控条件下隔离transcorrelation对基组收敛与特征向量条件数的影响，并考察两种不同的单参数Jastrow关联因子；推导渐近能量误差标度。随后将分析扩展到二次量子化电子结构哈密顿量，并使用粒子数扇区诊断区分目标扇区与辅助扇区的条件数。

Result: 在可解模型中，对一般固定参数，裸投影的渐近能量误差标度为$N^{-1/2}$，两种TC关联因子均为$N^{-3/2}$（$N$为基组大小）。此外，证明这种更快收敛可在渐近查询复杂度上界方面带来多项式改进。在二次量子化电子结构哈密顿量中，粒子数扇区诊断可区分目标扇区与辅助扇区条件数。

Conclusion: 本文为理解TC哈密顿量中精度-成本权衡的核心问题提供了框架，揭示transcorrelation如何改善基组收敛与特征向量条件数，并影响量子算法复杂度与资源需求。

Abstract: The electron--electron Coulomb singularity forces cusp structure in electronic wavefunctions. This cusp structure limits wavefunction regularity and slows the convergence of expansions in smooth bases, requiring large basis sets to reach a target accuracy. The transcorrelated (TC) method incorporates this short-range behavior into the Hamiltonian, thereby improving the regularity of the transformed wavefunction and accelerating convergence toward the complete-basis-set limit. The resulting Hamiltonian is non-Hermitian and non-normal, which limits the direct applicability of standard quantum algorithms. For such non-normal operators, the complexity of several recent quantum algorithms depends explicitly on quantities related to eigenvector conditioning and eigenvalue sensitivity. Understanding these quantities is essential for assessing both algorithmic complexity and quantum resource requirements. In this work, we address these issues in two stages. We first study a solvable model, which provides a controlled setting for isolating how transcorrelation affects basis convergence and eigenvector conditioning. Within this setting, we investigate two distinct one-parameter Jastrow correlators. We derive the asymptotic energy-error scaling: $N^{-1/2}$ for the bare projection and $N^{-3/2}$ for both TC correlators at generic fixed parameters, with $N$denoting the basis size. Furthermore, we show that this faster convergence can yield a polynomial improvement in the asymptotic query-complexity upper bound. We then extend the analysis to second-quantized electronic-structure Hamiltonians and use particle-number-sector diagnostics to distinguish target-sector from auxiliary-sector conditioning. Our work provides a framework for understanding the central accuracy--cost trade-off in TC Hamiltonians.

</details>


### [102] [Optimal Scaling of Unitary Design Formation in $U(1)$-Symmetric Random Circuits: A Bottleneck Slower than Charge Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39458)
*Toshihiro Yada*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了$U(1)$对称随机电路中酉$k$-设计形成速率的最优标度，发现其由双粒子相遇率而非单粒子输运率支配，证伪了此前的猜想，并给出使用对称破缺门的加速协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解守恒律下随机性的形成，对于刻画孤立量子系统中的混沌现象以及对称约束下的量子信息处理都很重要。$U(1)$对称随机电路提供了一个最小可处理模型，此前猜想酉设计的形成速率由电荷输运决定。然而，由于难以对对称设计形成速率给出紧下界，电荷输运是否确实决定最慢弛豫模式一直是悬而未决的问题。

Method: 作者通过确定$k\leq O(\sqrt{\log N})$时酉$k$-设计形成速率的最优标度，覆盖晶格、扩展图与全连接相互作用等多种电路几何。为推导匹配下界，提出了一种基于辅助子群集合的新证明技术。

Result: 对于$k\geq 2$，发现形成速率由双粒子相遇率（即两个随机行走者在给定电路几何中相遇的速率）支配。该相遇率在多种几何下参量上小于单粒子输运速率，从而证伪了先前猜想。所提出的匹配下界进一步确认相遇机制确实决定了设计形成速率。此外，通过使用对称破缺局部门可绕开该瓶颈，并给出了实现更快设计形成的显式协议。

Conclusion: 本文确立了守恒律下随机性形成的新图景，为刻画并高效生成对称约束随机性提供了指导原则。

Abstract: Understanding randomness formation under conservation laws is important both for characterizing chaotic phenomena in isolated quantum systems and for symmetry-constrained quantum information processing. $U(1)$-symmetric random circuits on an $N$-qubit system provide a minimal tractable setting for this problem, in which the formation rate of unitary designs was conjectured to be governed by charge transport in the underlying circuit geometry. However, whether charge transport indeed sets the slowest relaxation mode has remained an open problem, due to the difficulty in deriving a tight lower bound on the symmetric design formation rate. In this work, we resolve this problem by determining the optimal scaling of the unitary $k$-design formation rate for $k\leq O(\sqrt{\log N})$ across a wide range of circuit geometries, including lattices, expander graphs, and all-to-all interactions. In particular, for $k\geq 2$, we find that the formation rate is governed by a two-particle encounter rate---the rate at which two random walkers encounter each other in a given circuit geometry. This encounter rate is parametrically smaller than the single-particle transport rate in many geometries, thereby disproving the previous conjecture. We further establish that this encounter mechanism indeed sets the design formation rate by deriving its matching lower bound with a novel proof technique based on an auxiliary subgroup ensemble. Although this encounter mode imposes a robust bottleneck on design formation in $U(1)$-symmetric random circuits, we also show that this bottleneck can be circumvented by using symmetry-breaking local gates and provide an explicit protocol that achieves faster design formation. These results establish a new picture of randomness formation under conservation laws and provide guiding principles for characterizing and efficiently generating symmetry-constrained randomness.

</details>


### [103] [Generation of large-amplitude squeezed cat states with near-unity efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39482)
*Simon K. Yung,Matthew S. Winnel,Timothy C. Ralph,Jie Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种全光学方案，利用量子非破坏相互作用与光子数测量，以近单位概率制备大振幅猫态，且不后选择特定光子数结果，对光子损耗具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP编码是连续变量容错量子计算的重要候选，但光子架构中高质量资源态的制备是主要挑战，其可由大振幅猫态确定性合成。现有实验仅能制备中等尺度光学猫态，且方法高度概率性。

Method: 全光学方案，结合量子非破坏（QND）相互作用与光子数测量来制备大振幅猫态；不后选择特定光子数结果，所有测量结果均累加贡献于总光子数，重复协议直至达到所需阈值。

Result: 该方案能以近单位概率制备大振幅猫态，并在实际光子损耗水平（光学系统主要误差来源）下表现出鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了主动高斯操作在态制备中的能力，为利用玻色子码实现高效量子纠错铺平道路。

Abstract: The Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill encoding has emerged as a leading candidate for fault-tolerant quantum computation with continuous variables. For photonic architectures, the major challenge is preparing high-quality resource states, which can be deterministically synthesised from many large-amplitude cat states. Thus far, only modest-sized optical cat states have been prepared experimentally, and the implemented methods are highly probabilistic. We propose an all-optical scheme utilising quantum non-demolition interactions and photon-number measurements to prepare large-amplitude cat states with near-unity probability. Importantly, no particular photon-number outcome is postselected: all outcomes contribute to the accumulated photon number, with the protocol repeated until a required threshold is reached. We demonstrate that the scheme is robust to realistic levels of photon-loss, the dominant source of error in optical systems. Our results highlight the power of active Gaussian operations for state preparation and pave the way for efficient quantum error correction using bosonic codes.

</details>


### [104] [On quantum interactive proofs with a laconic prover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39495)
*Zihan Hu,Yupan Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究具有简洁证明者的两消息量子交互证明系统，定义类 QIP_{ℓ-bit}(2)，用多状态可区分性给出其完整刻画，证明 QSD 是其 1-bit 完全问题，并给出若干塌缩到 QSZK 或 BQP 的易处理情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典简洁证明者的交互证明可用对数证明者通信验证；量子两消息情形下即使单比特响应也包含 QSZK，而若限制验证者问题为经典公共硬币则该类塌缩为 BQP。作者希望系统刻画量子简洁证明者两消息系统的能力边界与结构。

Method: 引入类 QIP_{ℓ-bit}(2)（ℓ 为证明者响应长度），通过多状态可区分性（Multi-State Distinguishability）建立完整刻画；结合极化（polarization）与答案压缩技术分析易处理区间。

Result: 1. 给出 QIP_{ℓ-bit}(2) 的多状态可区分性完全刻画，QSD 为 QIP_bit-完全，且因 QSD 是 QSZK-难，故 ℓ≥2 时该类位于 QSZK“略上方”。2. 证明当 a(n)−b(n)≥1/O(log n) 时 QSD[a,b]（从而 QIP_bit[a,b]）属于 QSZK；并借助从 QIP_{ℓ-bit}[2,c,s] 到 QIP_bit 的答案压缩，得到当 2c>(1+2^{ℓ/2})s 时的另一易处理区间。改进的极化方法同时适用于 SD 与 SZK，解决了 Sahai 和 Vadhan (JACM 2003) 的公开问题。3. 量子公共硬币也使交互无用：恒定间隙的 qc-QAM[O(√log n)] 属于 BQP。

Conclusion: 该工作刻画了简洁证明者两消息量子交互证明系统的能力：QSD 刻画 1-bit 情形并使其略高于 QSZK，给出多种塌缩到 QSZK 的易处理条件，并证明量子公共硬币下此类协议对计算能力无增益（塌缩到 BQP）。

Abstract: Interactive proof systems with a laconic prover, studied by Goldreich, Vadhan, and Wigderson (CC, 2002), capture problems verifiable with logarithmic prover communication in the classical setting. For two-message quantum analogs, even a single-bit prover response contains quantum statistical zero-knowledge ($\sf QSZK$), introduced by Watrous (FOCS 2002). However, restricting the verifier's question to classical public coins collapses the corresponding class to $\sf BQP$, as shown by Beigi, Shor, and Watrous (ToC, 2011).
  We further study two-message quantum interactive proof systems with a laconic prover. To this end, we introduce the class ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$, where $\ell$ is the length of the prover's response, and establish:
  1. A natural complete characterization of ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ by Multi-State Distinguishability. In particular, Quantum State Distinguishability (QSD) is ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$-complete. Since QSD is $\sf QSZK$-hard, our result places ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$, for $\ell\geq 2$, in a landscape "just above" $\sf QSZK$.
  2. Easy regimes for ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ collapsing to $\sf QSZK$. We prove that QSD$[a,b]$ (and thus ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}[a,b]$) is in $\sf QSZK$ when $a(n)-b(n)\geq 1/O(\log n)$, and combine this with an answer compression from ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}[2,c,s]$ to ${\sf QIP}_{\rm bit}$ to obtain another easy regime when $2c>(1+2^{\ell/2})s$. Remarkably, our improved polarization applies to SD and $\sf SZK$, resolving an open problem in Sahai and Vadhan (JACM, 2003).
  3. Quantum public coins also make the interaction useless: ${\sf qc}\text{-}{\sf QAM}[O(\sqrt{\log{n}})]$ with constant gap is in $\sf BQP$, where ${\sf qc}\text{-}{\sf QAM}[\ell]$ is a subclass of ${\sf QIP}_{\ell\text{-}{\rm bit}}(2)$ in which the verifier's question is exactly halves of EPR pairs.

</details>


### [105] [Quantum double lock-in detection via sequential orthogonal quantum mixing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39498)
*Min Zhuang,Sijie Chen,Jiahao Huang,Chaohong Lee*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅用单次量子干涉仪实现量子双锁定检测的通用协议，基于顺序正交周期多脉冲序列，可同时提取振荡信号的振幅、频率和初相位，并在GHZ态输入下逼近海森堡极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度测量振荡信号是基础科学与实用技术中的重要问题。传统量子双锁定检测需两次独立的量子干涉测量，每次都要进行态制备与读出，而实际实验中态制备时间不可忽略甚至长于传感时间，导致实验资源浪费。因此，如何在仅用单次量子干涉仪的条件下仍能提取信号完整信息（振幅、频率、初相位）成为挑战。

Method: 提出一种通用协议：在顺序正交周期多脉冲序列作用下，仅通过单次量子干涉仪实现量子双锁定检测。特别地，采用Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger（GHZ）态作为输入态，并在干涉过程中施加两种基于相互作用的操作。

Result: 对频率、振幅和初相位的测量精度均可逼近海森堡极限。

Conclusion: 该研究为用单次量子干涉仪测量振荡信号开辟了新途径，并为实现交变信号的海森堡极限检测提供了可行方法。

Abstract: High-precision measurement of oscillating signal is a ubiquitous issue in fundamental science and a critical task in practical technologies. In quantum metrology, quantum lock-in detection provide an efficient method for measuring such signal. In general, when the initial phase of the oscillating signal is unknown,quantum double lock-in detection can effectively extract complete information about the signal's amplitude, frequency, and initial phase. Conventional quantum double lock-in detection requires two individual quantum interferometry, each of which must undergo state preparation and readout. However, the time for state preparation and readout need not be negligible in practical experiments. In particular, the time for state preparation is longer than the time for sensing. To save experimental resources, it is challenging to achieve quantum double lock-in detection just via a single quantum interferometry while still extracting complete information about the signal's amplitude, frequency, and initial phase. Here, we present a general protocol for achieving a quantum double lock-in detection just via a single quantum interferometry under a sequential orthogonal periodic multipulse sequences. In particular, if the input state is a Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger state and two interaction-based operations are applied during interferometry, the measurement precisions for frequency, amplitude, and initial phase can both approach the Heisenberg limit. Our study paves a new way for measuring oscillating signals with a single quantum interferometry, and provides a feasible method for achieving Heisenberg-limited detection of alternating signals.

</details>


### [106] [Layer codes as quantum memories: syndrome extraction, thresholds and idle robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39522)
*Zhu Sun,Zhenyu Cai*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文为层码（layer codes）设计了一种无解码器的综合征提取调度器，生成并评估了18个层码在电路级噪声下作为主动存储器的性能，发现其容错阈值达5.06(7)×10⁻³，空闲鲁棒性优于同距离旋转表面码，但代价是更多操作与约五倍的物理量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层码此前的研究仅描述码本身的性质（三维局部CSS码，校验权重至多6），而非测量它的电路。作为主动存储器在电路级噪声下的表现未知，且耦合不同码片的结稳定子（junction stabilizers）使其无法直接套用表面码码片的CNOT调度方案，因此需要专门构建综合征提取电路并评估其容错性能。

Method: 提出一种无解码器的调度器：先约束钩子传播（hook propagation），再压缩电路深度。对四种输入码共生成18个层码的电路，与同距离的表面码在相同模拟与解码流程下对比；并对构造所需的跨平面（beyond-2D）CNOT门成本进行建模分析。

Result: 在[[4,2,2]]输入码上，凡能进行电路距离证明之处，电路均未因钩子错误损失距离；相较通用CSS码调度器，逻辑错误率更低，且每轮CNOT深度约为其一半。主要族（基于[[4,2,2]]输入码）达到电路级阈值5.06(7)×10⁻³。空闲鲁棒性方面，同距离下层码可容忍的综合征提取轮次间隔比旋转表面码长1.7–3.0倍。代价是单位时间内操作数多1.7–5.0倍、每逻辑量子比特所需物理量子比特约多五倍。跨平面CNOT的速度比其保真度更为关键。

Conclusion: 层码作为电路级噪声下的主动存储器具有可行性与竞争力：具备更优的空闲鲁棒性和可观的阈值，代价是更高的操作密度与量子比特开销。跨平面CNOT门的速率是可扩展性中最关键的设计瓶颈，而非其保真度。

Abstract: Layer codes are three-dimensional local CSS codes of check weight at most six, obtained by quasi-concatenating a CSS input code with unrotated surface code patches. What is established about them describes the code, not the circuit that measures it. In this work, we evaluate them as active memories under circuit-level noise. Doing so first requires a syndrome extraction circuit, and the junction stabilizers that couple the patches rule out borrowing a surface code patch's CNOT schedule. We present a decoder-free scheduler that constrains hook propagation first and compresses depth afterwards, generate circuits for eighteen layer codes across four input codes, and compare them with surface codes of the same distance, simulated and decoded in the same way. The circuits lose no distance to hook errors on [[4,2,2]]-input codes wherever a circuit-distance proof is affordable, and reach a lower logical error rate than those of a general-purpose scheduler for CSS codes at roughly half its CNOT depth per round. Our primary family built from a [[4,2,2]] input code reaches a circuit-level threshold of $5.06(7)\times 10^{-3}$. The layer code outperforms the surface code in terms of idle robustness: at the same distance it tolerates an interval between syndrome extraction rounds 1.7-3.0 times longer than the rotated surface code. This comes at the cost of 1.7-5.0 times more operations per unit time and approximately five times more physical qubits, both per logical qubit. Finally, we model the cost of the beyond-2D cross-plane CNOTs that the construction demands, and find that their speed matters far more than their fidelity.

</details>


### [107] [Optimal local recovery cannot alter critical orthogonality exponents in quantum spin environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39528)
*Zhixuan Zhao,Jun Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明正基态的局域振幅比界与有限区域擦除下保真度的逆向比较，并以临界Ising链的精确格点计算给出振幅指数1/8及其最优控制不变性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限区域擦除（erasure）下量子基态信息的可恢复性，明确振幅比、保真度与局域压缩的定量界限，并检验更强投影态推断是否成立。

Method: 证明局域振幅比界与保真度逆向比较；对连通临界Ising链做精确格点计算；推导有限Cauchy乘积及其均匀条带渐近；给出双自旋优化与干涉测量协议；建立有限中介（finite-mediator）局域压缩与解码定理；给出基态制备的Ramsey界及反例，并进行独立的全哈密顿量计算。

Result: 临界Ising链振幅指数为1/8，且在任意固定自旋集上的最优控制下保持不变，在o(log N)自旋上于对数指数层面亦保持；得到有限Cauchy乘积与均匀条带渐近；给出精确双自旋优化与测量恢复的干涉协议；有限中介定理实现局域压缩并保留环境其余部分精确。

Conclusion: 擦除后基态振幅与保真度具有明确且受控的局域结构，1/8指数在最优控制下稳健；更强投影态推断不成立，但可通过有限中介压缩与解码协议量化恢复。

Abstract: We prove a local amplitude-ratio bound for positive ground states and an explicit reverse comparison for fidelity under finite-region erasure. An exact lattice calculation for a connected critical Ising chain gives amplitude exponent $1/8$, preserved by arbitrary optimal control on any fixed spin set and, at the level of logarithmic exponents, on $o(\log N)$ spins. We derive the finite Cauchy product and its uniform strip asymptotics, and document exact two-spin optimization and an interferometric protocol for measuring the recovery. A separate finite-mediator theorem quantifies local compression and a decoder while retaining the rest of the environment exactly. We also give a ground-preparation Ramsey bound, counterexamples to stronger projected-state inferences, and independent full-Hamiltonian calculations.

</details>


### [108] [Boron vacancies in bulk h-BN created by high-energy He+ irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39535)
*P. G. Vilyuzhanina,S. V. Bolshedvorskii,R. Isayev,A. S. Doroshkevich,A. A. Tatarinova,V. V. Soshenko,I. S. Cojocaru,A. M. Kozodaev,S. M. Drofa,A. Chernyavskiy,N. I. Salangin,A. N. Smolyaninov,A. Y. Kuntsevich,A. V. Akimov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过约1 MeV氦离子注入块体h-BN，成功制备了带负电的硼空位色心，并用光致发光和光探测磁共振予以确认。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石等三维晶体中的色心已成为多种传感器的关键元件，二维材料中的色心也在快速发展并有望用于高技术应用。h-BN中带负电的硼空位在磁测量方面已显现潜力，但可靠制备硼空位仍具挑战，本文旨在解决这一制备难题。

Method: 采用约1 MeV能量的氦离子注入块体h-BN，制备带负电的硼空位色心；通过特征光致发光谱和光探测磁共振进行确认；测量色心沿h-BN晶体c轴的深度，并与SRIM（Stopping and Range of Ions in Matter）模拟预测结果进行对比。

Result: 光探测磁共振谱线宽度为180 MHz，并呈现出与预期一致的塞曼频移。色心沿c轴的实测深度与SRIM模拟预测在报道的不确定度范围内一致（1σ区间存在重叠），但实验测得的深度更大、分布更宽。

Conclusion: 氦离子注入是制备块体h-BN中带负电硼空位色心的可行方法，所制备色心具备良好的磁共振特性，其深度行为在误差范围内与SRIM模型相符。

Abstract: While color centers in diamond and other three-dimensional crystals are nowadays key elements of several types of sensors, color centers in two-dimensional materials are rapidly developing and promise high-tech applications. One of the interesting centers in 2D materials is the negatively charged boron vacancy in hexagonal boron nitride (h-BN), which has already shown some potential for magnetometry, but the reliable creation of boron vacancies remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate the fabrication of negatively charged boron vacancy color centers in bulk h-BN via implantation of helium ions at ~1 MeV, as confirmed by characteristic photoluminescence and optically detected magnetic resonance. The resonance has a width of 180 MHz and exhibits the expected Zeeman shift of the resonance lines. The depth of the color center along the c-axis of the h-BN crystal was measured and compared with predictions from Stopping and Range of Ions in Matter modeling. The experimental value and the modeled prediction are consistent within the reported uncertainties, as their 1σ intervals overlap, although the experiment shows a greater depth and a wider distribution.

</details>


### [109] [Exact Maximum Likelihood Decoding beyond Treewidth via Rank-Decomposition Dynamic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39556)
*Bin Cheng,Feng Pan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于秩分解动态规划（Rank DP）的量子纠错精确最大似然解码算法，将解码配分函数表示为局部故障因子的二次幂和，复杂度对输入规模多项式、对Tanner图秩宽指数，为穿刺量子Reed-Muller码和Steane级联码族给出行之有效的多项式时间解码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错的最优解码是最大似然解码，但计算逻辑类概率困难，主流的精确张量网络收缩方法所需时间对树宽呈指数增长，亟需新的高效精确解码途径。

Method: 将解码配分函数表达为独立局部故障因子的幂的二次和，应用秩分解动态规划；考虑分解构建后，精确求值器的算术复杂度对输入规模多项式、对故障划分对应的Tanner图秩宽指数。经高斯消元后，在独立单量子比特Pauli噪声下对穿刺量子Reed-Muller码及距离增长的Steane级联码族实现多项式时间解码。此外给出非负实现下的相对浮点误差界。

Result: 在选定的码容量与电路级实例上，运行时优于所测试的张量网络实现，可对最多1023量子比特的Reed-Muller码完成完整似然评估；所对应的张量网络标准收缩需要超多项式时间。

Conclusion: 该工作进一步利用这些似然从校正子学习电路噪声参数、评估稀有后选择概率并量化解码器最优性差距，为在量子解码与噪声表征中利用代数结构开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Maximum-likelihood decoding provides an optimal decoding strategy for quantum error correction under stochastic Pauli noise. However, computing logical-class probabilities is challenging, and the leading exact tensor-network contraction requires time exponential in treewidth. In this work, we introduce a new decoding algorithm based on rank-decomposition dynamic programming (Rank DP). We express decoding partition functions with independent local fault factors as quadratic sums of powers and apply Rank DP. The resulting exact evaluator has arithmetic complexity polynomial in the input size and exponential in the Tanner graph's rank-width with respect to the fault partition, including decomposition construction. After Gaussian elimination, it gives polynomial-time decoding for punctured quantum Reed-Muller codes and a Steane-concatenated family with growing distance under independent single-qubit Pauli noise. Standard contraction of the corresponding tensor networks requires superpolynomial time. A nonnegative realization gives relative floating-point error bounds under explicit arithmetic assumptions. Numerical experiments demonstrate runtime advantages over the tested tensor-network implementations on selected code-capacity and circuit-level instances, with full likelihood evaluation for Reed-Muller codes up to $1{,}023$ qubits. We further use these likelihoods to learn circuit noise parameters from syndromes, evaluate rare postselection probabilities, and quantify decoder optimality gaps. Our work opens new avenues for exploiting algebraic structure in quantum decoding and noise characterization.

</details>


### [110] [Rate 1/5 Non-Malleable Codes against Entangled Split-State Tampering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39589)
*Naresh Goud Boddu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文构造了针对两个不通信的局域量子篡改操作（允许任意预共享纠缠）的高效信息论不可延展码，首次在最坏情况经典消息上实现恒定码率，达到 1/5-ξ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前基于置换的两分裂构造（Batra, Boddu, Jain）仅在均匀随机消息下使码率趋近 1/5，无法保证最坏情况经典消息的安全性；本文旨在解决纠缠两分裂状态模型下最坏情况经典消息的恒定码率问题。

Method: 保留原置换式两分裂架构，将其中的均匀消息输入替换为"指定消息 + 新鲜均匀填充"的拼接，并给出针对这一修改的最坏情况安全性归约。

Result: 对任意足够小的固定 ξ>0 和足够大的首份额长度 n，码率达到至少 1/5-ξ，具有完美正确性和 2^{-n^{Ω(1)}} 的误差；安全性对每条消息成立，且对每种攻击存在单一的消息无关模拟器。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了纠缠两分裂状态模型中、最坏情况经典消息的恒定码率问题，将原有均匀消息结果推广至最坏情况安全性。

Abstract: We construct efficient information-theoretic non-malleable codes for classical messages that are secure against two noncommunicating local quantum tampering operations with arbitrary pre-shared entanglement. For every sufficiently small fixed $ξ>0$ and all sufficiently large first-share lengths $n$, the codes have rate at least $1/5-ξ$, perfect correctness, and error $2^{-n^{Ω(1)}}$. Security holds for every message, with a single message-independent simulator for each attack. This resolves the constant-rate question for worst-case classical messages in the entangled two-split-state model.
  Our construction builds on the permutation-based two-split construction of Batra, Boddu, and Jain, which achieves rate approaching $1/5$ for uniformly random messages. We retain their architecture but replace the uniform message input to the permutation with a prescribed message concatenated with fresh uniform padding. Our main contribution is a worst-case security reduction for this modification.

</details>


### [111] [The Complexity of Single-Interaction Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39592)
*Elad Hecker*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究单一相互作用哈密顿量（SIH）模型，通过精确编译器将任意有限局部相互作用字母表归约为单一固定相互作用，证明SIH_3的QMA完全性及几何局部SIH_8的QMA完全性，并将多种哈密顿量复杂度现象统一到单一相互作用框架下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Local Hamiltonian问题的复杂度通常允许每个局部项为任意矩阵，且耦合强度可独立调节。作者希望探究：若所有局部项必须是同一固定相互作用的无权出现，是否仍能保持广泛的哈密顿量复杂度现象（如QMA完全性、量子PCP猜想等）。

Method: 核心工具是一个精确编译器，将任意固定有限局部相互作用字母表转换为单一相互作用：在指定辅助子空间上精确复现源哈密顿量，并保持低于分离尺度的完整谱及重数。结合有限字母表归一化，得到从k-Local Hamiltonian到SIH_{3k+3}的多项式时间归约（至已知全局能量尺度与逆多项式近似）。进一步构造更紧的归约，分别处理几何局部、stoquastic及钉扎情形。

Result: 证明SIH_3的QMA完全性；几何局部SIH_8（有界度与相互作用范围）的QMA完全性；stoquastic情形下Stoq-SIH_3的StoqMA完全性与1-Pinned Stoq-SIH_4的QMA完全性；对均匀系统尺寸依赖相互作用给出硬度结果；并导出哈密顿量量子PCP猜想、无挫折、指数精确及引导Local Hamiltonian问题的单一相互作用形式。

Conclusion: 独立选择的局部矩阵与独立可调耦合强度并非广泛哈密顿量复杂度现象的必要条件，单一固定相互作用即可承载QMA/StoqMA完全性、量子PCP猜想等核心结果。

Abstract: We study the complexity of Local Hamiltonian problems under the restriction that every local term is an unweighted occurrence of the same fixed interaction. In the resulting Single-Interaction Hamiltonian (SIH) model, once the interaction is fixed, the Hamiltonian is specified entirely by its ordered interaction hypergraph.
  Our main technical tool is an exact compiler that converts any fixed finite alphabet of local interactions into a single interaction. On a designated auxiliary subspace, the compiled Hamiltonian reproduces the source Hamiltonian exactly, while the complete spectrum below a chosen separation scale is preserved with multiplicity. Combined with a finite-alphabet normalization, this gives a universal polynomial-time reduction from $k$-Local Hamiltonian to SIH$_{3k+3}$ up to a known global energy scale and inverse-polynomial approximation.
  Sharper constructions give QMA-completeness of SIH$_3$ and of geometrically local SIH$_8$ with bounded degree and interaction range. In the stoquastic setting, we obtain StoqMA-completeness of Stoq-SIH$_3$ and QMA-completeness of 1-Pinned Stoq-SIH$_4$. We also establish hardness results for uniformly system-size-dependent interactions and derive single-interaction formulations of the Hamiltonian quantum PCP conjecture, frustration-free, exponentially precise, and guided Local Hamiltonian problems. These results show that independently chosen local matrices and independently tunable coupling strengths are not necessary for a broad range of Hamiltonian-complexity phenomena.

</details>


### [112] [Thermodynamics of Ahn--Doherty--Landahl Continuous Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39597)
*Juan Garcia-Nila,Pedro B. Melo,Lucas Johns*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将基于测量的连续量子纠错（CQEC）建模为信息引擎，区分系统侧与控制器侧功率，并以单量子比特ADL协议和三量子比特重复码为例，揭示了逻辑稳定与能量消耗之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续量子纠错以连续综合征提取和实时哈密顿反馈替代离散测量与恢复操作，但其运行所需的热力学资源尚不明确，需要从信息引擎角度量化能耗与稳定性的关系。

Method: 将基于测量的连续量子纠错表述为信息引擎，区分反馈场与被保护系统之间能量转移对应的系统侧功率，以及反馈哈密顿量对应的控制器侧功率；先在单量子比特ADL协议上建立框架，再推广至连续稳定子监测下的三量子比特重复码，并进行数值模拟。

Result: 数值模拟表明，增大反馈强度可提升稳态保真度并降低条件态熵，但在保真度开始饱和之后，两类能量贡献的幅度仍持续增长。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了逻辑稳定与连续反馈所需能量资源之间的直接权衡，与量子测量和量子Zeno稳定中已确立的能量—精度权衡密切相关。

Abstract: Continuous quantum error correction (CQEC) replaces discrete syndrome measurements and recovery operations with continuous syndrome extraction and real-time Hamiltonian feedback. Here we investigate the thermodynamic resources required by this process by formulating measurement-based continuous quantum error correction as an information engine. We distinguish the system-side power associated with energy transfer between the feedback field and the protected system from the controller-side power associated with the feedback Hamiltonian. The framework is first developed for the one-qubit Ahn-Doherty-Landahl (ADL) protocol and subsequently extended to the three-qubit repetition code under continuous stabilizer monitoring. Numerical simulations show that increasing the feedback strength improves the steady-state fidelity and reduces the conditional-state entropy, while both energetic contributions continue to increase in magnitude after the fidelity begins to saturate. These results expose a direct tradeoff between logical stabilization and the energetic resources required for continuous feedback, closely related to previously established energy--precision tradeoffs in quantum measurement and quantum-Zeno stabilization.

</details>


### [113] [Warm-Start Iterative QITE for Distribution Network Reconfiguration via Branch-Exchange Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39602)
*Noah Crum,Pete Pritchard,Tanner Rase,Phillip C. Lotshaw,Shaked Regev,Claudio Girotto,Ananth Kaushik,Paul Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种结合径向保持支路交换编码与迭代热启动量子虚时演化的混合量子-经典算法，用于配电网重构以最小化有功线损，在十个测试案例中六个达到最小损耗配置，其余四个损耗高出参考最小值0.2%~2.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配电网重构是电力配电网上的组合优化问题，目标是降低有功线损；传统方法在大规模网络上计算成本高，而量子算法在此类问题上具有潜在优势，故需设计兼顾可行性与求解质量的混合量子-经典方案。

Method: 采用径向保持的支路交换编码，以固定宽度二进制表示顺序支路交换动作，保证每个寄存器输出均解码为径向配置；量子子程序由基于经典求解的交流潮流（ACPF）标签拟合的代理模型提供信息，并使用迭代热启动量子虚时演化；在给定ACPF求解次数与电路采样预算耗尽后，返回所见过的最低损耗配置；另引入两种缩减问题规模的方法以支持更大网络。

Result: 在十个测试案例（含七个常用基准案例及三个由这些及类似标准系统修改得到的案例）上进行了验证；六个案例成功达到最小损耗配置，其余四个案例的配置损耗比已报道的参考最小损耗高出0.2%至2.18%。

Conclusion: 该混合量子-经典算法结合径向保持编码与迭代热启动量子虚时演化，能够有效求解配电网重构问题，并通过问题规模缩减方法具备向更大网络扩展的潜力。

Abstract: We present a hybrid quantum-classical algorithm for distribution network reconfiguration, a combinatorial optimization problem on power distribution networks, that combines a radiality preserving branch-exchange encoding with iterative warm-start quantum imaginary-time evolution to minimize active line losses. The encoding uses a fixed-width binary representation of sequential branch-exchange actions, ensuring that every register outcome decodes to a radial configuration. The quantum subroutine is informed by a surrogate model fit to classically-solved alternating current power flow (ACPF) labels. After a set budget of ACPF solves and circuit samples is exhausted, the algorithm returns the lowest-loss configuration seen. We employ our algorithm on ten test cases, including seven commonly-used benchmark cases and three cases we modified from these and similar standard systems. We include two methods to reduce problem size, which enable extensions of the algorithm to larger networks. We successfully reach minimal-loss configurations for six cases and find configurations with losses from 0.2 percent to 2.18 percent above the reported reference minimal loss for the other four.

</details>


### [114] [Weakly Measured Loops for Quantum Amplitude Amplification: Oracle Savings and Adaptive Search with Unknown Target Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39621)
*Wang Fang,Chris Heunen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一类将Grover旋转与可调弱测量交替循环的量子振幅放大算法：测量成功即停止，失败则从测量后状态继续。通过对期望Grover迭代次数的主阶分析，给出了已知、仅知下界和完全未知目标概率三种情形下的最优弱测量强度与常数级加速结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Grover算法与优化重启策略的期望迭代常数存在改进空间，作者希望借助弱测量引入可控的定点机制，在保持Θ(1/√p)搜索标度的同时降低oracle代价。

Method: 算法将普通Grover旋转与可变弱测量交替嵌入循环，失败后不重启而从后测量态继续。分析手段包括连续角度分析、离散Lyapunov方程求解固定测量强度下的精确期望oracle代价，以及对κ(t)=Θ(1/t)临界标度下κ_b(t)=min{1/2,b/t}的闭式Gamma函数推导与数值最小化。

Result: 已知p且角度接近π/4时，精确弱测量条件循环使用(π/8+1/4+o(1))/√p次迭代，优于标准Grover常数π/4及优化重启Grover，并达到连续角度分析下确界；仅知下界p0时，期望迭代次数至多为(1/2√2)(1/√p+1/√p0)，在承诺边界p=p0给出主阶常数1/√2；完全未知p时，每个固定b>2的闭式C(b)给出(C(b)/2+o(1))/√p次迭代，数值最小化得b≈5.2、C(b)/2≈1.01。

Conclusion: 结果表明弱测量在维持Θ(1/√p)搜索标度的同时，提供了显式的定点控制机制并带来可证明的oracle节省。

Abstract: We present a family of quantum amplitude amplification algorithms that interleave ordinary Grover rotations with tunable weak measurements in loops. A successful measurement yields the target state and stops the algorithm, while after a failure, the loop resumes from the post-measurement state without restarting. We express the leading costs in expected Grover iterations as $p \to 0$. For a known target probability $p$, an exact weak measurement-conditioned loop for states near the angle $π/4$ uses $(π/8+1/4+o(1))/\sqrt{p}$ iterations, improving on the standard Grover constant $π/4$ and on optimised restart Grover, and matching the corresponding infimum in our continuous-angle analysis. When only a lower bound $0 < p_0 \leq p < 1/2$ is available, a discrete Lyapunov equation gives the exact expected oracle cost for every fixed measurement strength and a closed-form optimal strength. Using the strength selected from $p_0$, the expected number of Grover iterations is at most $\frac{1}{2\sqrt{2}}(\frac{1}{\sqrt{p}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{p_0}})$, where the expected cost decreases as the actual $p$ increases, and gives the leading constant $1/\sqrt{2}$ at the promise boundary $p = p_0$. For completely unknown $p$, we identify measurement strength $κ(t)=Θ(1/t)$ as the critical scale within the regular schedules considered here and analyse $κ_b(t)=\min\{1/2,b/t\}$. For each fixed $b > 2$, we rigorously derive a closed-form Gamma-function expression $C(b)$ giving $(C(b)/2+o(1))/\sqrt{p}$ expected iterations. Numerical minimisation of this explicit expression gives $b \approx 5.2$ and $C(b)/2 \approx 1.01$. The results show that weak measurements preserve the $Θ(1/\sqrt{p})$ search scale while adding an explicit fixed-point control mechanism and provable oracle savings.

</details>


### [115] [Phase-sensitive avalanche quantum sensing of sub-shot-noise fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39650)
*Nikolai D. Klimkin,Misha Ivanov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于原子云-光子腔耦合的雪崩式放大机制，可对低于量子散粒噪声极限的极弱光波实现非微扰探测，并保持原始相位信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统精密测量（如盖革计数器、单光子雪崩探测器）利用雪崩机制探测小扰动，但探测低于量子散粒噪声极限的弱光信号仍面临灵敏度与相位保持的挑战，现有方案难以兼顾放大与相位信息保留。

Method: 将原子云囚禁于光子腔中，把腔模调谐至宇称禁戒跃迁；用连续波激光泵浦，通过数值模拟研究原子云对腔场变化的响应及所产生的发射放大机制。

Result: 原子云产生的发射将初始低于散粒噪声的涨落放大约两个数量级；该放大对频率具有宽可调性，且不依赖原子能级结构；更重要的是放大过程保留了原始极弱光波赋予原子的相位。

Conclusion: 该发现对量子态表征与弱经典信号探测均具意义，提供了一种可调、普适且保留相位的弱光放大探测新途径。

Abstract: Avalanche-based detection of small perturbations is commonplace in precision measurement devices from Geiger counters to single-photon avalanche detectors. Here, we expand this principle to sensing of light waves below the quantum shot noise limit, demonstrating numerically how atomic clouds trapped in photonic cavities can exhibit non-perturbative sensitivity to changes in the cavity field when the cavity mode is tuned to a parity-prohibited transition. We find that, when pumped by a continuous-wave laser, the atomic cloud creates emissions which amplify the initially sub-shot-noise fluctuation by two orders of magnitude. Crucially, this amplification is broadly tunable with respect to frequency and independent of atomic energy structure. Even more strikingly, our amplification mechanism preserves the phase imparted on the atoms by the original ultra-weak light wave, marking a qualitative improvement over conventional protocols. Our finding has implications for both quantum state characterization and detection of weak classical signals.

</details>


### [116] [QMA(2) with Limited Shared Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39668)
*Alex Della Schiava,Ranitha Mataraarachchi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在 QMA(2) 协议中，最多对数级 EPR 对（$h=O(\log n)$）的共享纠缠不会增加计算能力，即 $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$；并给出 EPR 预算单调性与 LOCC 模型下的相关结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Aaronson 等人提出带共享纠缠的 QMA(2;h) 模型并留下公开问题：共享纠缠能否增强 QMA(2) 的能力。本文旨在刻画对数级 EPR 预算下纠缠对 QMA(2) 计算能力的影响，并将其推广到 LOCC 预备模型。

Method: 通过对数级 EPR 对下的模拟归约，将带纠缠协议转化为使用四个非纠缠见证的协议，并结合 Harrow–Montanaro 等式 $\mathsf{QMA}(4)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$。此外证明 EPR 预算的单调性 $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)\subseteq\mathsf{QMA}(2;H)$（保持完备性与可靠性），并结合输入填充进行推广；进一步将结论扩展至允许 LOCC 的见证预备模型。

Result: 主要结果：$h=O(\log n)$ 时 $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$。辅助结果：EPR 预算单调性成立；若对某固定 $\varepsilon>0$ 有 $\mathsf{QMA}(2;n^\varepsilon)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$，则所有多项式有界预算均相等，从而解决 Aaronson 等人的公开问题。在 LOCC 模型中，对数规模见证与逆多项式间隙下，$h=O(\log n)$ 时两模型仍等价于非纠缠对应模型；若将 LOCC 模型的等价性推广到任意超对数 EPR 预算，则蕴含 $\mathsf{NP}\subseteq\mathsf{BQP}$。

Conclusion: 对数级共享纠缠不增强 QMA(2) 的验证能力；EPR 预算单调且与多项式预算问题紧密关联；LOCC 预备模型在超对数预算下的进一步等价将带来复杂性类层面的强烈推论 $\mathsf{NP}\subseteq\mathsf{BQP}$。

Abstract: A $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ protocol involves two provers submitting unentangled witnesses to a polynomial-time quantum verifier. In The Power of Unentanglement (ToC, 2009), Aaronson et al. proposed $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)$, a variant of $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ in which the two provers may share $h$ EPR pairs. Our main result shows that the power of $\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ remains unchanged for up to logarithmically many shared EPR pairs: $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ for $h=O(\log n)$, where $n$ is the input length. The result follows from a simulation using four unentangled witnesses, combined with the Harrow-Montanaro equality $\mathsf{QMA}(4)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ (FOCS, 2010).
  We also prove monotonicity in the EPR budget: $\mathsf{QMA}(2;h)\subseteq\mathsf{QMA}(2;H)$ for $h\le H$, preserving completeness and soundness. Combined with input padding, this shows that establishing $\mathsf{QMA}(2;n^\varepsilon)=\mathsf{QMA}(2)$ for any fixed $\varepsilon>0$ would imply equality for every polynomially bounded budget, resolving the open problem raised by Aaronson et al.
  Finally, we extend these results to a variant of the model in which the provers may use local operations and classical communication (LOCC) during witness preparation. For logarithmic-size witnesses and inverse-polynomial gaps, both models remain equivalent to their unentangled counterpart when $h=O(\log n)$. We show that extending this equivalence to any superlogarithmic EPR budget in the LOCC model would imply $\mathsf{NP}\subseteq\mathsf{BQP}$.

</details>


### [117] [Separable decompositions of 2xn states with operator Schmidt rank three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39669)
*Perrine Vantalon,Nicolas Macris*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 证明算子Schmidt秩为3的qubit-n能级系统二体态可分解为rank(ρ)个纯积态混合，以及至多n+1个混合积态，且后者紧致；证明是构造性的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究二体量子态分解为积态混合所需的最少项数（即态的"长度"问题），此前结果多局限于纯积态分解，本文希望统一处理纯积态与混合积态分解，并给出紧致边界。

Method: 针对qubit耦合n能级系统且算子Schmidt秩为3的二体态，构造显式算子并进行适当幺正膨胀（unitary dilation）的特征分解，据此给出获得积态分解的构造性方法。

Result: 算子Schmidt秩为3的二体态可写成rank(ρ)个纯积态的混合，也可写成至多n+1个混合积态的混合；n+1这一上界是紧的，存在无法用更少项分解的态。该框架复现了纯积态分解长度的已知结论，并给出与已有结果不同的纯积态分解。

Conclusion: 建立了统一处理纯积态与混合积态分解的构造性框架，给出了紧致的分解项数上界，推进了二体态分解长度问题的理解。

Abstract: We prove that every bipartite state $ρ$ of a qubit coupled to an $n$-level system whose operator Schmidt rank equals three can be written as a mixture of $\text{rank}(ρ)$ pure product states, and as a mixture of at most $n+1$ mixed product states in general. The second result is tight in the sense that there exist states that cannot be decomposed with a smaller number of terms. Our proof gives constructive methods to obtain the decompositions. It involves eigendecompositions of suitable unitary dilations of an operator explicitly determined by the state. Our framework recovers previously known results for the length of decompositions into pure product states and is also able to deal with decompositions into mixed states. For pure product states, our decomposition is different from existing ones.

</details>


### [118] [Polylog-depth Quantum Thermal Simulation via Local Recovery Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39674)
*Hideaki Hakoshima,Atsushi Iwaki,Nobuyuki Yoshioka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作给出在任意固定空间维度下，以 polylog 电路深度制备非对易局域哈密顿量量子热态的充分条件，条件涉及局域性与稳定性、定量高温条件及粗粒度经典参考哈密顿量；当参考可局域生成时，经典预处理代价为 N^{1+o(1)}，制备电路由空间局域 Petz 恢复映射构造，最终以 N·polylog(N/ε) 个门和量子比特制备正则纯化态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子热态制备是量子算法与量子多体物理的核心问题，但一般非对易局域哈密顿量的吉布斯态难以高效实现。作者希望在固定空间维度下建立可判定的充分条件，把热平衡中关联的静态组织结构与可显式构造的制备电路联系起来，从而为平衡态局域性赋予构造性的计算意义。

Method: 方法结合三方面要素：(1) 对约化密度矩阵有效相互作用的局域性与稳定性界；(2) 定量高温条件；(3) 可获取的粗粒度经典参考哈密顿量。制备电路由空间局域的 Petz 恢复映射构造：利用微观哈密顿量导出的量子修正实现精确恢复，而经典参考保持粗糙。当参考可局域生成时，先进行经典预处理，再据此构造制备电路。

Result: 在满足上述充分条件时，可在任意固定空间维度以 polylog 电路深度制备非对易局域哈密顿量的量子热态。当经典参考可局域生成时，针对 N 个格点、逆多项式全局精度，经典预处理代价为 N^{1+o(1)}。预处理后，电路以 N·polylog(N/ε) 个门和量子比特制备正则纯化态，其中 ε 为制备误差。

Conclusion: 该结果将两个基本问题联系起来：热平衡中关联如何组织，以及相应态如何通过量子力学允许的操作被高效实现。通过把静态平衡结构转化为显式制备电路，为平衡态局域性提供了构造性的计算解释，并使其在量子算法设计中具有基于物理的角色。

Abstract: We establish sufficient conditions for preparing quantum thermal states of noncommuting local Hamiltonians with polylogarithmic circuit depth in arbitrary fixed spatial dimension. Our conditions combine locality and stability bounds on the effective interactions of reduced density matrices of a Gibbs state with a quantitative high-temperature condition and access to coarse classical reference Hamiltonians. When these references can be generated locally, the classical preprocessing cost is $N^{1+o(1)}$ for $N$ sites at inverse-polynomial global accuracy. We construct the preparation circuit from spatially localized Petz recovery maps. Quantum corrections derived from the microscopic Hamiltonian enable accurate recovery while the classical references remain coarse. After preprocessing, the resulting circuit prepares the canonical purification using $N\operatorname{polylog}(N/\varepsilon)$ gates and qubits, where $\varepsilon$ is the preparation error. These results link two fundamental questions: how correlations are organized in thermal equilibrium, and how efficiently the corresponding states can be realized through operations allowed by quantum mechanics. By translating static equilibrium structure into explicit preparation circuits, they give equilibrium locality a constructive computational interpretation and a physically grounded role in quantum algorithm design.

</details>


### [119] [Scaling Laws of Quantum Networks: An Entanglement Transport Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39690)
*Jaemin Kim,Petar Popovski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"纠缠传输"指标与两层框架（资源层、物理层），研究多用户量子网络中纠缠分发性能随节点数 N 与用户数 M 的标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子网络正走向多用户架构，但既有研究在目标函数、资源假设与归一化方式上各不相同，难以比较，也缺乏统一刻画纠缠分发性能随 N、M 如何标度的框架。

Method: 定义纠缠传输量：将满足固定保真度阈值的每个已交付 Bell 对按其端点距离加权，以在多人需求下计入交付距离。构建两层框架：资源层在给定聚合方式（含渐近聚合与固定窗口）下测量每个激活时隙的容量；物理层在实现约束下测量每秒服务量。并在扩展蜂窝晶格（单随机会话）与均匀链上构造纠缠渗流方案进行对比分析。

Result: 扩展蜂窝晶格上，固定窗口纠缠渗流构造在阈值下达到每个激活时隙 Θ(N^{-1}) 的纠缠传输，阈值上为 Θ(√N)。均匀链上，渐近聚合容量比固定窗口容量高 Θ(N^2/M) 倍；在空间扩展下，含噪声的物理实现达到每秒 Θ(M/N) 的服务量。

Conclusion: 纠缠传输的标度不仅取决于网络连通性，还取决于资源聚合方式与物理实现细节；资源层给出的渐近容量只是服务上界，实际可达服务需单独确定。该框架为建立量子网络标度律提供了统一基础。

Abstract: As quantum networks move toward multi-user architectures, a central question is how the performance of entanglement distribution scales with the numbers of network nodes $N$ and active users $M$, yet existing studies use different objectives, resource assumptions, and normalizations. To account for delivery distance under multi-user demand, we define entanglement transport, which weights each delivered Bell pair meeting a fixed fidelity threshold by its endpoint separation. In a two-layer framework, (i) a resource layer measures capacity per activation slot under different aggregation regimes, and (ii) a physical layer measures service per second under implementation constraints. For matched implementations with a bounded number of activation slots per second, asymptotic-aggregation capacity sets a service ceiling, whereas achievable service must be established separately. On expanding honeycomb lattices with one random session, fixed-window entanglement-percolation constructions achieve $Θ(N^{-1})$ entanglement transport per activation slot below their thresholds and $Θ(\sqrt N)$ above them. For homogeneous chains, asymptotic aggregation exceeds fixed-window capacity by a factor $Θ(N^2/M)$, and in spatial expansion a noisy physical implementation achieves $Θ(M/N)$ service per second. These examples show that entanglement-transport scaling depends not only on connectivity but also on resource aggregation and physical implementation. The framework provides a common basis on which quantum-network scaling laws can be established.

</details>


### [120] [Quantum--classical break-even in electronic dynamics of macrocyclic molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39693)
*Shu Kanno,Kenji Sugisaki,Takashi Imamichi,Toshinari Itoko,Rei Sakuma,Hajime Nakamura,Qi Gao,Naoki Yamamoto*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作实现了化学动力学中的量子-经典盈亏平衡，用IBM量子处理器完成12量子比特苯和120量子比特[60]轮烯的电子动力学模拟，QPU墙钟时间与估计的经典高性能计算方法相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子硬件能否在化学相关电子动力学问题上达到与经典计算资源可比的实用精度和时间，即实现量子-经典盈亏平衡。

Method: 构建基于张量网络电路压缩的电子动力学量子工作流，利用空间局域性将压缩推广到一维周期边界系统。仅对短时、小空间区域做经典优化，再在空间和时间上复制以构造全系统长时动力学。以环状共轭大环（12量子比特苯、120量子比特[60]轮烯）为对象，将其电子结构嵌入IBM量子处理器的量子比特环上。

Result: 对[60]轮烯的洞掺杂非平衡动力学，QPU墙钟时间与两种经典方法（Majorana传播、基于含时变分原理的矩阵乘积态时间演化）的估计墙钟时间相当，对应数十至数百个计算节点（每节点36 CPU核，假设近理想并行扩展），使量子计算进入可与常规并行高性能计算资源相比的墙钟时间区间。并展示了混合量子-经典计算在精度和计算时间上均为合理选择的场景。

Conclusion: 量子硬件可以成为化学相关电子动力学的一个现实计算选项。

Abstract: Here we demonstrate quantum--classical break-even for chemical dynamics applications, reaching useful accuracy with a quantum-hardware wall-clock time comparable to that estimated for practical classical computing resources. Specifically, we construct a quantum workflow for electronic dynamics based on tensor-network circuit compression leveraging spatial locality, extending circuit compression beyond one-dimensional open-boundary systems. This enables application of the workflow to problems that are hardware-native but classically hard. The workflow targets one-dimensional periodic Hamiltonians: circuits classically optimized only for short-time evolution within a small spatial region can be replicated in both space and time to construct long-time dynamics of the full system without classically simulating the corresponding large-scale evolution. We apply the workflow to cyclic conjugated macrocycles with rotational periodicity, 12-qubit benzene and 120-qubit [60]annulene, by embedding their electronic structure onto loops of IBM quantum processors. For the annulene, the quantum-processing-unit (QPU) wall-clock times for hole-doped nonequilibrium dynamics became comparable to the estimated wall-clock time of two classical methods, Majorana propagation and matrix-product-state time evolution based on the time-dependent variational principle; this corresponds to tens to hundreds of compute nodes depending on the task (36 CPU cores per node), assuming near-ideal parallel scaling, bringing the quantum calculation into a wall-clock-time regime comparable to conventional parallel high-performance-computing resources. We also show a situation in which a hybrid quantum--classical calculation is a reasonable choice in terms of both accuracy and computational time. These results show that quantum hardware can become a realistic computational option for chemically relevant electronic dynamics.

</details>


### [121] [The Intrinsic Cost of Quantum Syndrome Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39705)
*Chia-Tung Chu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出从稳定子群本身导出的一种内在度量，刻画综合征提取所需的相互作用，并给出与硬件连接图相关的精确综合征提取深度下界；据此构造环形表面码反例，并在量子Tanner族上得到渐近紧的空间-深度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作（DBT21、BFS23）建立了综合征提取与量子硬件局域性约束之间的界限，但这些界限依赖于描述码所用的生成元集合，且部分结论（如DBT21推论1）存在局限。作者希望得到一个不依赖生成元选取的内在度量，从而给出更本质、与硬件相关的结果。

Method: 首先直接从稳定子群（而非特定生成元集合）出发，定义一种刻画综合征提取所需相互作用的内在度量；然后证明该度量给出任意稳定子码在给定硬件连接图下精确综合征提取深度的下界；并据此指导构造常深度局域综合征提取的环形表面码示例；最后将该度量应用于固定的量子Tanner族，导出更强的深度界并给出匹配构造。

Result: 该内在度量给出任意稳定子码的硬件相关精确综合征提取深度下界；构造出具有常深度局域综合征提取的环形表面码，构成DBT21推论1的反例；在固定量子Tanner族上加强了BFS23的显式深度界，并结合匹配构造得到渐近紧的空间-深度权衡。

Conclusion: 理论上，结果将综合征提取所需的信息量与设备的通信容量联系起来；实践上，提供了一种在确定硬件布局之前评估不可避免电路开销的方法。

Abstract: How much time is fundamentally required to extract the syndrome of a stabilizer code on hardware with a given connectivity graph? Previous work by Delfosse--Beverland--Tremblay (2021) and Baspin--Fawzi--Shayeghi (2023) established bounds connecting syndrome extraction to locality constraints on quantum hardware. Here, we address this question by first formulating an intrinsic measure of the interaction required for syndrome extraction directly from the stabilizer group, independent of the generating set used to describe the code. We then show that this measure yields hardware-dependent lower bounds on exact syndrome-extraction depth for arbitrary stabilizer codes. Guided by this measure, we also construct annular surface-code examples with constant-depth local syndrome extraction that provide a counterexample to Corollary 1 of DBT21. Furthermore, for a fixed quantum Tanner family, this measure strengthens the depth bound explicitly stated in BFS23 and, together with our matching construction, establishes an asymptotically tight space--depth tradeoff. In theory, our results connect the information required by syndrome extraction to the communication capacity of a device; while in practice, they provide a way to assess unavoidable circuit costs before committing to a hardware layout.

</details>


### [122] [Complex Quantum Dynamics Versus Classical Simulability of Noisy Random Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39734)
*Anjali Waghmare,Sergii Strelchuk,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在有噪声的Clifford+T及近邻matchgate+SWAP电路中，动力学诊断量（magic、operator scrambling、反集中、非高斯性）与经典可模拟性会双向分离：诊断量可能在经典模拟已高效后仍非平凡，或在其变平凡前已知高效算法已适用。根源在于诊断量依赖Pauli谱的四阶统计量，而模拟算法主要依赖二阶矩，局域噪声以不同速率压制两者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势的主张依赖量子线路经典模拟的困难性。magic、算符scrambling、反集中、费米子非高斯性等被当作复杂量子动力学的标准诊断量，对纯态已有严格结论联系经典可模拟性。但在噪声下这些诊断量是否能可靠标示高效经典模拟的边界尚不清楚。

Method: 在含噪声的Clifford+T线路与近邻matchgate+SWAP线路中，对比动力学诊断量的行为与经典模拟算法（基于二阶矩）的适用条件，分析两者所探测的统计性质差异。

Result: 诊断量与经典可模拟性出现双向分离：诊断量在经典模拟已变得高效之后仍可保持非平凡，或在已知高效算法适用之前就变得平凡。这一错配源于诊断量（magic、scrambling）依赖Pauli谱的四阶统计量，而模拟算法主要依赖二阶矩，局域噪声对二者的压制速率不同。

Conclusion: 在噪声量子设备上测得的动力学诊断量本身不构成经典困难性的证据。

Abstract: Claims of quantum advantage rest on the classical hardness of simulating quantum circuits. Magic, operator scrambling, anticoncentration, and non-Gaussianity for fermionic circuits are standard diagnostics of complex quantum dynamics. For pure states, some of these have been rigorously connected to classical simulability. Whether these diagnostics reliably track the limits of efficient classical simulation under noise remains unclear. Here, we show that in noisy Clifford+$T$ and nearest-neighbour matchgate+SWAP circuits, dynamical diagnostics and classical simulability can separate in both directions. In particular, the diagnostics can remain nontrivial after classical simulation becomes efficient, or become trivial before known efficient classical algorithms apply. We trace this mismatch to the different statistical properties they probe: magic and scrambling depend on fourth-order statistics of the Pauli spectrum, whereas the simulation algorithms depend primarily on second-order moments, which local noise suppresses at different rates. Thus, dynamical diagnostics measured on a noisy quantum device do not by themselves constitute evidence for classical hardness.

</details>


### [123] [What makes a causal loop consistent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39735)
*Hippolyte Dourdent,Andreas Leitherer,Emanuel-Cristian Boghiu,Kyrylo Simonov,Ravi Kunjwal,Antonio Acín*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文给出了确定性经典因果环逻辑一致性的充要条件：当且仅当过程不含信号回路（允许不定因果序），且事件列表两两互斥且完备；由此修复了既有无干预判据漏判全局环的问题，并揭示其背后是多重Specker原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果环要在任意独立局部干预下产生合法概率才不引发悖论，但已有的"无干预"一致性判据存在缺陷，会漏掉全局环，因此需要更严格、基于事件且非递归的一致性刻画。

Method: 以确定性经典过程为对象，在任意独立局部干预下定义逻辑一致性，分析信号回路与因果回路的区别；用事件列表的两两互斥性与完备性给出等价刻画，并与Specker原理的多体形式建立联系。

Result: 证明确定性经典过程逻辑一致当且仅当不含信号回路——循环信号被禁止而循环因果不被禁止，从而允许不定因果序；一致性恰好等价于过程事件列表两两互斥且完备，这是首个基于事件、无干预、非递归的一致因果环刻画，并修复了此前遗漏全局环的判据。

Conclusion: 无因果序的确定性经典通信之所以能保持一致，本质上源于一种多体形式的Specker原理：若若干问题中任意两个可联合回答，则全部可联合回答。该结果澄清了信号回路与因果回路的边界，为不定因果序下的一致性提供了事件层面的基础判据。

Abstract: Causal loops escape paradoxes if they generate valid probabilities under arbitrary independent local interventions. We show that a deterministic classical process is logically consistent if and only if it contains no signaling loops: cyclic signaling is forbidden but cyclic causation is not, allowing indefinite causal order. Logical consistency is then found to hold exactly when the list of events of a process is pairwise exclusive and complete, providing the first event-based, intervention-free, non-recursive characterization of consistent causal loops. Our results repair a previously introduced intervention-free criterion that misses global loops and reveal that what makes deterministic classical communications without causal order consistent is a multipartite form of Specker's principle: if any two of several questions can be answered jointly, so can all of them.

</details>


### [124] [A depolarizing choir sings in Gaussian harmony](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39747)
*Rabsan Galib Ahmed,Sujeet Bhalerao,Sungjai Lee,Felix Leditzky,Debbie Leung,Luke Schaeffer,Graeme Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子比特退极化信道的正量子容量噪声阈值，揭示其与玻色高斯信道的联系并改进下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在确定量子比特退极化信道维持正量子容量的噪声阈值，深化对对称码的理解。

Method: 解析研究退极化信道在输入量子比特对称子空间上的作用，在渐近多次使用极限下，发现涌现出玻色高斯信道；将退极化信道已有的码映射到涌现高斯信道并优化，再转换回退极化信道。

Result: 获得退极化信道相干信息的极佳输入态，得到正容量噪声阈值的改进下界。

Conclusion: 退极化噪声与高斯信道的新联系不仅改进了阈值下界，也为理解对称码提供了新视角。

Abstract: We study the noise threshold for positive quantum capacity for the qubit depolarizing channel. We explore analytically the action of the qubit depolarizing channel on the symmetric subspaces of the input qubits, in the limit of asymptotically many uses of the channel. We observe the emergence of a bosonic Gaussian channel. Furthermore, the codes previously developed for the depolarizing channel can be translated to codes for the emergent Gaussian channel, and it is easier to further optimize these codes for the simpler emergent Gaussian channel. Translating these codes back to the depolarizing channel leads to extremely good input states for the coherent information of the depolarizing channel producing new lower bounds on the noise threshold for positive capacity. In addition to improved lower bounds on the threshold, this newly found link between depolarizing noise and Gaussian channels offers a novel perspective contributing to our understanding of these symmetric codes.

</details>


### [125] [Theory of spacetime quantum fault tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39758)
*Yijia Xu,Yixu Wang,Zi-Wen Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的Clifford时空量子容错代数理论，基于张量网络构建时空链复形，统一了多种容错表述并涵盖多种容错模型与构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子容错领域存在多种分散的表述（时空码、Gottesman gadget框架、fault complexes、ZX演算等），以及分别建立的容错模型（唯象模型与电路级模型），缺乏统一的代数框架来刻画故障传播、探测子综合征与逻辑作用。

Method: 从电路的张量网络表示出发，构建时空链复形，通过相关的gauge与stabilizer结构来编码故障传播、探测子综合征及逻辑动作。

Result: 该理论(1)统一了多种量子容错表述；(2)将时空距离与Gottesman形式中的gadget级正确性性质相联系；(3)通过不同张量分解与故障分配，自然容纳唯象与电路级等故障模型；(4)复原了大量已知容错构造与结果，如重复综合征提取、横向与折叠横向门的关联探测子、Steane型和Knill型综合征提取gadget以及单次纠错。

Conclusion: 该代数理论为量子容错提供了一个统一的时空框架，将此前分别表述的描述纳入共同代数体系，并涵盖众多已知构造作为特例，具有广泛的适用性与统一性。

Abstract: We present a unified algebraic theory of Clifford spacetime quantum fault tolerance, which underpins universal fault-tolerant quantum computation. We construct a spacetime chain complex from a tensor network representation of a circuit that encodes fault propagation, detector syndromes, and logical actions through the associated gauge and stabilizer structure. We show that this theory (1) unifies various formulations of quantum fault tolerance including spacetime codes, Gottesman's gadget framework, fault complexes, and ZX calculus; (2) connects spacetime distances to the gadget-level correctness properties in Gottesman's formalism; (3) naturally accommodates a range of fault models, in particular phenomenological and circuit-level models, bringing these often separately formulated descriptions into a common algebraic framework through different tensor decompositions and fault assignments; and (4) recovers many known fault tolerance constructions and results as special cases, including repeated syndrome extraction, correlated detectors for transversal and fold-transversal gates, Steane- and Knill-type syndrome extraction gadgets, and single-shot error correction.

</details>


### [126] [Measurement and feedforward circuits from quantum error correcting codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39764)
*Georgios Styliaris,Rahul Trivedi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了测量与前馈量子电路协议与量子纠错码之间的一般对应关系：测量前的电路充当编码器，酉前馈能消除后选择当且仅当测量投影算符是码空间上的可检测错误；由此将态制备与全局酉实现转化为码设计问题，并揭示了稳定子码下非稳定性的来源及其突破途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量与前馈能增强浅层量子电路能力，可确定性实现全局酉操作并制备长程纠缠态，但此类协议缺乏统一的理论框架，其能力来源与内在限制尚不清楚。

Method: 建立测量与前馈协议与量子纠错码的一般对应：将测量前的电路视为编码器，分析酉前馈消除后选择的条件，即测量投影算符须为码空间上的可检测错误；并针对稳定子码与Pauli测量情形分析非稳定性的来源，进而采用非Pauli测量或非加性码构造新协议。

Result: 证明了酉前馈消除后选择当且仅当测量投影算符是码空间上的可检测错误；对Pauli测量的稳定子码，所得操作虽可非Clifford，但其非稳定性完全源于编码器；利用非Pauli测量或非加性码可突破该限制，构造出仅需单量子比特酉修正即可产生长程非稳定性的协议。

Conclusion: 测量与前馈协议可统一为码设计问题，为态制备与全局酉实现提供共同框架；稳定子码框架下非稳定性受限于编码器，而通过非Pauli测量或非加性码可生成长程非稳定性且仅需单量子比特修正。

Abstract: Measurements and feedforward enhance the power of shallow quantum circuits, enabling the deterministic implementation of global unitary operations and the preparation of long-range entangled states. We establish a general correspondence between all such protocols and quantum error-correcting codes: the circuit preceding the measurements acts as an encoder, and unitary feedforward can eliminate post-selection if and only if the measurement projectors are detectable errors on the codespace. This correspondence provides a common framework for state preparation and implementation of global unitaries with measurements and feedforward, turning both into a code-design problem. For stabilizer codes with Pauli measurements, although the resulting operation can be non-Clifford, we show that its nonstabilizerness originates entirely from the encoder. We overcome this restriction using non-Pauli measurements or non-additive codes, constructing protocols that generate long-range nonstabilizerness while requiring only single-qubit unitary corrections.

</details>


### [127] [Double Localization for Quantum Gibbs Sampler Gaps: From an Abstract Framework to Finite-Group Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39802)
*Ryu Hayakawa,Angus Southwell,Caesnan M. G. Leditto,Kuo-Chin Chen,Min-Hsiu Hsieh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出"双重局域化"框架，通过几何局域化与界面局域化两个互补操作，证明量子Gibbs采样的谱隙；并将其应用于有限群量子双模型的无plaquette哈密顿量，给出与图规模无关的一致谱隙下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子Gibbs采样的谱隙（gap）控制其收敛速度，但一般模型的全局谱隙估计困难，尤其是需要处理非不变子空间上的量子方向。作者希望在不需要界面在生成元下不变的前提下，将全局估计（如经典比较）与局部量子控制结合起来，从而为特定自旋模型给出与系统规模无关的谱隙保证。

Method: 提出双重局域化框架，包含：(1) 几何局域化：选取支撑在小空间区域内的更新；(2) 界面局域化：聚焦于模型定义的观测量子空间（界面），但保持几何上的全局性。谱隙定理将界面子空间的全局界与在定量耦合、几何装配条件下的局部量子谱隙估计相结合，给出显式的全动力学谱隙下界，且不要求界面在生成元下不变。应用于Kitaev有限群量子双构造中顶点项构成、无plaquette项的哈密顿量，定义在有限简单三正则、girth至少为6的图上，针对指定的局部Davies动力学。

Result: 对任意固定的非平凡有限群和每个固定的逆温度β（0≤βJ<log(5/3)，J>0为耦合强度），证明谱隙下界为与图规模无关的正常数。对最小非阿贝尔群S₃，双重局域化给出在图规模和整个区间0≤βJ≤log 3上一致的正下界，这是现有结果无法直接推出的保证。

Conclusion: 双重局域化框架通过分离全局界面估计与局部量子控制，为量子Gibbs采样的谱隙提供了统一且显式的下界工具；应用于量子双顶点模型表明，模型特定的有限计算可以扩展完整量子动力学的谱隙保证。

Abstract: We introduce double localization, a framework for proving spectral gaps of quantum Gibbs samplers through two complementary operations: geometric localization, which selects updates supported in small spatial regions, and interface localization, which focuses on a model-defined subspace of observables while remaining geometrically global. Our abstract gap theorem combines a global bound on this subspace, called the interface, with local quantum gap estimates under quantitative coupling and geometric assembly conditions. The resulting explicit lower bound controls the gap of the full dynamics without requiring the interface to be invariant under the generator. This separation allows global estimates, including classical comparison, to be combined with local control of the remaining quantum directions.
  We apply the framework to Hamiltonians built from the vertex terms of Kitaev's finite-group quantum double construction, without plaquette terms, on finite simple three-regular graphs of girth at least six. For the specified local Davies dynamics, we prove an unconditional spectral-gap lower bound by a positive constant independent of graph size for every fixed nontrivial finite group and each fixed inverse temperature $β$ with $0\leβJ<\log(5/3)$, where $J>0$ is the coupling strength. For the smallest non-Abelian group $S_3$, double localization yields a positive lower bound uniform in both graph size and the entire interval $0\leβJ\le\log 3$, providing a guarantee that does not follow directly from existing results. This demonstrates how model-specific finite calculations can extend spectral-gap guarantees for the full quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [128] [Learning Random Quantum Circuits and the Emergence of Pseudorandomness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39821)
*Srinivasan Arunachalam,Qizhao Huang,Makrand Sinha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种高效学习k维brickwork随机量子电路的算法，仅需U作用于全零态的副本即可高概率恢复原电路，时间复杂度为poly(n,2^{ℓd})；在ℓd=O(log n)时为多项式时间，并给出独立于矩阵维数的Carbery-Wright型反集中不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机量子电路学习的复杂度阈值与伪随机性密切相关：伪随机态需要ℓd=ω(log n)。此前方法受限于需学习整个反向光锥的瓶颈，难以触及该自然边界。作者希望设计算法达到或接近该阈值，并澄清伪随机性出现的条件。

Method: 核心思想是局部关联判据，可直接识别最后一层的门而无需学习其完整反向光锥，从而绕开先前方法的瓶颈。关键技术工具是针对Haar随机酉变换的低次多项式的Carbery-Wright型反集中不等式，其小 ball 指数与矩阵维数无关，用于处理维数增长的门局域性。

Result: 对n个站点、深度d、随机2ℓ量子比特门的电路，算法以poly(n,2^{ℓd})时间高概率学习原电路U，适用于任意常数维格点；当ℓd=O(log n)时为多项式时间。在一维中达到伪随机性所暗示的自然边界（伪随机态需ℓd=ω(log n)），高维情形下同一尺度可能仍是无辅助比特伪随机性的阈值。
