Conclusion: 该算法通过局部关联判据与维数无关的反集中不等式，有效学习随机量子电路，逼近伪随机性的自然边界，并厘清了该设定下伪随机性出现的条件；反集中结果本身也可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: We give an efficient algorithm for learning $k$-dimensional brickwork random quantum circuits using only copies of the output state obtained by applying $U$ to the all-zero input. For a depth-$d$ circuit on $n$ sites with random $2\ell$-qubit gates, the algorithm learns the original circuit $U$ with high probability in $\text{poly}(n,2^{\ell d})$ time for every constant dimensional lattice. In particular, the algorithm is polynomial time as long as $\ell d = O(\log n)$. In one dimension, this reaches the natural boundary suggested by pseudorandomness: pseudorandom states require $\ell d=ω(\log n)$, and structured cryptographic constructions suggest that this scale may be achievable from above. In higher dimensions, it remains plausible that the same $\ell d=ω(\log n)$ scale remains the threshold for ancilla-free pseudorandomness, and our results help clarify the conditions under which pseudorandomness can arise in this setting.
  The main idea behind our algorithm is a local correlation criterion that identifies gates in the final layer without learning their entire backward light cones, avoiding a bottleneck in the previous approaches. A key technical ingredient is a Carbery-Wright type anticoncentration inequality for low-degree polynomials of Haar random unitaries whose small-ball exponent is independent of the matrix dimension. The dimension-independent exponent is crucial for handling gates of growing locality. This anticoncentration result may also be of independent interest.

</details>


### [129] [Improved Quantum Random Self-Reduction for Linear Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39823)
*Vahid R. Asadi,Shuichi Hirahara,Nobutaka Shimizu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上线性问题的量子随机自归约，给出一种统一的量子归约，在 $\widetilde{O}(nT^{1/3})$ 时间内以至少 $2/3$ 概率计算 $Mx$，改进了此前 $\widetilde{O}(n^{3/2}+T)$ 的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进 Asadi 等人 (SODA 2024) 的 $\widetilde{O}(n^{3/2}+T)$ 归约。此前方法需要显式学习 Bogolyubov–Ruzsa 子空间（或其正交补的基），计算代价高昂，因此希望避免显式学习该子空间以降低复杂度。

Method: 利用加性组合学保证存在的 Bogolyubov–Ruzsa 子空间，但不显式学习它、也不恢复其正交补的基。核心技术步骤是将输入分解为稀疏片段，并基于振幅放大 (amplitude amplification) 的量子搜索寻找位于 Bogolyubov–Ruzsa 子空间之外的向量，从而在查询平均情形 oracle 的开销与验证矩阵-向量乘积的开销之间实现可调权衡。

Result: 对于常数域大小与常数 $\varepsilon$，当 $n\le T\le n^{3/2}$ 时，归约在时间 $\widetilde{O}(nT^{1/3})$ 内以至少 $2/3$ 概率计算任意给定输入 $x$ 的 $Mx$。特别地，当 $T=\widetilde{O}(n)$ 时运行时间为 $\widetilde{O}(n^{4/3})$，优于先前 $\widetilde{O}(n^{3/2}+T)$ 的归约。

Conclusion: 通过避免显式学习 Bogolyubov–Ruzsa 子空间并结合振幅放大量子搜索，实现了更快的量子随机自归约，并在 oracle 查询代价与矩阵-向量乘积验证代价之间提供了可调权衡。

Abstract: We study quantum random self-reductions for linear problems over finite fields. Let $M\in\mathbb{F}^{n\times n}$ be an arbitrary matrix, and let $\mathcal{O}$ be an oracle that agrees with the linear map $x\mapsto Mx$ on an $\varepsilon$-fraction of inputs $x\sim\mathbb{F}^n$. Given coherent access to $\mathcal{O}$ and coherent entry access to $M$, we give a uniform quantum reduction that computes $Mx$ on any prescribed input $x$ with probability at least $2/3$ in time $\widetilde{O}(nT^{1/3})$, for $n\le T\le n^{3/2}$ and constant field size and $\varepsilon$, where $T$ is the cost of one coherent query to $\mathcal{O}$. In particular, when $T=\widetilde{O}(n)$, the reduction runs in time $\widetilde{O}(n^{4/3})$, improving the $\widetilde{O}(n^{3/2}+T)$ reduction of Asadi, Golovnev, Gur, Shinkar, and Subramanian (SODA 2024).
  Our reduction uses the Bogolyubov--Ruzsa subspace guaranteed by additive combinatorics, but it avoids learning this subspace explicitly, which was computationally expensive for the previous reduction; in particular, it does not recover a basis for its orthogonal complement. The main technical step is to decompose the inputs into sparse pieces and find a vector that lies outside the Bogolyubov--Ruzsa subspace via a quantum search based on amplitude amplification. This yields a tunable tradeoff between the cost of querying the average-case oracle and the cost of verifying matrix-vector products.

</details>


### [130] [Dynamic distributed quantum sensing of radio-frequency fields via time-bin entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39831)
*Vedansh Nehra,Richard Birrittella,Benjamin Malia,Nicholas J. Barton,Christopher Tison,James Schneeloch,David Hucul,Benjamin Kyle,Erin Sheridan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于时间-bin纠缠量子比特的离散变量分布式量子传感框架，实现动态射频信号相干映射与灵敏度增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于偏振Bell态的协议仅限静态测量，无法应对动态射频相位信号，需要一种可调、内置的时间差分采样参考机制。

Method: 用时间-bin纠缠量子比特替代偏振探针，利用时间-bin Bell态的内在时间结构作为可调的内置双时间差分采样参考；将时间-bin间隔匹配到RF或中频半周期（反极点采样），把动态RF相位信号相干映射为静态量子光学相位。理论推导结合Monte Carlo仿真验证。

Result: 相比单样本静态偏振编码，灵敏度提升6 dB，同时保持每对相对可分离标准量子极限基准的3 dB纠缠优势；Monte Carlo仿真验证了该增强效果。

Conclusion: 该方案为DV光子量子网络中的RF传感提供解决路径，有助于量子增强、频率捷变的分布式传感器阵列及时间域量子探针编码的更广泛应用。

Abstract: We propose a novel framework for discrete-variable (DV) distributed quantum sensing, wherein traditional polarization-based probes are replaced with time-bin entangled qubits. We show that the intrinsic temporal structure of time-bin Bell states can be utilized as a tunable, built-in two-time differential sampling reference for sensing radio-frequency (RF) fields. Unlike existing protocols based on polarization Bell states, which are limited to static measurements, our proposed architecture enables the coherent mapping of dynamic RF phase signals onto static quantum optical phases. By matching the time-bin separation to the RF or intermediate frequency (IF) half-period (antipodal sampling), we theoretically demonstrate that our protocol provides a 6 dB sensitivity enhancement over one-sample static polarization encodings while preserving the 3 dB per-pair entanglement advantage over the separable standard quantum limit benchmark. The performance enhancement is validated with Monte Carlo simulations. This approach provides a solution for RF sensing in DV photonic quantum networks, offering a clear path toward quantum-enhanced and frequency-agile distributed sensor arrays and broader utilization of time-domain quantum probe encodings.

</details>


### [131] [All-Microwave Multiqubit Gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39834)
*Guanqi Wang,Yao Song,Peng-Bo Li,Xiu-Hao Deng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出全微波方案，在可调频率transmon网络中利用cross-resonance驱动一步实现多比特门，三比特门保真度超99.9%（60 ns），并扩展验证五比特CXXXX可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真多比特门对降低近term量子处理器与容错架构的电路深度至关重要，但多比特相互作用与纠缠的实现仍是限制多比特门落地的关键挑战，传统共振方案的内禀两体ZX耦合强度受限且往往依赖可调耦合器。

Method: 针对频率可调超导transmon网络，设计包含n个控制比特与m个目标比特的全微波单步多比特门方案：通过cross-resonance（CR）驱动诱导有效两体ZX相互作用；采用quad frame旋转与严格块对角化（block diagonalization）联合解析推导系统有效哈密顿量，从而定位同时增强目标耦合、抑制ZXX等寄生高阶项的最优参数区间；结合数值模拟与基于梯度的脉冲波形优化进行验证。

Result: 三比特门在60 ns门时长内保真度超过99.9%；将优化扩展至五比特CXXXX架构，验证了多目标驱动方案的物理可行性；解析方法可定量刻画参数区间与寄生项抑制关系。

Conclusion: 该全微波、多目标驱动框架无需可调耦合器即可实现强ZX耦合与高保真多比特门，天然对应稳定子测量操作，可用单步多比特门替代顺序两比特门分解，从而以硬件高效方式降低稳定子类操作的电路深度。

Abstract: Implementing high-fidelity multiqubit gates is critical for reducing circuit depth in near-term quantum processors and fault-tolerant architectures. However, realizing multiqubit interactions and entanglement remains a critical challenge that limits the implementation of multiqubit gates. Here we propose an all-microwave scheme to realize single-step multiqubit gates incorporating n control and m target qubits, tailored for frequency-tunable superconducting transmon networks. By leveraging cross-resonance (CR) drives, this approach induces effective two-body ZX interactions that are significantly stronger than those achieved in conventional resonant regimes, circumventing the need for tunable couplers. The system's effective Hamiltonian is analytically derived using both quad frame rotation and rigorous block diagonalization. These combined theoretical methods facilitate the identification of optimal parameter regimes that simultaneously enhance desired target couplings and suppress parasitic higher-order terms, such as ZXX interactions. Through numerical simulations and gradient-based pulse-shape optimization, we demonstrate three-qubit gates achieving fidelities exceeding 99.9% within a 60 ns gate duration. Furthermore, we evaluate the fundamental scalability of this framework by extending the optimization to a five-qubit CXXXX architecture, confirming the physical viability of the multi-target driving scheme. Because these multiqubit operations naturally emulate stabilizer measurements, this architecture provides a hardware-efficient strategy for reducing the circuit depth of stabilizer-type operations by replacing sequential two-qubit gate decompositions with single-step multiqubit gates.

</details>


### [132] [A self-tallying quantum anonymous voting protocol for multiple-selection elections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39835)
*Guang-Bao Xu,Meng-Na Li,Yu-Guang Yang,Dong-Huan Jiang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出首个支持多选投票的自计票量子匿名投票协议，利用纠缠态分组测量编码选票，满足自计票、不可重用、可验证与公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自计票量子匿名投票（SQAV）协议受设计难题限制，均只支持单选投票，无法支持多选投票，功能受限。

Method: 将 $nm$ 个 $n$-粒子纠缠态均分为 $n$ 组，每组纠缠态的 $n$ 个粒子分别分发给 $n$ 个选民（每人每态一粒子）。每个选民依次测量其属于 $n$ 个不同组的全部粒子，得到 $n$ 个投票向量，并将投票信息编码到与秘密索引对应的向量中。借助纠缠关联，选民可通过计算验证自己的选票是否正确，任意参与者也可通过计算获得各候选人得票数。

Result: 该协议实现了多选投票功能，并满足自计票、不可重用性、可验证性和公平性。

Conclusion: 所提协议突破了SQAV仅支持单选投票的限制，将量子匿名投票扩展至多选场景，同时保留关键安全属性。

Abstract: Self-tallying quantum anonymous voting (SQAV) has drawn considerable attention since it was proposed. However, constrained by design challenges, all existing protocols only accommodate single-selection voting and cannot support multi-selection voting. In this paper, we propose a SQAV protocol with multi-selection voting functionality. In our protocol, $nm$ $n$-particle entangled states are equally divided into $n$ groups, and the $n$ particles in each entangled state are delivered to $n$ voters, one particle per voter. Each voter obtains $n$ voting vectors by sequentially measuring all his (or her) particles that belong to $n$ different group, and then encodes his or her voting information into the vector corresponding to the secret index. Due to entanglement correlation, each voter can verify whether his or her ballot is correct through computation, while any participant can obtain the vote count for each candidate through computation. Our protocol satisfies self-tallying, nonreusability, verifiability, and fairness.

</details>


### [133] [Natural Barriers to Quantum Extraction: On the Post-Quantum (In)security of (O)EKE and Masny-Rindal OT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39844)
*James Bartusek,Jake Januzelli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明EKE族协议与Masny-Rindal OT在量子多项式时间敌手下不具备UC安全性（即便使用后量子KEM），但二者仍可达到若干基于博弈的安全性概念，并给出紧致优势的单向到隐藏引理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EKE与Masny-Rindal OT因简洁、可插拔（可自由选择KEM）及经典模型下的UC安全证明，被视为实用化PAKE与OT的首选方案；在后量子KEM标准化的背景下，其能否直接迁移为高效后量子PAKE/OT取决于UC安全是否在量子敌手下成立。

Method: 构造量子多项式时间敌手策略，针对一般(O)EKE协议族与Masny-Rindal OT，证明其可阻止模拟器提取输入（PAKE中的口令、OT中接收方选择位），从而推翻UC安全；同时分析两编译器的基于博弈的安全性，并证明一个新的“优势紧致”的单向到隐藏（one-way to hiding）引理。

Result: 一般(O)EKE协议族与Masny-Rindal OT均非量子UC安全，即使实例化于后量子KEM之上；但两个编译器在量子模型下仍可满足特定基于博弈的安全定义。

Conclusion: 这类编译器的经典UC安全证明不能迁移到量子敌手模型，不能仅凭后量子KEM即宣称得到量子UC安全的PAKE/OT；其安全保证应退化为相应的博弈型安全，所提出的紧致单向到隐藏引理或有独立价值。

Abstract: Encrypted key exchange (EKE), introduced by Bellovin and Merritt (IEEE S\&P 1992), and Masny-Rindal OT, introduced by Masny and Rindal (ACM CCS 2019), are highly-efficient methods for compiling essentially any KEM into advanced cryptographic protocols, namely password-authenticated key exchange (PAKE) and oblivious transfer (OT), by relying only on idealized symmetric-key primitives. They have become leading candidates for practically-implementable PAKE and OT due to (1) their simplicity, (2) their plug-and-play nature, allowing for flexibility in the choice of KEM, and (3) existing proofs of UC-security (in the classical adversarial model).
  Due to point (2) above, these compilers yield attractive candidates for efficient \emph{post-quantum} PAKE and OT, especially given the recent post-quantum KEM standardization efforts. This motivates the question of whether the (UC-)security of these compilers translates to the quantum adversarial model. In this work, we show that it does not. In particular, we prove that a general family of (O)EKE protocols, as well as Masny-Rindal OT, are \emph{not} UC-secure against quantum polynomial-time adversaries, even when instantiated with a post-quantum KEM. To establish UC-insecurity, we devise an adversarial strategy that provably thwarts any attempt by the simulator to extract its input (the password in the case of PAKE, and the receiver's choice bit in the case of OT).
  To complement these negative results, we establish that both compilers yield certain notions of \emph{game-based} security. Along the way, we establish a novel ``advantage-tight'' one-way to hiding lemma that may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [134] [Optical measurement is almost quantum: Quantum inspired universal optical bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39857)
*Daniel Ish,Chase T. Ellis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文借鉴量子信息理论，提出了一种抽象化的光学传感形式体系，剥离具体光学系统细节，仅基于待测物理量推导测量性能的普适界限，并证明该界限由唯一测量方案达到，且该体系与量子形式体系几乎完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光学传感研究多依赖具体光学系统与技术细节，缺乏统一的理论框架来刻画测量的根本极限，因此作者希望建立一种与具体技术无关、仅由待测量决定的一般性理论边界。

Method: 借鉴量子信息中的形式化方法，构建抽象的光学传感形式体系，定义待测状态与测量操作等概念，并推导普适的性能界限，同时分析达到该界限的测量方案。

Result: 发现一个控制所有被动光学系统性能的普适界限，该界限不依赖系统技术实现方式；对每个待测量，存在唯一测量方案可达到该界限；该光学形式体系与量子形式体系几乎相同，包含类似的状态、测量操作以及相应的不确定性原理。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了光学传感的普适理论框架，揭示了光学与量子形式体系之间深刻的相似性，为光学测量极限提供了统一描述，具有重要的理论与应用意义。

Abstract: Inspired by results in quantum information, we report the development of a formalism for optical sensing which abstracts the details of specific optical systems and allows us to form general bounds on measurement based only on the quantity to be measured. We find a universal bound that controls the performance of any passive optical system regardless of the technology used to construct it and that this bound is attained by a unique measurement scheme for each quantity of interest. Shockingly, our optical formalism is nearly identical to the quantum formalism, with essentially the same notions of the state to be measured and the operation of the measurement, along with an accompanying uncertainty principle.

</details>


### [135] [Reverse quantum state diffusion from differential geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39861)
*Dinh-Long Vu,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将量子态扩散（Lindblad方程的纯态随机轨迹解）推广到时序反演，在Stratonovich形式下推导纯态流形上的反向随机微分方程，用以构建逆Lindbladian的非线性随机解，并借助score-based生成模型思想从先验分布生成接近原始的系综，且对去极化信道给出了误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态扩散将Lindblad主方程拆解为纯态随机轨迹，其轨迹系综在纯态流形上的概率密度比密度矩阵（一阶矩）包含更多信息。作者希望刻画这一扩散的时间反演，从而获得逆Lindbladian的随机解，并将其与生成建模联系以重构系综。

Method: 在流形上以Stratonovich形式写出一般扩散的反演，并限制到纯态流形，得到态矢量的反向随机微分方程（适用于任意Hilbert空间维度与任意Lindbladian）；该反向方程为逆Lindbladian提供仅对该系综成立的物理非线性随机解。随后仿照score-based生成模型，用反向方程生成接近原始的新系综。

Result: 对去极化信道，该随机解为复射影空间上的布朗运动，其平稳分布在任意维度下均为纯态均匀分布。以该分布为先验启动反向方程，在精确/学习到的score、连续/离散时间等不同设定下，给出了生成分布相对原始分布的误差界。

Conclusion: 纯态流形上的时间反演扩散给出了逆Lindbladian的系综依赖随机解，并可作为量子系综的生成式建模框架；去极化信道的平稳性分析与多设定误差界为该方法的正确性和可控性提供了理论保证。

Abstract: Quantum state diffusion unravels a Lindblad master equation into stochastic trajectories of pure states. The ensemble of trajectories is described by a probability density on the manifold of pure states, which contains much more information than the density matrix, its first moment. We derive the time reversal of this diffusion. Writing the reversal of a general diffusion on a manifold in Stratonovich form, and passing through the manifold of pure states, we obtain a backward stochastic differential equation for the state vector, valid for any Hilbert space dimension and any Lindbladian. The backward stochastic equation provides a physical, nonlinear unravelling of the inverse Lindbladian that holds only for the ensemble from which it was constructed. Following score-based generative models, we then use the backward equation to generate a new ensemble close to the original. For the depolarizing channel the unravelling is a Brownian motion on complex projective space, so the uniform distribution of pure states is its stationary law in any dimension. Starting the backward equation from that prior, we bound the error of the resulting distribution compared to the original one in different settings: exact and learned scores, continuous and discrete time.

</details>


### [136] [Reducing the Entanglement Cost of Distributed Bipartite Quantum Computation with Constant Qubit Overhead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39865)
*Kosuke Matsui,Jun-Yi Wu,Min-Hsiu Hsieh,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明：在纠缠辅助分布式量子计算模型中，任意二分Clifford酉算子的纠缠代价可达到算子Schmidt秩下界，且每台QPU仅需至多2个辅助量子比特；对T-count为t的Clifford+T酉算子，纠缠代价至多超出该下界2t个Bell对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式量子计算需通过量子通信连接多个QPU协同完成大规模计算，而单台QPU量子比特数有限，因此需在保持低量子比特开销的同时降低量子通信量。已有结果表明算子Schmidt秩给出纠缠代价的一般下界（量子比特开销不受限时），但常数量子比特开销下能否逼近该下界尚不明确。

Method: 在纠缠辅助模型中，以纠缠消耗度量量子通信代价；针对二分酉算子，分析其算子Schmidt秩下界与常数辅助量子比特开销下的可实现性。对Clifford酉算子构造达到下界的实现；对由Clifford+T精确分解给出、T-count为t的非Clifford酉算子，构造同样量子比特开销的实现并分析其纠缠代价上界。

Result: 任意二分Clifford酉算子均可在每台QPU至多2个辅助量子比特下达到算子Schmidt秩下界；对T-count为t的Clifford+T酉算子，所构造实现的纠缠代价至多比该下界高2t个Bell对。

Conclusion: 算子Schmidt秩下界在常数量子比特开销下对Clifford酉算子是可紧的，对Clifford+T酉算子其额外纠缠代价由T-count线性控制，表明低量子比特开销下逼近通信下界具有可行性。

Abstract: Distributed quantum computation connects multiple quantum processing units (QPUs) through quantum communication to jointly perform large-scale quantum computations. Since the number of qubits available at each QPU is limited, it is important to reduce quantum communication while keeping the qubit overhead small. To this end, we study an entanglement-assisted model, in which entanglement consumption serves as a measure of quantum communication cost. For exact deterministic implementations in this model, the operator Schmidt rank of the target bipartite unitary provides a general lower bound on entanglement cost when qubit overhead is unrestricted. However, it has remained unclear how closely this bound can be approached with constant qubit overhead. In this work, we show that this lower bound is attainable for every bipartite Clifford unitary using at most two auxiliary qubits per QPU. In addition, for non-Clifford unitaries specified by exact Clifford+$T$ decompositions with $T$-count $t$, we construct implementations with the same qubit overhead whose entanglement cost is at most $2t$ Bell pairs above this lower bound.

</details>


### [137] [Fault Tolerant Quantum Phases of Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39879)
*Colin V. Coane,Shouzhen Gu,Aleksander Kubica*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息保持与容错传输能力的混合态量子相分类，用浅层局域信道电路（辅以局域测量与全局经典通信）定义相等价，证明存在局域噪声恢复阈值是整相的性质，并给出子系统的码分类及2D/3D颜色码的相等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有量子相分类多针对纯态或平衡态，缺乏对混合态下容错量子信息能力的刻画；同时子系统码分类依赖规范子系统特定状态，且需理解综合征结构（点状vs扩展）对容错性的影响。

Method: 定义由浅层局域信道电路、局域测量与测量结果全局经典通信联系的两组态为相等价，且所有量子操作（含测量）需噪声鲁棒；以此研究局域噪声恢复阈值是否为相不变量，并分析有限深度电路连接态集的归属；进一步分析测量电路中综合征结构对容错性影响。

Result: 证明局域噪声恢复阈值的存续是整相性质；任意两组非零阈值态若由有限深度局域信道电路相连，则属同一容错相；得到不偏向规范子系统任何状态的子系统码分类，并借助3D规范颜色码中的维度跃迁证明2D与3D颜色码相等价；此外，具扩展综合征的qudit稳定子码可单次态制备，而测量点状综合征的浅层电路（含2D拓扑码与非阿贝尔拓扑序可解任意子理论态制备）不具容错性。

Conclusion: 该框架将容错能力纳入混合态量子相分类，给出噪鲁棒相等价判据，统一子系统码分类并揭示颜色码跨维度相等价，同时表明综合征结构决定单次容错可实现性。

Abstract: We introduce a classification of quantum phases of mixed states based on the ability to preserve and fault tolerantly transfer information. We formulate phase equivalence between sets of states that encode logical quantum information and are connected by shallow local channel circuits assisted by local measurements and global classical communication of measurement outcomes, where all quantum operations, including measurements, must be robust to noise. We show that the existence of a recovery threshold for local noise is a property of an entire phase. We prove that any two sets of states with nonzero thresholds that are related by finite-depth local channel circuits belong to the same fault tolerant phase. Our approach leads to a classification of subsystem codes without favoring any state of the gauge subsystem, and we demonstrate phase equivalence between 2D and 3D color codes using dimensional jumps within the 3D gauge color code. Lastly, we study how syndrome structure affects fault tolerance in circuits with measurements. We show that qudit stabilizer codes with extended syndromes permit single-shot state preparation, whereas a broad class of shallow circuits measuring point-like syndromes, including state preparation for 2D topological codes and non-Abelian topological orders with solvable anyon theories, is not fault tolerant.

</details>


### [138] [Quantum State Routing and Perfect State Transfer on Signed Graphs under Environmental Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39890)
*Nur Mohammad Sanfui,Supriyo Dutta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于边复制符号图上硬币式Szegedy量子行走的确定性拓扑量子路由架构，通过将边符号作为平衡硬币反射中的局域相移来消除背散射，在符号哑铃开关与可扩展粘合二叉树等结构中实现单位保真度的确定性完美态传输（PST），并分析了开放系统振幅与相位阻尼噪声下的路由性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式通信网络中路由未知量子信息需要无需测量、能防止波函数坍缩的自主机制。Szegedy量子行走可用于空间态输运，但传统无权图上的行走存在严重的背反射、空间色散和信道串扰问题，因此需要新的架构来克服这些缺陷。

Method: 在边复制符号图上构建硬币式Szegedy量子行走：将边符号视为平衡硬币反射中的局域相移，从而在路由节点上强制满足严格的零背散射条件。

Result: 在符号哑铃开关D_{2m,0,2n}和可扩展粘合二叉树等基本结构上，实现了在精确到达时刻单位保真度的确定性完美态传输；同时分析了在真实开放系统振幅阻尼与相位阻尼噪声信道下的路由性能。

Conclusion: 该架构为分布式量子网络中的自主、无测量量子信息路由提供了确定性且拓扑鲁棒的方案，并给出了其在现实噪声条件下的性能评估。

Abstract: Routing unknown quantum information across distributed communication networks requires autonomous, measurement-free mechanisms to prevent wave-function collapse. Szegedy quantum walks provide a mechanism for spatial state transport. The conventional walks on unweighted graphs suffer from severe back-reflection, spatial dispersion, and channel crosstalk. In this paper, we introduce a deterministic topological quantum routing architecture based on coined Szegedy quantum walks on edge-duplicated signed graphs. By treating edge signs as localized phase shifts within a balanced coin reflection, we enforce an exact zero back-scattering condition across routing nodes. We demonstrate deterministic Perfect State Transfer (PST) with unit fidelity at exact arrival times across fundamental archetypes, including the signed dumbbell switch $D_{2m,0,2n}$ and scalable glued binary trees. Furthermore, we analyse routing performance under realistic open-system amplitude and phase damping noise channels.

</details>


### [139] [Quantum Sensing and Hamiltonian Learning under Stochastic Parameter Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39908)
*Kelvin Koor,Patrick Rebentrost*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出随机演化哈密顿量（SEH）概念，即系数由随机微分方程（SDE）支配的局部哈密顿量，并研究其哈密顿量学习问题，特别是Heisenberg极限能否保持的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望将量子信息处理中的控制参数从确定性推广到由随机微分方程支配的随机情形，以探索更广泛的量子信息处理设定。

Method: 首先建立SEH的理论基础；随后重新分析并扩展Huang-Tong-Fang-Su协议（首个达到哈密顿量系数学习Heisenberg极限的协议），并发展量子传感与哈密顿量模拟中的若干工具。

Result: 从理论上刻画了在SEH情形下Heisenberg极限能够保持和无法保持的区间，并对原协议作出必要扩展。

Conclusion: 该工作为随机控制参数下的哈密顿量学习提供了理论框架，并鼓励探索更多系数由SDE支配的量子信息处理设定。

Abstract: We introduce the notion of stochastically evolving Hamiltonians (SEHs), which are local Hamiltonians whose coefficients are governed by stochastic differential equations (SDEs). After developing some theoretical aspects of these objects, we consider the task of Hamiltonian learning for SEHs. We reanalyse and make necessary extensions to the Huang-Tong-Fang-Su protocol, which first achieved the Heisenberg limit for Hamiltonian coefficient learning, under these circumstances. We delineate theoretically the regimes where the Heisenberg limit can and cannot be retained. Along the way we develop a few tools in quantum sensing and Hamiltonian simulation that may be of independent interest under similar settings in quantum information processing. The broad aim is to encourage the exploration of various settings in quantum information processing in which the control parameters are governed by SDEs.

</details>


### [140] [Distillation of N-Qubit Stabilizer States on a Star Network Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39916)
*Theodore M. Mahaffey,Chaohan Cui,Saikat Guha,Murphy Yuezhen Niu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于任意[[n,k,d]]稳定子码的纠缠蒸馏协议，可将n份N量子比特GHZ态在Pauli噪声下转化为k份逻辑拷贝，在星型网络拓扑上高效可扩展、仅需局域操作，并通过数值实验与稳定子形式化证明验证其性能与普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在噪声信道中分发高保真纠缠态是量子网络的核心需求，需要一种可扩展、仅依赖局域操作且能抑制闲置量子比特错误的纠缠蒸馏方案，以支持任意数量量子处理器间的高保真逻辑纠缠分发。

Method: 利用任意[[n,k,d]]稳定子码构造纠缠蒸馏协议：在星型网络拓扑上对N量子比特GHZ态的n份原始拷贝进行编码，生成k份逻辑拷贝；仅使用局域操作并最小化闲置物理比特受错误影响。采用5量子比特码、环面码和[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车码进行数值实验，针对所有量子比特上的独立单比特去极化噪声。并用稳定子形式化方法证明协议可推广至任意N量子比特CSS稳定子态。

Result: 在去极化率高达或超过6%时，所有测试码的编码方案相较直接发送单份裸GHZ态均提高了最终GHZ态保真度；5量子比特码、环面码和[[144,12,12]]双变量自行车码的实验均验证了该提升。

Conclusion: 该协议在星型拓扑上对任意N高效可扩展、仅需局域操作并降低闲置比特错误影响，可分发高保真逻辑纠缠态；稳定子形式化证明其适用于任意N量子比特CSS稳定子态（GHZ为特例），明确了既往基于稳定子的Bell对与GHZ蒸馏方案的工作原理并将其推广，且部分稳定子码可用该协议蒸馏任意稳定子态。

Abstract: We introduce an entanglement distillation protocol that utilizes an arbitrary $[[n,k,d]]$ stabilizer code to convert $n$ raw copies of an $N$-qubit Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) state into $k$ logical copies in the presence of Pauli noise. Our explicit formulation of the scheme on a star network topology is efficiently scalable to arbitrary $N$, requires only local operations, and minimizes the impact of errors on idle physical qubits, enabling the distribution of high-fidelity entangled logical states between any number of quantum processors. We report the results of numerical experiments using the 5-qubit code, toric code, and $[[144,12,12]]$ bivariate bicyle code to protect against independent single-qubit depolarizing noise on all qubits. Our encoding scheme improves the final GHZ state fidelity relative to a single bare GHZ state sent over the same noisy channel for depolarizing rates up to or above $6 \%$ for all codes tested. Additionally, we use the stabilizer formalism to prove that our protocol can be applied to any $N$-qubit Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) stabilizer state, from which the GHZ state emerges as a special case. This result explicitly establishes the working principle of previous stabilizer-based Bell pair and GHZ state distillation schemes, and generalizes such protocols beyond the traditionally considered states. We also show that some stabilizer codes can distill any stabilizer state with our protocol.

</details>


### [141] [Verifiable quantum advantage based on polynomials with planted structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39918)
*Markus Bläser,Michael Gullans,Dominik Hangleiter,Yuxuan Liu,Youming Qiao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"模拟秘密"(simulation secrets)思想用于可验证量子优势：验证者利用模拟秘密可比亚经典对手更快完成交叉熵测试，并在带有植入独立空间的立方多项式IQP电路上实例化，在两条猜想下实现验证时间与经典对手通过时间之间的多项式差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子优势理论的核心问题之一：是否存在与随机电路采样资源需求相当、且仅凭量子计算的经典输出即可验证的量子优势协议。现有方案在可验证性与资源开销之间难以兼顾。

Method: 提出simulation secrets概念：验证者持有模拟秘密后，能以快于经典对手的速度评估交叉熵测试。用由立方多项式描述的IQP电路实例化，这些多项式带有植入的独立空间——即多项式在一般线性群轨道下最大独立集，并给出对应态的低秩稳定子分解。基于两条猜想：(1)大独立空间对计算受限对手不可见，无法借此通过协议；(2)对均匀随机多项式生成能通过交叉熵测试的样本具有特定精细复杂度。

Result: 在上述两条猜想下，该方案使验证时间与经典对手通过协议所需时间之间存在多项式差距——两者均为指数级。协议具有生成经典可认证随机性的潜在应用，因输出分布具有高最小熵。估计植入多项式方案可在约100个逻辑量子比特、逻辑错误率约10^-6下实现。

Conclusion: 模拟秘密为可验证量子优势提供了新范式，通过植入独立空间的IQP电路在合理猜想下实现多项式级验证优势，并具备可认证随机性等应用前景，且对物理实现资源要求较为适中。

Abstract: A central question in the theory of quantum advantage is whether there are quantum advantage protocols with similar resource requirements as random circuit sampling that are also verifiable just from the classical outputs of the quantum computation. Here, we develop the idea of simulation secrets for verifiable advantage. A verifier can use a simulation secret to evaluate a cross-entropy test faster than it would take a classical adversary to pass the test. We instantiate this idea using IQP circuits described by cubic polynomials with planted independent spaces. These correspond to the largest independent set in the orbit of a polynomial under the general linear group and yield a low-rank stabilizer decomposition of the corresponding state. We conjecture that large independent spaces are invisible to a computationally bounded adversary, and therefore they cannot exploit them to pass the protocol. A second conjecture regards the fine-grained complexity of producing samples that pass the cross-entropy test for uniformly random polynomials. Under these conjectures, our scheme results in a polynomial gap between the verification time and the time a classical adversary would need to pass the protocol---both are exponential. It has a potential application to generating classically certifiable randomness, since the output distributions have high min-entropy. We estimate that the planted polynomial scheme is implementable using 100 logical qubits at logical error rates around $10^{-6}$.

</details>


### [142] [Fault-tolerant cost of shallow QAOA on near-symmetric optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39922)
*Jernej Rudi Finžgar,Martin Leib,Elisabeth Wybo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文分析了低深度QAOA在容错量子计算下的编译开销，证明小角度Clifford+T旋转合成可将非Clifford门成本降至关于比特数的二次量级，并探讨了编译所需信息与经典困难性共存的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Montanaro和Zhou此前证明深度一QAOA能以常数概率求解某些近对称约束满足问题的植入解，且经典通用求解器在显式实例上呈指数级运行时间。本文追问：这种低深度QAOA电路在容错实现下的真实成本是多少。

Method: 从成本哈密顿量出发，虽然其包含Θ(n^ℓ)个约束子句，但QAOA成功对应的旋转角随n^{1-ℓ}缩小；作者采用小角度Clifford+T旋转合成技术进行编译，并分析编译电路所需的显式子句列表所带来的信息泄漏问题，进而构造使二次非Clifford缩放与经典精确优化困难性并存的函数族。

Result: 对任意子句局域性ℓ，小角度Clifford+T旋转合成将每个电路的非Clifford成本降至O~(n^2)。分析表明常数QAOA成功率所依赖的机制会固定成本函数的一阶Fourier系数，其符号可揭示植入解；但作者证明该泄漏未必直接给出解，并设计出二次非Clifford缩放与困难精确优化共存的问题族。

Conclusion: 低深度QAOA在容错实现下的非Clifford开销由小角度旋转合成主导，呈二次缩放；编译虽需要显式子句表，但由此产生的信息泄漏不必然使求解变易，二次量子成本与经典困难性可以共存。

Abstract: Montanaro and Zhou proved that depth-one Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) finds the planted solution of certain near-symmetric constraint satisfaction problems with probability $Ω(1)$, and observed exponential runtimes for general-purpose classical solvers on explicit realizations, providing strong evidence of an empirical exponential speedup of low-depth QAOA. We ask what such a circuit costs fault-tolerantly. Although the cost Hamiltonian carries $Θ(n^\ell)$ clauses, the rotation angle at which QAOA succeeds shrinks as $n^{1-\ell}$, and we show that small-angle Clifford$+T$ rotation synthesis reduces the non-Clifford cost per circuit to $\widetilde O(n^2)$ for every clause locality $\ell$. Compiling the circuit, however, requires the explicit clause list. We find that the mechanism underlying constant QAOA success fixes the degree-one Fourier coefficients of the cost, whose signs reveal the planted solution. However, this leakage need not reveal the solution: we design families in which the quadratic non-Clifford scaling and hard exact optimization coexist.

</details>


### [143] [A Few Constrain Many: Correlation-Enhanced Learning of Many-Body Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39935)
*Matteo Tedde,Davide Girolami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文指出量子可观测量之间的关联可降低量子学习的复杂度：多项式数量的测量可约束指数级多未测依赖量，从而实现高效的量子态函数估计，并在100量子比特系统上高效估计纠缠与魔法等量子资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体量子系统参数量巨大，直接刻画其性质在计算与实验上都很困难；现有层析与影子层析、神经态等方法在效率或实验可行性上存在局限，因此需要一种更高效的量子学习途径。

Method: 利用量子可观测量之间的相关性（量子约束）进行学习：选取多项式数量的测量，构建对指数级多依赖但未测量量的值界，进而估计量子态的通用函数；据此设计“关联信息学习”算法，仅使用多项式数量Pauling字符串的测量信息来估计纠缠、魔法等量子资源，且无需测量前纠缠门。

Result: 在100量子比特系统中估计纠缠与魔法等关键量子资源，相对误差低于影子层析与神经态方法；所提协议可在当今量子计算机上直接测试，因为不依赖预测量纠缠门。

Conclusion: 量子约束本身构成探索大规模量子系统的一种资源；通过利用可观测量间相关性可显著降低量子学习复杂度并提升资源估计效率。

Abstract: Discovering properties of many-body quantum systems is challenging because of the large number of parameters to determine. Here, we show that correlations among quantum observables help reduce the complexity of quantum learning. A polynomial number of selected measurements can bound the values of exponentially many dependent yet unmeasured quantities, enabling efficient estimation of general functions of quantum states. We leverage this result to design correlation-informed learning algorithms that estimate key quantum resources, such as entanglement and magic, in systems of 100 qubits. They achieve a lower relative error than shadow tomography and neural-state methods by using information about a polynomial number of measured Pauli strings. These protocols are readily testable with today's quantum computers, as they do not require premeasurement entangling gates. Quantum constraints themselves are therefore a resource for exploring large quantum systems.

</details>


### [144] [Fast and Sure-ious Quantum Process Tomography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39941)
*A. Afham,Sayantan Sen,Marco Tomamichel*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明量子过程层析中的"部分归一化"等价于Choi态上关于保迹约束在保真度意义下的精确投影，从而可将任意有保真度保证的量子态层析算法以单一闭式同余变换提升为量子过程层析算法，并据此提出Fidelity Projected Least Squares，达到1/ε的样本复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子过程层析（QPT）多通过Choi-Jamiolkowski同构从量子态层析（QST）提升而来，但需额外施加保迹（TP）约束。已有两类方法各有缺陷：经验性的部分归一化只有闭式形式却仅具渐近分析且损失关于维数的多项式因子；基于投影的方法虽有原则性但无闭式解、随维数扩展性差。作者希望弥合这一分歧。

Method: 作者首先证明部分归一化正是量子信道Choi态集合上在保真度（等价于Bures或纯化距离）意义下的精确投影。基于此，任何在保真度上有保证的QST算法都能通过一个单一闭式同余变换提升为QPT算法，该变换保持Choi秩，并可在Kraus形式下计算，无需对Choi维数做特征分解。作者据此提升两个单拷贝协议，其中非自适应的Fidelity Projected Least Squares被重点分析。

Result: 理论上证明非自适应的Fidelity Projected Least Squares能够恢复Choi秩，并在与1/ε成正比的信道使用次数下使不保真度达到ε。数值上，对于低Choi秩信道，该方法在精度上优于最先进的投影最小二乘，并在TP正则化步骤及返回估计的时间与内存上提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 部分归一化并非仅是启发式手段，而是保迹约束下的精确保真度投影，这为QPT提供了一个统一且高效的理论框架。该框架可将QST的样本复杂度保证直接提升至QPT，并在低Choi秩情形下显著优于现有方法，兼具闭式表达与良好扩展性。

Abstract: Quantum process tomography (QPT) protocols are often designed by lifting quantum state tomography (QST) protocols through the Choi-Jamiolkowski isomorphism, at the cost of an extra step enforcing the trace-preserving (TP) constraint. Existing approaches fall into two classes. The folklore partial normalization, which sandwiches the density estimate between inverse square roots of its input marginal, has a closed form but is used as a heuristic, its only analyses being asymptotic and losing a factor polynomial in the dimension. Projection-based methods are principled but have no closed forms and scale poorly with dimension. We resolve this dichotomy by showing that the partial normalization is the exact projection onto the Choi states of quantum channels in fidelity (equivalently in Bures or purified distance). Consequently, any QST algorithm with guarantees in fidelity lifts to a QPT algorithm that inherits its sample complexity, through a single closed-form congruence that preserves the Choi rank and is computed in Kraus form without any eigendecomposition of Choi dimension. We lift two single-copy protocols and prove that the non-adaptive one (Fidelity Projected Least Squares) recovers the Choi rank and reaches infidelity $\varepsilon$ from a number of channel uses proportional to $1/\varepsilon$. Numerically, for channels of low Choi rank, it improves on the state-of-the-art projected least squares in accuracy, and by orders of magnitude in the time and memory of the TP-regularization step and of the returned estimate.

</details>


### [145] [Scaling law of variational quantum algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39949)
*Ningfeng Wang,Zhengwei Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过高达5000量子比特的大规模数值实验研究变分量子算法在组合优化上的标度律，采用O(log n)线路深度的经典可模拟拟设，收敛能量与基准最优/参考能量偏差不超过0.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究变分量子算法在组合优化问题上的标度行为，验证其在量子比特规模增大时能否保持解质量，为算法的可扩展性提供实验依据。

Method: 大规模数值实验，规模扩展至n=5000量子比特，使用O(log n)线路深度且经典可模拟的拟设（ansatz）进行组合优化求解。

Result: 在n=5000量子比特规模下，收敛能量与基准中报告的最优或参考能量偏差始终不超过0.5%。

Conclusion: 变分量子算法在所用拟设与浅层线路下对组合优化表现出良好的标度性与精度，但结果的经典可模拟性提示需审慎评估其潜在量子优势。

Abstract: We study scaling law of variational quantum algorithms by large-scale numerical experiments on combinatorial optimizations. On experiments up to n = 5000 qubits with O( log(n)) circuit depth with a classically simulable ansatz, the converged energies consistently differ by no more than 0.5% from the optimal or reference energies reported in the benchmarks.

</details>


### [146] [Quantum Algorithms for Computational Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39962)
*Mario Guillaume Cecile,Nis-Luca van Hülst,Tomohiro Hashizume,Pia Siegl,Abhishek Setty,José Diogo da Costa Jesus,Paul Over,Sergio Bengoechea,Muhammad Umer,Spyros Tserkis,Eleftherios Mastorakis,Tristan Kraft,Francisco Cárdenas-López,Leonardo Scandurra,Thomas Rung,Felix Motzoi,Belda Yesil,Barbara Kraus,Martin Kiffner,Dimitris G. Angelakis,Eugene de Villiers,Dieter Jaksch*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了求解计算流体力学（CFD）偏微分方程的量子方法，涵盖全量子算法（QLSA、HHL、QSVT、哈密顿模拟、QLBM）、混合量子-经典方法（QPINN、变分PDE求解器）以及张量网络表示，并评估其优势与局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFD中偏微分方程的经典数值求解在高维、复杂几何与多尺度问题上计算代价高昂，研究者希望借助量子计算获得潜在加速，因此需要系统梳理现有量子方法以明确其能力边界与适用条件。

Method: 采用综述性分析：分别考察全量子方法（QLSA，从HHL到QSVT、哈密顿模拟、量子格子玻尔兹曼方法）与混合量子-经典方法（量子物理信息神经网络、振幅编码变分PDE求解器）；对每个框架分析其数学形式、算法结构与主要局限；引入张量网络（TN）表示以低秩形式编码CFD场、微分算子与几何信息，并转化为张量可编程变分量子算法（TP-VQA）；选取Poisson、反应、扩散及非线性模型方程作为基准问题进行评估。

Result: 分析表明，量子优势高度依赖于具体问题，主要受条件数、表示复杂度、态制备与测量约束支配；TN形式可桥接CFD离散化与量子态、算子及电路，实现紧凑表示向TP-VQA的转化；同时给出了各算法框架的适用性与局限性对比，并评估了其对流体动力学关键特征的刻画能力。

Conclusion: 面向CFD的可扩展量子算法仍面临诸多挑战，是否取得量子优势取决于问题特性与上述约束条件；未来需在算法设计、态制备、测量效率及容错架构等方面进一步突破。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive review of quantum approaches for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) arising in computational fluid dynamics (CFD). We examine fully quantum approaches, including quantum linear system algorithms (QLSAs), ranging from the Harrow--Hassidim--Lloyd (HHL) algorithm to quantum singular value transformation (QSVT), Hamiltonian simulation, and quantum lattice Boltzmann methods (QLBMs), while emphasizing hybrid quantum--classical approaches, including quantum physics-informed neural networks (QPINNs) and amplitude-encoded variational PDE solvers. We focus on hardware-agnostic algorithms compatible with present noisy processors and emerging fault-tolerant architectures. For each framework, we analyze the mathematical formulation, algorithmic structure, and principal limitations. We also examine tensor-network (TN) representations, since CFD fields, differential operators, and geometrical information can often be encoded efficiently in low-rank form. The TN formalism bridges CFD discretizations and quantum states, operators, and circuits, enabling compact representations to be translated into tensor-programmable variational quantum algorithms (TP-VQAs). We further review benchmark problems, including Poisson, reaction, diffusion, and nonlinear model equations, and assess how well quantum algorithms capture key features of fluid dynamics. Our analysis highlights that potential quantum advantage is highly problem dependent and governed by condition number, representational complexity, state preparation, and measurement constraints. We outline capabilities, limitations, and challenges toward scalable quantum algorithms for CFD.

</details>


### [147] [Single-shot state preparation threshold: rigorous theorem and statistical mechanical mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39965)
*Yuanchen Zhao,Yi Yuan,Zhengyi Han,Dong E. Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明线性confinement足以保证CSS量子LDPC码具有非零的单次态制备阈值，挑战了soundness为必要条件的传统认知，并通过统计力学映射与数值模拟进一步探讨更弱条件下的阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单轮含噪syndrome测量制备逻辑码字可降低容错量子计算的时间开销，但何种码结构性质足以保证单次态制备阈值尚不清楚，且普遍认为soundness是必要条件。本文旨在澄清这一结构条件问题。

Method: 对CSS量子LDPC码，证明线性confinement足以保证非零单次态制备阈值；在码距至少为对数时，分析非零局部随机读出与数据错误阈值下的逻辑错误概率；在线性soundness假设下证明残余数据错误为局部随机；并发展统计力学映射，将最终逻辑错误率表示为经典配分函数，对三维toric code进行Monte Carlo模拟。

Result: 线性confinement即可保证非零单次态制备阈值，与soundness必要性观点相反；当码距至少为对数时，在非零局部随机读出与数据错误阈值下，经形式理想最终恢复后的逻辑错误概率指数小并随码尺寸趋于无穷而趋近零；线性soundness下残余数据错误为局部随机，可直接与其他容错gadget复合；三维toric code数值结果表明次线性soundness亦可支持单次态制备阈值。

Conclusion: 线性confinement是保证单次态制备阈值的充分结构条件，soundness并非必要；线性soundness可保证残余错误的局部随机性以利于模块复合；统计力学映射与数值模拟提示次线性soundness也可能支持单次态制备阈值。

Abstract: Preparing logical codewords with a single round of noisy syndrome measurements can reduce the time overhead of fault-tolerant quantum computation. It remains unclear which structural properties of the code suffice to guarantee a threshold for single-shot state preparation. Here we prove that linear confinement suffices for a nonzero single-shot state-preparation threshold for CSS quantum low-density parity-check codes, in contrast to the common belief that soundness is necessary. When the distance is at least logarithmic, we prove that below nonzero local stochastic readout and data error thresholds, the logical error probability after a formal ideal final recovery is exponentially small and approaches zero when the code size approaches infinity. Under the stronger assumption of linear soundness, we show that the residual data error of the preparation protocol is local stochastic, directly allowing composition with other fault-tolerant gadgets. To investigate single-shot state-preparation threshold beyond the above assumptions, we further develop a statistical mechanical mapping that expresses the final logical error rate in terms of a classical partition function. We perform Monte Carlo simulations of this model for the three-dimensional toric code. The numerical results suggest that sublinear soundness can also support a single-shot state-preparation threshold.

</details>


### [148] [The quantum query complexity of the semigroup product problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39974)
*Troy Lee,Miklos Santha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究半群乘积x₁⋯xₙ的量子查询复杂度，引入"积宽度"β刻画代数结构，并给出交换非周期幺半群、带稳定偏序幺半群以及一般有限非周期半群的量子查询上界与下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作（Aaronson, Grier, Schaeffer）给出规模为N-1的有限非周期半群的上界√n·(N log(nN+2))^{O(N)}，但其对半群规模的依赖过重，未能反映代数结构。作者希望获得能体现代数结构的查询界，因此引入积宽度β这一代数参数进行研究。

Method: 定义积宽度β：使每个输入词都存在不超过β个字母的子序列具有相同乘积的最小界。对非平凡有限交换非周期幺半群，证明有界误差量子查询复杂度为Θ(min{n,√(nβ)})；利用非周期指数k给出β的界β=O(k log(|M|+1) log log(|M|+2))。对以单位元为最小元的稳定偏序幺半群，证明上界√(n+1)((β+2)log(n+2))^{O(log(β+2))}。对一般有限非周期半群，改进原上界为min{n, √n·log^{O((N log(N+2))^{1/3})}(n+2)}，并借助Ambainis等的有界深度Dyck下界给出近乎紧的下界√n·2^{Ω(N^{1/3})}。

Result: 交换非周期幺半群的量子查询复杂度被积宽度β完全刻画为Θ(min{n,√(nβ)})；β由非周期指数k控制，β=O(k log(|M|+1) log log(|M|+2))。稳定偏序幺半群上界为√(n+1)((β+2)log(n+2))^{O(log(β+2))}。一般有限非周期半群上界改进为min{n, √n·log^{O((N log(N+2))^{1/3})}(n+2)}，下界为√n·2^{Ω(N^{1/3})}，对半群规模的依赖近乎紧。

Conclusion: 积宽度β是刻画半群乘积量子查询复杂度的有效代数参数；所给上界显著改进了Aaronson等人的结果，且对半群规模的依赖近乎紧，显示出代数结构与量子查询复杂度之间的紧密联系。

Abstract: We study the quantum query complexity of computing a semigroup product $x_1\cdots x_n$, when one query reveals one input element and the multiplication table is given. For a finite aperiodic semigroup of size $N-1$, the argument of Aaronson, Grier, and Schaeffer gives an upper bound of $\sqrt n\,(N\log(nN+2))^{O(N)}$ queries.
  To obtain query bounds that reflect algebraic structure, we study the product breadth $β$: the smallest bound such that every input word has a subsequence of at most $β$ letters with the same product.
  For nontrivial finite commutative aperiodic monoids, the bounded-error quantum query complexity is $Θ(\min\{n,\sqrt{nβ}\})$, and is thus characterized by product breadth. We further show that if such a monoid $M$ has aperiodicity index $k$ (the least positive integer satisfying $x^k=x^{k+1}$ for every $x\in M$), then $β=O(k\log(|M|+1)\log\log(|M|+2))$.
  For monoids with a stable partial order in which the identity is the minimum element, we prove that the bounded-error quantum query complexity is at most $\sqrt{n+1}((β+2)\log(n+2))^{O(\log(β+2))}$.
  For arbitrary finite aperiodic semigroups of order $N-1$, we improve the bound of Aaronson, Grier, and Schaeffer, obtaining a bounded-error quantum query complexity of at most \[ \min\left\{n,\sqrt n\, \log^{O((N\log(N+2))^{1/3})}(n+2)\right\}. \] The dependence on semigroup size is nearly tight: the bounded-depth Dyck lower bound of Ambainis et al. yields aperiodic monoids requiring $\sqrt n\,2^{Ω(N^{1/3})}$ quantum queries in the relevant parameter range.

</details>


### [149] [Logical information localisation in stabiliser codes via single-qubit measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39980)
*Jelena Mackeprang,Hemant Sharma,Jonas Helsen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文对稳定子路径寻找（SPF）进行系统研究，提出g-SPF，证明在p<1/2的i.i.d.损失下大平面表面码可实现局部化阈值，并提出精确（整数线性规划）与启发式（译码问题）两种求解算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限和飞行量子比特场景中，需要快速读出逻辑信息以实现容错通信。SPF只需一轮单量子比特测量即可将逻辑信息局部化到预指定目标量子比特，但此前缺乏深入的理论与计算研究，且现有算法在规模较大时耗时过长。

Method: 提出g-SPF，将逻辑信息局部化到至多g个未指定目标量子比特；基于渗流理论与稳定子码的不相交性进行解析论证；并提出两种算法求解SPF，其一将SPF表述为整数线性规划（精确算法），其二将SPF表述为译码问题（启发式算法）。

Result: 证明对i.i.d.量子比特损失概率p<1/2且足够大的平面表面码，常数g下g-SPF局部化成功概率趋于1，确立了局部化阈值；数值上重现了表面码的局部化阈值，并表明启发式算法显著更快，可在远大于文献曾考虑规模的稳定子码中研究局部化。

Conclusion: 该工作大幅缩短了求解SPF的时间，结合理论与计算结果为容错量子隐形传态和高效逻辑融合等应用开辟了道路。

Abstract: Stabiliser path finding (SPF) has previously been introduced as a method to localise logical information in a stabiliser code undergoing loss onto a single pre-specified target qubit, using only one round of single-qubit measurements. When working with limited resources and flying qubits, this fast read-out of logical information is a helpful tool for fault-tolerant communication. In this work, we provide a broad analytical and computational study of localisation via SPF. We introduce $g$-SPF, where the task is to localise the logical information onto a set of at most $g$ unspecified target qubits. Through analytical arguments based on percolation theory and the disjointness of stabiliser codes, we prove that for i.i.d. qubit loss with probability $p<1/2$ and sufficiently large planar surface codes, localisation via $g$-SPF for constant $g$ succeeds with a probability converging to one, which establishes a localisation threshold. Furthermore, we propose and implement two algorithms to solve SPF. The first is exact and formulates SPF as an integer linear program, whereas the second is heuristic and formulates SPF as a decoding problem. We validate both algorithms by numerically reproducing the localisation threshold for the surface code and demonstrate that the heuristic algorithm is considerably faster. Our work significantly reduces the time required to solve SPF compared to current state-of-the-art algorithms, allowing us to study localisation in substantially larger stabiliser codes than previously considered in the literature. Together, these theoretical and computational results open the door to various applications, such as fault-tolerant teleportation and efficient logical fusion.

</details>


### [150] [Quantum state learning beyond approximate unitary designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39994)
*Gyungmin Cho,Changhun Oh,Dohun Kim*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究近似酉设计（approximate unitary designs）能否替代精确设计以保持量子态学习任务的性能。作者证明即使设计误差指数小，也不一定保留精确设计的学习保证；转而从测量电路结构直接推导学习保证。证明对数深度两层Clifford电路在经典阴影（classical shadows）可观测量估计中给出无偏估计量，且方差与全局Clifford一致，并推广到层析、混合态计量与稳定子结构学习；同时给出逆阴影信道的紧致精确张量网络表示，并指出测量基复用下高阶近似设计需近线性深度才能匹配Haar方差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近似酉设计可用对数深度电路实现，从而支持浅层测量协议以完成各类量子态学习任务并保持性能。这引出一个问题：近似设计能否在更一般情形下替代精确设计。论文指出该问题的关键：即便设计误差指数小，也未必保留精确设计的学习保证，因此需要直接从测量电路结构推导学习保证，而非依赖设计误差。

Method: 论文针对经典阴影下的可观测量估计，分析对数深度两层Clifford电路，构造无偏估计量并推导其方差界，证明其与全局Clifford的方差标度在所有态和Hermitian可观测量上一致。将该界推广到其它任务（设置高效层析、混合态计量、稳定子结构学习），并给出逆阴影信道的紧致精确张量网络表示。进一步分析测量基复用情形下近似设计阶数与Haar方差匹配对电路深度的要求。

Result: 证明指数小的设计误差不一定保持学习保证。对数深度两层Clifford电路在经典阴影可观测量估计中达到与全局Clifford相同的方差标度（对所有态与Hermitian可观测量），并保留全局Clifford在设置高效层析、混合态计量、稳定子结构学习等任务中的保证。给出逆阴影信道的紧致精确张量网络表示。当测量基复用时，任意高阶近似酉设计在多次采样极限下不一定一致匹配Haar方差；对全连接随机两局域电路，实现匹配需近线性深度。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了浅层量子态学习的能力边界，强调"近似Haar随机性"与"再现其学习保证"之间的本质区别：浅层电路结构本身可提供学习保证，而不能仅由近似设计误差推出。

Abstract: Approximate unitary designs reproduce the statistics of Haar-random unitaries to a given order and accuracy. Recent constructions realize such designs with logarithmic-depth circuits, enabling shallow measurement protocols for various quantum state-learning tasks while preserving performance. These results raise the question of whether approximate designs can replace exact designs more generally. We show that even exponentially small design error need not preserve the learning guarantees of exact designs. This motivates deriving learning guarantees directly from the measurement circuit structure. For observable estimation with classical shadows, we prove that logarithmic-depth two-layer Clifford circuits yield an unbiased estimator matching the global Clifford variance scaling for every state and Hermitian observable. Beyond observable estimation, this bound allows shallow measurements to retain global Clifford guarantees for other tasks, including setting-efficient tomography, mixed-state metrology, and stabilizer structure learning. We complement these statistical guarantees with a compact, exact tensor-network representation of the inverse shadow channel. When measurement bases are reused, approximate unitary designs of arbitrarily high order need not uniformly match the Haar variance in the many-shot limit. For the all-to-all random two-local circuits considered, doing so requires nearly linear depth. Together, our results reveal the capabilities and limitations of shallow quantum state learning, highlighting the distinction between approximating Haar randomness and reproducing its learning guarantees.

</details>


### [151] [An exponential query advantage from access to Stinespring dilation unitaries in quantum channel learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40001)
*Manaki Arihara,Satoshi Yoshida,Philip Taranto,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个量子信道学习任务，证明在两种访问模型之间查询复杂度存在关于量子比特数的指数分离：黑盒信道访问 vs. 可访问信道的Stinespring扩张酉算子及其逆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究量子信道的不同访问模型（黑盒 vs. 系统-环境联合演化及其逆）对学习信道内在性质的能力差异，以揭示联合演化访问是否带来指数级优势。

Method: 以学习未知信道与酉信道集合的接近程度为任务，该程度由最优平均门保真度（OAGF）刻画，即对所有酉信道取最大化的平均门保真度。常数Kraus秩且扩张环境维度为polylog(d)时，设计算法使用polylog(d)次对Stinespring扩张酉算子及其逆的查询，即可将OAGF估计到固定加性精度。

Result: 在Stinespring扩张酉算子及其逆访问下，算法以polylog(d)次查询达到固定加性精度估计OAGF；而在黑盒访问模型下，即使信道为常数Kraus秩和常数加性精度，估计也至少需要Ω(√d)次查询，形成指数分离。作为推论，排除了仅用poly(log d, q)次查询未知信道即可近似任意q次查询算法的通用模拟器（该算法可访问随机采样的Stinespring扩张酉算子及其逆），即使允许足够小的常数误差。

Conclusion: 访问系统-环境联合演化及其逆，能在估计量子信道内在性质时提供指数级优势，并否定了通用模拟器的存在性。

Abstract: We present a learning task for quantum channels that exhibits an exponential separation in query complexity (in terms of the number of qubits) between two access models: i) black-box channel access and ii) access to a Stinespring dilation unitary of the channel along with its inverse. We consider the task of learning how close the unknown channel is to the set of unitary channels. This is characterized by the \textit{optimal average gate fidelity (OAGF)}, defined as the average gate fidelity maximized over all unitary channels. For constant Kraus rank and a dilation environment of dimension $\operatorname{polylog}(d)$, we provide an algorithm that estimates the OAGF to fixed additive accuracy using $\operatorname{polylog}(d)$ queries to the Stinespring dilation unitary and its inverse. In contradistinction, under the black-box access model, this estimation requires at least $Ω(\sqrt{d})$ queries, even for channels of constant Kraus rank and constant additive accuracy. As a corollary, we rule out a universal simulator that uses only $\operatorname{poly}(\log d,q)$ queries to the unknown channel to approximate the output of arbitrary $q$-query algorithms with access to a randomly-sampled Stinespring dilation unitary and its inverse, even to sufficiently small constant error. These results demonstrate that access to the system-environment joint evolution and its inverse can provide an exponential advantage in estimating an intrinsic property of a quantum channel.

</details>


### [152] [Technical analysis of the Resource-efficient Quantum Walkers Quantum Random Access Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40020)
*Giuseppe De Riso,Giuseppe Catalano,Seth Lloyd,Vittorio Giovannetti,Dario De Santis*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文是量子随机存取存储器（qRAM）架构的技术性后续工作，给出基于离散时间量子行走器的完整数学推导、协议规范与资源分析，并提出长程与短程两种算法范式，其中短程方案达到最优 O(n+m) 电路深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流 qRAM 方案存在明显瓶颈：Bucket Brigade 架构需要指数级 O(2^n) 个活跃节点，而此前的量子行走器方案需要多棵并行树，造成空间瓶颈。因此需要一种资源高效、更贴近实际实现的 qRAM 架构。

Method: 采用基于离散时间量子行走器的资源高效 qRAM 架构，并引入新技巧提升可实现性；提出长程与短程两种算法范式，前者使用多目标受控门，后者将相互作用分解为 2 体和 3 体局域门序列；形式化描述路由、消息复制与行走器检索三个阶段；对比玻色子、双轨量子比特和四能级 qudit 等不同量子行走器。

Result: 量子行走器数量随地址尺寸 n 与消息尺寸 m 线性增长，避免了 O(2^n) 的活跃节点开销，并消除了多棵并行树的空间瓶颈；短程方案通过局域门分解提升了近期实验可行性，并确认达到最优 O(n+m) 电路深度。

Conclusion: 该工作系统性地补充并扩展了原 qRAM 提案，在资源效率与实验可实现性之间取得平衡，为 qRAM 的实际落地提供了兼顾理论完整性与工程可行性的技术路线。

Abstract: Quantum Random Access Memory (qRAM) is a critical component for achieving quantum advantage in algorithms ranging from database search to quantum machine learning. In a recently introduced model [arXiv:2508.02855], we proposed a resource-efficient qRAM architecture based on discrete-time quantum walkers. This article serves as a comprehensive technical follow-up, providing the full mathematical derivations, detailed protocol specifications, and in-depth resource analysis. Moreover, we extend the original proposal with novel techniques for the purpose of making the qRAM implementation more realistic. Our model resolves the primary drawbacks of leading qRAM proposals: it avoids the exponential number of active nodes $\mathcal{O}(2^n)$ required by the "Bucket Brigade" architecture by employing a number of quantum walkers that scales linearly with the address and message sizes, $n$ and $m$, respectively. Simultaneously, it overcomes the spatial bottlenecks of previous quantum-walker schemes by eliminating the need for multiple parallel trees. We propose two algorithmic paradigms: the long- and the short-range approaches, which differ by the length of interaction of the main routing gates employed in the qRAM. We formalize the routing, message-copy, and walker retrieval phases for both variants and we show how, while the long-range scheme employs controlled gates having a multitude of target systems, the short-range approach decomposes these interactions into sequences of 2- and 3-body local gates, which improves the architecture's feasibility for near-term experimental implementation. Finally, our comprehensive resource analysis confirms that the short-range approach achieves the optimal $\mathcal{O}(n+m)$ circuit depth. Within these two paradigms, we explore the potential of different types of quantum walkers, namely bosons, dual-rail qubits and four-level qudits.

</details>


### [153] [CSS codes for Quantum Metrology with Discrete-time Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40022)
*Ugnė Liaubaitė,Debora Ramacciotti,Robert Raußendorf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析基于CSS码的量子度量协议，证明对垂直于传感哈密顿量的噪声，离散时间纠错可在有限探测时间窗口内保持类海森堡时间标度，窗口长度依赖纠错频率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子度量旨在以超越经典精度估计未知参数，理想情况下达到海森堡标度。但现实噪声会显著削弱该优势，常恢复至标准量子极限（SQL）级别性能。因此需借助量子纠错等噪声鲁棒策略来保持量子增强。

Method: 构建基于Calderbank-Shor-Steane（CSS）码的量子度量协议，建立适用于任意CSS码的解析框架，并针对Steane码与Shor码具体求解离散时间纠错动力学、类海森堡到类SQL的交叉点及最优探测时间，同时进行数值分析。特别考察仅于传感演化后进行恢复的情形。

Result: 对垂直于传感哈密顿量的噪声，离散时间纠错在有限探测时间窗口内保持类海森堡时间标度，窗口时长取决于纠错频率；仅于传感演化后施加恢复时，相较于未纠错噪声协议无度量优势；对Steane和Shor码确定了交叉行为与最优探测时间。

Conclusion: 离散时间CSS纠错可在特定噪声方向下于有限窗口内维持量子度量优势，但纠错时机至关重要；仅在演化后恢复无效。该解析框架可推广至任意CSS码，为噪声鲁棒量子度量提供设计指导。

Abstract: The goal of quantum metrology is to estimate an unknown parameter with better-than-classical precision and, ideally, to attain Heisenberg scaling. In realistic settings, however, noise can substantially reduce this advantage and, in many cases, restore standard-quantum-limit-like performance. Preserving the quantum enhancement therefore requires noise-robust strategies, which can be implemented using quantum error correction. In this work, we develop and analyze a quantum-metrology protocol based on Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) codes. The main result is that, for noise perpendicular to the sensing Hamiltonian, discrete-time error correction preserves Heisenberg-like temporal scaling over a finite interrogation-time window whose duration depends on the correction frequency. It is further shown that recovery applied only after the sensing evolution provides no metrological advantage over the corresponding uncorrected noisy protocol in the setup considered here. An analytical framework is developed for arbitrary CSS codes and applied to the Steane and Shor codes, for which the discrete-time corrected dynamics, the Heisenberg-like-to-SQL-like crossover, and the optimal interrogation time are determined and numerically analysed.

</details>


### [154] [Measurement-induced dynamics and emergent symmetries of particles moving in a one-dimensional lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40026)
*Salvatore Di Lorenzo,Klaus Mølmer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究证明，对一维晶格中单个格点占据数的弱连续测量能够诱导纠缠，并自发地将无先验置换对称性的两粒子、三粒子隧穿系统引导到稳定的玻色子、费米子及准统计对称性区段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续量子测量不仅揭示而且会改变量子系统动力学。作者希望探究对一维晶格中粒子隧穿运动的弱连续探测（尤其是单格点占据数的探测）如何影响系统性质，特别是纠缠的产生以及波函数宇称、置换对称性等属性的确定。

Method: 采用一维晶格模型描述粒子在格点间的隧穿运动；对单个格点的占据数施加弱连续测量；通过数值模拟考察两粒子与三粒子系统在初始不具备置换对称性的条件下，测量过程如何驱动对称性选择。

Result: 模拟表明，弱连续测量可诱导纠缠，并促使系统属性获得确定取值（如波函数宇称与置换对称性）；两粒子和三粒子系统能够在测量引导下进入稳定的玻色子、费米子以及准统计（parastatistical）对称性区段。

Conclusion: 连续测量可作为调控量子多体对称性与纠缠的有效工具，能够从无对称性初始态出发主动“制备”特定置换对称性扇区，为量子态工程与对称性控制提供新途径。

Abstract: Continuous measurements simultaneously reveal and modify the dynamics of a quantum system. In this work we consider the tunneling motion of particles between the sites of a one-dimensional lattice. We investigate how weak continuous probing of the occupation of a single lattice site induces entanglement and selects definite values for system properties such as wave function parity and permutation symmetry. Our simulations show how continuous measurement can steer systems of two and three particles, with no prior permutation symmetry, into stable bosonic, fermionic and parastatistical symmetry sectors.

</details>


### [155] [Pauli instability in arbitrary states: detecting magic in physical correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40032)
*Tanner Jackson,Alexey Milekhin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出用非时序关联函数（OTOC）在任意量子态（含有限温度吉布斯态）中提取量子演化"魔力"（non-stabilizerness）的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子演化的魔力（non-stabilizerness/"magic"）衡量其在经典计算机上模拟的难度。如何从物理关联函数（如有限温度或基态下）中提取关于魔力的信息，是一个自然且重要的问题。

Method: 作者利用在任意态中求值的非时序关联函数（OTOC）来度量魔力。对每个满秩态（特别是每个吉布斯态），引入的度量量当且仅当演化为Clifford时为零；并将其与Choi态的稳定子熵（stabilizer entropy）建立联系。

Result: 该度量具有若干基本性质；与Choi态的稳定子熵相关；即使仅使用单一固定探针算符，也能给出在任何线路架构下实现该动力学所需T门数量的下界。

Conclusion: OTOC可作为从物理关联函数中提取量子演化魔力的有效工具，并提供对T门数量的下界估计。

Abstract: Measures of non-stabilizerness (``magic'') of a quantum evolution quantify how hard it is to simulate on a classical computer. A natural question is how to extract information about magic from physical correlators, for example at finite temperature or in the ground state. In this paper we show how to measure magic using out-of-time-order correlators evaluated in an arbitrary state. For every full-rank state, and in particular for every Gibbs state, the resulting quantity vanishes if and only if the evolution is Clifford. We establish its basic properties, relate it to the stabilizer entropy of the Choi state, and prove that, even with a single fixed probe operator, it gives a lower bound on the number of $T$ gates needed to implement the dynamics in any circuit architecture.

</details>


### [156] [Experimentally realized local-global tradeoff in quantum resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40033)
*Yue Dai,Zhiyu Wang,Xinzhi Zhao,Xinglei Yu,Chengjie Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并实验验证了量子资源中"局域—全局权衡"关系：局域最大相干性与量子互信息之和受一个确定上限约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同量子资源间的相互转化（如相干性与纠缠的权衡）已受广泛关注，但局域量子资源与全局量子资源之间的关系仍缺乏研究。

Method: 以局域最大相干性（local maximum coherence）量化局域资源，以量子互信息（quantum mutual information）量化全局资源，通过实验测量二者的联合取值以检验其和的界限。

Result: 实验表明局域最大相干性与量子互信息之和被一个确定的上限所界定，为局域与全局量子资源之间的互补性提供了直接实验证据。

Conclusion: 研究建立了一个基本权衡不等式，揭示量子网络中的重要约束：各节点可用的局域量子资源与网络的全局量子资源相互排斥，增强其一必然限制另一。

Abstract: The interconversion among different quantum resources has attracted considerable attention, exemplified by the tradeoff between coherence and entanglement. However, the relationship between local and global quantum resources remains largely unexplored. Here, we propose and experimentally demonstrate a local global tradeoff in quantum resources, quantified respectively by the local maximum coherence and the quantum mutual information. Our experiments show that the sum of the local maximum coherence and the quantum mutual information is bounded by a definitive upper limit. These results not only establish a fundamental tradeoff inequality, but also provide direct experimental evidence of the complementarity between local and global quantum resources. Moreover, our findings reveal an important constraint in quantum networks: the local quantum resources available at individual nodes and the global quantum resources of the network are mutually exclusive, implying that enhancing one necessarily limits the other.

</details>


### [157] [Cycle Codes and Decoded Quantum Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40036)
*Anuj Apte,Shouvanik Chakrabarti,Andi Gu,Stephen P. Jordan,Ojas Parekh,Ruslan Shaydulin,Jacob Watkins,Noureldin Yosri,Adam Zalcman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究解码量子干涉（DQI）在不可完美解码情形下的真实性能，将二变量约束优化问题归约为环码解码问题。作者推导了固定实例与随机实例下的满意度保证，证明任意q>2有限域上的最小权重解码是NP难的，并在Linial–Simkin正则图上给出最大似然恢复界与强逆定理，以及LP解码器可多项式时间恢复正比例误差。这些界排除了所分析范围内的量子优势，同时识别出一类DQI可高效取得非平凡满意度保证的Max-k-Cut实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作表明DQI仅在MaxCut经典易解的线性围长图上取得非平凡满意度保证，而这些no-go结论依赖的最小距离假设并不代表常见噪声信道下的真实可解码阈值，因而低估了DQI的实际性能。作者希望刻画考虑不完美解码时的真实性能，并厘清经典解码器限制下DQI能否带来量子优势。

Method: 1）针对固定实例，在解码不完美条件下推导DQI满意度保证，并将既有随机实例结果推广；2）研究任意有限域上的同调环码，分析其最小权重解码的计算复杂性；3）在Linial–Simkin对数围长正则图集合上，对一族非均匀加性噪声信道证明最大似然恢复界与强逆定理；4）利用LP解码器实现多项式时间恢复；5）基于上述结果推导DQI在经典解码器限制下近似最优值的上下界。

Result: 对任意q>2的有限域，环码的最小权重解码是NP难的，与二元环码的已知结果形成对比；在Linial–Simkin正则图上得到最大似然恢复界及非均匀加性噪声信道族的强逆定理；LP解码器可多项式时间恢复正比例、取值任意的随机位置错误；由此获得DQI所达近似最优值的上下界，这些界在所分析范围内排除了量子优势，同时识别出一族正则Max-k-Cut实例，使DQI能高效取得非平凡满意度保证。

Conclusion: DQI的性能评估应基于真实可解码阈值而非最小距离假设。本文通过不可完美解码下的满意度保证、环码解码复杂性、最大似然恢复界与LP解码恢复结果，给出了DQI在经典解码器限制下近似最优值的上下界：在其分析范围内排除量子优势，并指出DQI在特定正则Max-k-Cut实例上仍能高效获得非平凡满意度保证。

Abstract: Decoded Quantum Interferometry (DQI) reduces optimization problems with two-variable constraints to decoding cycle codes. For one such problem, namely MaxCut, prior work showed that DQI achieves a nontrivial satisfaction fraction guarantee only on linear-girth graphs, for which MaxCut is classically easy. However, these no-go results rely on minimum distance assumptions that do not represent true decodability thresholds for common noise channels, thus underestimating actual DQI performance. To estimate the true performance, we derive DQI satisfaction guarantees in the presence of imperfect decoding for fixed instances and generalize prior results for random instances. We then study homological cycle codes over arbitrary finite fields, and show that minimum-weight decoding is NP-hard for every field of size $q>2$, in contrast with known results for binary cycle codes. On the Linial--Simkin ensemble of regular graphs with logarithmic girth, we prove maximum-likelihood recovery bounds and a strong converse for a family of non-uniform additive noise channels. Moreover, LP decoders provide polynomial-time recovery of a positive fraction of randomly located errors with arbitrary values. These results allow us to prove upper and lower bounds on the approximate optima achievable by DQI when restricted to classical decoders. These bounds rule out quantum advantage in the regimes we analyze but also identify a family of regular Max-$k$-Cut instances on which DQI efficiently achieves a nontrivial satisfaction fraction guarantee.

</details>


### [158] [Finite-Bandwidth Protection of a Three-Level Quantum Heat Engine Against Parasitic Heat Leaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40038)
*Gilberto Aparecido Prataviera,Marcos César de Oliveira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究有限带宽频谱滤波（储能工程）在量子热机中对寄生跃迁的抑制与由此引入的有限响应时间之间的竞争关系，以连续三能级热机为模型，结合精确速率网络解与含阻尼辅助模的物理滤波器分析，给出正功率输出的阈值判据并揭示有限最大功率带宽的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限带宽的储能工程可抑制量子热机中不需要的跃迁，但物理滤波器本身会引入有限的响应时间；作者希望厘清这种"频谱选择性与动态响应代价"之间的竞争，明确滤波器选择性对热机有用循环的保护作用及其代价。

Method: 构建热环境寄生耦合到冷跃迁的连续三能级热机模型：1）对相应的三态速率网络进行精确求解，分析寄生跃迁导致的热-冷热力学短路并推导正功率运行丧失的闭式阈值；2）显式保留一个阻尼辅助模作为物理频谱滤波器，利用其洛伦兹响应抑制失谐的寄生跃迁；3）分别采用局域GKSL方程与非世俗Bloch–Redfield方程进行独立计算；4）采用频率分辨的洛伦兹速率约化方法进行对比分析。

Result: 精确速率网络解表明寄生跃迁形成热到冷的热力学短路，并给出正功率运行丧失的闭式阈值。物理滤波器的洛伦兹响应能抑制失谐的寄生跃迁，但过度窄化带宽会限制有用能量吞吐。局域GKSL与非世俗Bloch–Redfield两种独立计算在弱耦合极限下都回到无泄漏马尔可夫热机，并预测存在有限的最大功率带宽，但其具体位置依赖于模型；而频率分辨的洛伦兹速率约化方法则不存在内部最优值。

Conclusion: 频谱选择性可以保护有用的热力学循环，但真实的物理滤波器无法在不付出动态吞吐代价的情况下被任意窄化，这是有限带宽储能工程设计中必须权衡的设计原则。

Abstract: Finite-bandwidth reservoir engineering can suppress unwanted transitions in a quantum thermal machine, but a physical filter also introduces a finite response time. We study this competition in a continuous three-level heat engine whose hot environment couples parasitically to the cold transition. The corresponding three-state rate network is solved exactly, showing that the parasitic transition produces a hot-to-cold thermodynamic short circuit and yielding a closed threshold for the loss of positive-power operation. We then retain a damped auxiliary mode explicitly as a physical spectral filter. Its Lorentzian response suppresses the detuned parasitic transition, whereas excessive narrowing limits the useful energy throughput. Independent local-GKSL and nonsecular Bloch--Redfield calculations both recover the no-leak Markovian engine in the weak-coupling limit and predict a finite maximum-power bandwidth, although its precise location is model dependent. A frequency-resolved Lorentzian-rate reduction, by contrast, has no interior optimum. The resulting design principle is that spectral selectivity can protect the useful thermodynamic cycle, but a real filter cannot be narrowed without a dynamical throughput cost.

</details>


### [159] [Unconditional quantum advantage with noisy planar architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40050)
*Libor Caha,Robert Koenig,Louis Paletta*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 受限在二维局部操作、并受低于恒定阈值局部随机噪声影响的量子线路，其计算能力严格强于AC0线路；本文给出首个适用于平面量子器件、兼具噪声鲁棒性且无条件的复杂度分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将无条件量子优势的实验演示推向更现实的实验条件：现有分离结果多依赖复杂度假设、或未考虑几何局域性与噪声鲁棒性，本文旨在同时满足平面二维局域性、噪声容忍与无条件性。

Method: 构造一个计算问题，证明其可由某个几何二维局域量子线路以高概率正确求解，即使该线路实现不完美（存在低于恒定阈值的局部随机噪声）；同时证明任意多项式规模AC0线路在该问题的某些实例上以常数概率失败。

Result: 给出该问题的双重性质：(i) 几何二维局域量子线路在高概率下正确求解，且具备噪声鲁棒性；(ii) 任意多项式规模AC0线路在特定实例上以常数概率失败。从而得到二维局域量子线路相对AC0的复杂度分离。

Conclusion: 这是首个适用于平面量子器件、纳入噪声鲁棒性且无条件的复杂度理论分离（不依赖复杂度假设），使无条件量子优势的实验演示更接近实验现实。

Abstract: We consider quantum devices restricted to local operations in 2D and subject to local stochastic noise below a constant threshold. We show that such circuits are computationally more powerful than AC0-circuits, i.e., noise-free, geometrically-unconstrained constant-depth classical circuits with unbounded fan-in AND, OR and NOT gates. To this end, we exhibit a computational problem with the following properties: (i) Any instance of the problem is correctly solved with high probability by a certain geometrically 2D-local quantum circuit even if the latter is imperfectly implemented, but (ii) any polynomial-size AC0-circuit fails to solve certain instances of the problem with constant probability. To our knowledge, this isthe first complexity-theoretic separation which applies to planar quantum devices, incorporates noise-resilience and is unconditional, i.e., does not rely on complexity-theoretic assumptions. This brings the experimental demonstration of an unconditional quantum advantage closer to experimental realities.

</details>


### [160] [Equality in the Bosonic Quantum Entropy Power Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40061)
*Yonglong Li*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文确定了玻色量子熵功率不等式（QEPI）在有限平均光子数独立输入下的完整等号成立类：对任意模式和任意严格介于0与1之间的透射率，线性或指数形式的等号成立当且仅当输入为具有相同协方差矩阵的高斯态（位移任意）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子熵功率不等式刻画了分束器输出熵与输入熵的关系，但其等号成立条件（即何时最优）此前未被完整刻画。确定等号类有助于理解该不等式的紧致性及其物理含义。

Method: 主要步骤为：证明单输出的等号成立迫使两个输出独立；利用热辅助将等号转化为固定噪声信道下互信息的保持，并用双结果仪器（two-outcome instrument）证明互信息保持要求输入与参考态构成乘积态；随后运用量子Darmois-Skitovich定理推出高斯性，再通过中心极限论证将指数等号归约为线性等号。

Result: 对于任意模式数和任意0<透射率<1，线性或指数形式的玻色QEPI等号成立当且仅当输入为具有相同协方差矩阵的高斯态，位移可以任意。

Conclusion: 该工作完整刻画了玻色量子熵功率不等式的等号成立类，表明高斯性是等号成立的充要条件，深化了对QEPI紧致性的理解。

Abstract: The bosonic quantum entropy power inequality bounds the entropy of a beam-splitter output in terms of the input entropies. We determine its complete equality class among independent inputs of finite mean photon number: for any number of modes and any transmissivity strictly between zero and one, equality in either the linear or the exponential form holds if and only if the inputs are Gaussian with the same covariance matrix, allowing arbitrary displacements. The main step shows that equality for one output forces the two outputs to be independent: a thermal auxiliary converts equality into preservation of mutual information by a fixed noisy channel, and a two-outcome instrument shows that preservation of mutual information requires a product state with the reference. The quantum Darmois--Skitovich theorem then gives Gaussianity, while a central-limit argument reduces exponential equality to linear equality.

</details>


### [161] [A Fourier-Label Information-Loss Barrier for Dihedral Coset Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40062)
*Aparna Gupte,Seyoon Ragavan,Mark Zhandry*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 对二面体陪集问题(DCP)的Fourier采样-子集和测量类量子算法证明了一个no-go定理：若算法在测量子集和后丢弃任意ω(log n)比特的Fourier标签，则无法求解DCP；并据此证明Simon近期算法不能求解DCP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCP是量子算法研究的重要问题，Regev提出的Fourier采样与子集和测量模板是求解DCP的主要途径之一。作者希望厘清该模板成功求解DCP的必要条件，即算法必须充分使用Fourier标签信息，从而为后续算法设计提供指导，并检验近期声称求解DCP的算法的正确性。

Method: 针对Regev模板，考虑算法在测量子集和的下n−1位后，从每个Fourier标签中丢弃任意ω(log n)比特的情形，证明此类算法无法成功求解DCP。作为应用，分析Simon的新算法，证明其在子集和测量后可仅用Fourier标签的最高有效三分之一实现（误差为指数小），因而落入所证no-go定理的适用范围。并提供Lean 4代码以支持可验证性。

Result: 证明了广义no-go定理：丢弃ω(log n)比特Fourier标签的Fourier采样-子集和测量算法不能求解DCP；得出Simon的算法不能求解DCP，因其仅依赖最高有效三分之一的Fourier标签而受该定理约束；同时发布了Lean 4形式化代码。

Conclusion: 任何遵循Regev模板的DCP算法必须大量使用Fourier标签信息，否则无法成功；该结论为DCP算法设计提供指引，并否定了Simon算法的有效性。

Abstract: We establish a no-go theorem for a broad class of quantum algorithms for the dihedral coset problem (DCP). We consider the Fourier-sampling and subset-sum-measurement template proposed by Regev (SIAM Journal on Computing, 2004), which is one of the main approaches to solving DCP. Suppose that, after measuring the lower $n-1$ bits of the subset sum, the algorithm discards any $ω(\log n)$ bits from each of the Fourier labels. Then we prove that the algorithm cannot succeed in solving DCP. This shows that any algorithm following this template must make extensive use of the Fourier labels, and thus serves as a useful guide for developing algorithms for DCP.
  As a main application, we show that the recent algorithm by Simon (IACR ePrint:2026/1591, August 11 2026) does not solve DCP. We show that after the subset-sum measurement, this algorithm can be implemented (up to exponentially-small error) using only the most-significant third of the Fourier labels, and is therefore subject to our general no-go theorem. To help with verifiability, we release Lean 4 code for our results.

</details>


### [162] [Transducer-based linear combination of unitaries: theory and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40073)
*Dong An,Dekuan Dong,Changpeng Shao,Yuxin Zhang,Chenhao Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出基于transducer的LCU框架，将LCU代价从最坏情况依赖降低为加权平均查询复杂度，并应用于稀疏矩阵块编码、哈密顿量模拟和线性系统求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准LCU的查询复杂度由组合中最昂贵的酉算子的代价C_max主导，即使该算子的权重很小。这在高代价算子权重较低时造成不必要的开销，因此需要一种能利用权重分布、降低最坏情况依赖的方法。

Method: 基于Belovs、Jeffery和Yolcu提出的transducer框架，发展面向块编码的算子层理论。对于A=Σc_j U_j，在每种U_j可用C_j次原语查询实现的条件下，将transducer作用转换为A/α的ε-近似块编码；引入resolvent范数K作为transducer实现的关键复杂度度量，并证明仅需O(K log(1/ε))次查询即可实现，将精度依赖从多项式改进为对数。

Result: 给定上界a≥‖A‖，算法以α=O(a)的缩放因子实现A/α的块编码，使用Õ(C_max + C̄·λ/a)次原语查询，其中λ=Σc_j，C̄=Σc_jC_j/λ；而标准LCU需Õ(C_max·λ/a)次。当高代价算子权重小且λ/a较大时改进显著。应用于稀疏矩阵块编码，得到稀疏哈密顿量模拟和量子线性系统的量子算法，其查询复杂度在所有相关参数的polylog因子内接近最优。

Conclusion: 该工作将LCU的复杂度从最坏情况查询数降为加权平均查询数，并将transducer框架拓展到算子层块编码理论，识别resolvent范数为核心复杂度度量，精度依赖降至对数级，为稀疏矩阵相关量子算法提供了近优查询复杂度。

Abstract: Linear combination of unitaries (LCU) is a fundamental primitive in quantum algorithms, whose cost is typically governed by the most expensive unitary appearing in the combination. We develop a transducer-based LCU framework that reduces this worst-case dependence to a weighted average query complexity, when the constituent unitaries share access to a common set of primitive oracles.
  Consider $A=\sum_j c_j U_j$, where $c_j>0$ and each unitary $U_j$ can be implemented using $C_j$ primitive queries. Given an upper bound $a\geq \|A\|$, our algorithm implements a block-encoding of $A/α$ with rescaling factor $α=\mathcal{O}(a)$, using $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}} (C_{\max}+\overline{C} λ/{a} )$ primitive queries, where $λ=\sum_j c_j$, $C_{\max}=\max_j C_j$, and $\overline{C}= {\sum_j c_j C_j}/λ$. By comparison, the standard LCU construction requires $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}} (C_{\max} λ/{a} )$ primitive queries. The improvement can therefore be substantial when costly unitaries have small weights and $λ/a$ is large. As applications, we obtain improved block-encodings of sparse matrices, leading to quantum algorithms for sparse Hamiltonian simulation and quantum linear systems with near-optimal query complexity up to polylogarithmic factors in all relevant parameters.
  Our main technique is the transducer framework developed by Belovs, Jeffery, and Yolcu [Quantum, 8:1444 (2024)]. Here we develop a complementary operator-level theory tailored to block-encodings. We identify the resolvent norm $K$ as a key complexity measure for transducer implementation besides the existing catalyst complexity. We show that the transducer action can be converted into an $ε$-approximate block-encoding using only $\mathcal{O}\left(K \log(1/ε)\right)$ queries to the transducer, improving the precision dependence from polynomial to logarithmic.

</details>


### [163] [Oscillator-Qubit Primitives for a Molecular Quantum Dynamics Simulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40078)
*Jungsoo Hong,Joonsuk Huh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于广义Jacobi-Anger展开与量子信号处理的近最优量子原语，用于分子量子动力学的多模玻色相位门合成，电路长度较直接QSP方法缩短约三分之一，并给出电路QED噪声下的寿命阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典计算中电子与原子核的全波函数表示资源随粒子数指数增长，而振子-量子比特处理器（囚禁离子、电路QED）可分别用量子比特与振子表示电子态和核振动。但模拟需按序施加大量相位门，累积成本与误差限制了动力学可跟踪时长，因此需要更高效的量子原语。

Method: 在QSP框架内使用广义Jacobi-Anger（GJA）展开合成非线性及多模玻色相位门，其代价在相互作用强度与精度上均近最优增长；相互作用模型与门合成各设独立精度参数，二者联合选取以优化电路。

Result: 多模基准电路较此前直接QSP方法缩短约三分之一；模拟简化二氧化碳模型中伸缩与弯曲振动间的能量交换，在电路QED噪声、量子比特与振子寿命约为2 ms和5 ms时，短时模拟略未达到精度标准。

Conclusion: 该工作为振子-量子比特处理器上的分子量子动力学模拟建立了近最优原语与具体寿命检查点，2 ms/5 ms寿命是迈向非谐多模分子量子动力学模拟的关键工程目标。

Abstract: Molecular quantum dynamics simulations that treat both electrons and nuclei quantum mechanically are crucial for predicting chemical reactions. With classical computation, a full wave-function representation of both types of particles requires resources that grow exponentially with their number. Oscillator-qubit processors, including trapped-ion and circuit QED devices, represent electronic states with qubits and nuclear vibrations with oscillators. Their native operations produce linear vibronic interactions, from which programmable anharmonic interactions can be built. Product-formula approaches build the corresponding phase gates by repeating small noncommuting phase steps, and quantum signal processing (QSP) approaches approximate each gate directly with a Fourier series. Simulations apply many such gates in sequence, so accumulated cost and error limit how long the dynamics can be followed. We propose near-optimal quantum primitives for molecular quantum dynamics simulation, covering the representations of both types of particles and their interactions. We synthesize nonlinear and multimode bosonic phase gates using generalized Jacobi-Anger (GJA) expansions within QSP, with a cost that grows near-optimally in both interaction strength and precision. Our interaction model and gate synthesis each have their own accuracy setting. Chosen together, these settings make our multimode benchmark circuits about one-third shorter than those of the previous direct QSP approach. We then simulate energy exchange between the stretching and bending vibrations of a simplified carbon dioxide model. With circuit-QED noise, a short simulation narrowly misses our accuracy criteria at qubit and oscillator lifetimes near 2 ms and 5 ms. These lifetimes mark a concrete checkpoint on the way to simulating anharmonic multimode molecular quantum dynamics on near-term oscillator-qubit processors.

</details>


### [164] [Universal Entanglement Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40080)
*Salvatore Tirone,Francesco Anna Mele,Vittorio Giovannetti,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用纠缠蒸馏协议，可在未知量子态下渐近达到其可蒸馏纠缠率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纠缠蒸馏通常假设量子态已知，而实际场景中状态往往未知，因此需要研究在状态未知情况下是否会造成渐近速率损失。

Method: 结合基于局域操作与经典通信（LOCC）的态层析与自适应蒸馏流程，并利用局部鲁棒性引理控制有限样本估计误差。

Result: 协议对任意未知有限维双体态均可渐近达到最优可蒸馏纠缠率，误差以迹距离度量且趋于零；并刻画了一族态的最优一致蒸馏率为其闭包上可蒸馏纠缠的最小值。

Conclusion: 对量子态的未知性并不必然导致渐近速率惩罚，通用协议可实现与已知态情形相同的渐近效率。

Abstract: We present a universal entanglement distillation protocol that asymptotically achieves the distillable entanglement of any unknown bipartite state in a fixed finite dimension. That is, its ebit yield attains every rate below the optimum that would be available if the state were known, with vanishing trace-distance error. Our approach combines tomography by local operations and classical communication with an adaptive distillation routine, using a local robustness lemma to control finite-sample estimation errors. We also characterize worst-case distillation from a family of states: the optimal uniform rate is the minimum distillable entanglement on the family's closure. These results show that ignorance of the state need not incur an asymptotic rate penalty.

</details>


### [165] [Super-Quadratic Quantum Speedups for Combinatorial Optimization via Tilted Walks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40088)
*Guneykan Ozgul,Shouvanik Chakrabarti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出"量子倾斜游走"（quantum tilted walks）框架，用于求解精确组合优化问题，可对经典 conditioning-and-search 算法实现超二次加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有量子短路径算法（quantum short-path algorithms）相比穷举搜索有超二次加速，但 Le Gall 与 Tamaki 新提出的经典 conditioning-and-search 算法在许多问题上仅比短路径算法慢亚二次。因此，短路径算法无法相对于这一更强的经典基线给出超二次加速，需要新的量子框架。

Method: 框架对倾斜哈密顿量（tilted Hamiltonian）的幂取平均，该哈密顿量用目标函数对基马尔可夫链（mixer）的判别矩阵进行偏置。针对最大化问题，给出条件，使其从低目标值的初始态出发时，能以高振幅放大到高目标值的目标态。该框架涵盖了 conditioning-and-search，并将其作为特例，同时把量子短路径算法作为另一特例恢复；但不需要基态制备，也不需要在基 mixer 基态中初始化。

Result: 在加权 MAX-Ek-LIN2 与加权 MAX-k-CSP 等问题上，该框架在相同假设下相对于 conditioning-and-search 取得超二次加速。此外，在一个合成优化问题上，倾斜游走实现了超二次加速，而短路径算法不能。

Conclusion: 倾斜游走是一个更一般且更强的量子算法框架，统一并推广了短路径算法与 conditioning-and-search，放宽了初始化与基态制备要求，并在若干问题上建立了相对更强经典基线的超二次加速。

Abstract: We introduce quantum tilted walks, a quantum algorithmic framework for solving exact combinatorial optimization problems. The framework applies an average of powers of a tilted Hamiltonian that biases the discriminant matrix of a base Markov chain (mixer) with the objective function. Our starting point is quantum short-path algorithms, which prepare the ground state of such a Hamiltonian and obtain super-quadratic speedups over exhaustive search for certain combinatorial optimization problems.
