We show that in sufficiently high dimension and for a broad family of symmetries, which includes the global on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(d)$ symmetries, there exist finite unitary ensembles of arbitrarily high design strength whose symmetric design strength is strictly higher. Ensembles with this property may be more versatile under symmetry constraints for protocols that rely on unitary randomization than they are without symmetry. Our results extend the previous work [Mitsuhashi and Yoshioka, PRX Quantum 4.4 (Nov. 2023)] showing that the symmetric design strength of the Clifford group is strictly upper bounded by its unitary design strength for symmetries that do not trivialize the symmetry-compatible unitaries. Due to the special significance of unitary designs with a group structure, we also derive a variety of bounds on the unitary and symmetric design strengths of finite unitary ensembles whose operators form a group. In particular, we provide a simple proof that an arbitrary ensemble of operators forming a finite group must have a symmetric design strength of at most two under the global on-site $\mathrm{U}(1)$ and $\mathrm{SU}(d)$ symmetries. Along the way, we show that there exist uniformly weighted groups of arbitrarily high symmetric design strength in arbitrary dimension and analyze their structure.

</details>


### [200] [Perfect non-local quantum computation is impossible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40228)
*Marten Folkertsma,Dmitry Grinko,Gina Muuss,Florian Speelman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：即使对于两量子比特的情形，Haar-几乎所有的酉算子都不存在有限维资源的精确非局域量子计算协议；虽然每个二分酉算子都有有限纠缠的近似协议，但精确协议仅对特定结构族存在。证明使用半代数几何限制精确协议至可数多个局部酉轨道，并给出受控相位门在超越相位时无精确协议的显式例子，结果同样适用于可局部化量子测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非局域量子计算（NLQC）在量子位置验证攻击、通信复杂度和量子引力等领域有广泛应用。已知每个二分酉算子都存在有限纠缠的近似NLQC协议，若干结构族也有精确协议，但"是否每个固定酉算子都存在有限维资源的精确协议"这一基本问题长期未解决。本文旨在回答这一开放问题。

Method: 证明分两步：首先，证明精确协议的平滑形变只能通过局部酉变换改变其目标；其次，对固定架构，利用半代数几何将精确协议限制为有限多个连通族，因此每个架构只能达到有限多个局部酉轨道。对所有架构取并集，可精确实现的酉算子构成可数多个局部酉轨道。此外，通过受控相位门 diag(1,1,1,e^{iθ}) 在相位超越时无精确协议给出具体反例，并将结果推广至可局部化量子测量。

Result: 对于两个量子比特，Haar-几乎每一个酉算子都不存在有限维资源与定制资源态、局域操作的精确协议。可精确实现的酉算子仅构成可数多个局部酉轨道，是测度零的集合。受控相位门 diag(1,1,1,e^{iθ}) 在 e^{iθ} 为超越数（如 θ=1）时无有限维精确协议。Haar-随机基下的秩一投影测量几乎必然不能在有限维资源态与有限局部结果下实现可局部化。

Conclusion: 精确NLQC协议对资源的要求远比近似情形严苛：即便允许针对目标定制资源态与局域操作，几乎所有酉算子仍无法精确实现，只有可数多个局部酉轨道可精确实现。这表明精确协议是极其稀有的特例，并揭示了近似协议普遍存在性与精确协议稀有性之间的根本差距。

Abstract: Non-local quantum computation (NLQC) asks two parties to apply a joint operation to their quantum inputs using an entangled resource state and one round of simultaneous quantum communication. NLQC has applications across quantum information, including attacks on quantum position verification, communication complexity, and quantum gravity.
  Every bipartite unitary has an approximate NLQC protocol with finite entanglement, and exact protocols are known for several structured families. However, it has remained open whether every fixed unitary admits an exact protocol with finite-dimensional resources. We show that, even for two qubits, Haar-almost every unitary does not admit such a protocol, even when the resource state and local operations are tailored to the target.
  The proof first shows that smooth deformations of an exact protocol can only change its target by local unitaries. For a fixed architecture, semialgebraic geometry limits the exact protocols to finitely many connected families, so each architecture reaches only finitely many local-unitary orbits. Taking the union over all architectures, the exactly implementable unitaries form countably many local-unitary orbits. We also show that some natural targets do not admit a protocol: the controlled phase $\mathrm{diag}(1,1,1,e^{iθ})$ has no exact protocol with finite-dimensional resources whenever $e^{iθ}$ is transcendental, for instance for $θ= 1$.
  Our results also apply to localizable quantum measurements, whose outcome is obtained from local measurements on inputs and a shared entangled resource state without communication. A rank-one projective measurement in a Haar-random basis is almost surely not localizable with a finite-dimensional resource state and finitely many local outcomes.

</details>


### [201] [A Quantum Algorithm for $st$-Transport on Flat Connection Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40231)
*Stacey Jeffery,Tobias J. Osborne,Galina Pass*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将无向 st-连通性推广到边带量子操作（酉矩阵标签）的图，定义 st-transport 问题：判定 s、t 是否连通并估计量子态传输后与目标态的平方重叠。作者提出运行时间 O~(n/ε)、空间 O(log n+log k+log(1/ε)) 的有界错误量子算法，并证明即使承诺连通的 Ω(n) 量子查询下界，说明算法在常数 ε 下近似最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典无向 st-连通性是基础问题，但尚未在边携带量子操作（酉标签）且满足平坦联络（pure gauge）的图上被研究。此类图对应谱图论中的一致联络图与群同步的无噪声实例，具有独立意义，因此需要把 st-连通性推广到量子态传输并研究其量子算法复杂度。

Method: 将 st-transport 问题形式化：边标酉 U_uv，满足平坦联络，保证任意 s→t 路径给出唯一酉 U_s(t)。在可相干访问邻居及对应边酉的 oracle 下，判定连通性并估计重叠 |⟨ψ_t|U_s(t)|ψ_s⟩|^2 到加性误差 ε。算法通过设计 transducer 并对输入图施加 Metropolis-Hastings 重加权来实现高效采样与传输。

Result: 给出有界错误量子算法，时间复杂度 O~(n/ε)，空间复杂度 O(log n+log k+log(1/ε))。同时证明 Ω(n) 的量子查询下界，且该下界在 s 与 t 被承诺连通时仍成立，因此对常数 ε 算法在 polylog 因子内最优。

Conclusion: st-transport 为无向 st-连通性提供了自然的量子推广，且其量子复杂度可被近乎紧确地刻画：上界 O~(n/ε) 与下界 Ω(n) 在常数误差下匹配（差 polylog 因子）。技术上的 transducer 与 Metropolis-Hastings 重加权方法可能对其他带量子标签图的遍历/传输问题有借鉴价值。

Abstract: We study a generalization of undirected $st$-connectivity to graphs whose edges carry quantum operations. Let $G=(V,E)$ be an undirected graph on $n$ vertices in which each edge $\{u,v\}$ is labeled by a unitary $U_{uv}\in\mathbb{C}^{k\times k}$, with $U_{vu}=U_{uv}^\dagger$. We assume the labels form a \emph{flat} connection: the ordered product of labels along any path between a pair of vertices $u$ and $v$ is independent of the path. Equivalently, the connection is pure gauge, i.e., gauge-equivalent to the trivial connection; such graphs are exactly the consistent connection graphs of spectral graph theory and the noiseless instances of group synchronization. Consequently, whenever $s$ and $t$ are connected, transporting a state from $s$ to $t$ defines a unique unitary $U_s(t)$. Given states $|ψ_s\rangle,|ψ_t\rangle\in\mathbb{C}^k$ and an oracle that returns the neighbours of a vertex while coherently applying the corresponding edge unitaries, the \emph{$st$-transport problem} is to decide whether $s$ and $t$ are connected and, if so, to estimate the squared overlap between $U_s(t)|ψ_s\rangle$ and $|ψ_t\rangle$ to additive error $\varepsilon$. When $k=1$ and all labels are trivial, this is exactly undirected $st$-connectivity. We give a bounded-error quantum algorithm for $st$-transport that runs in time $\widetilde{O}(n/\varepsilon)$ and uses $O(\log n+\log k+\log(1/\varepsilon))$ space. We do this by designing a transducer and applying a Metropolis-Hastings reweighting to the input graph. We also prove an $Ω(n)$ quantum query lower bound that holds even when $s$ and $t$ are promised to be connected, so for constant $\varepsilon$ our algorithm is optimal up to polylogarithmic factors.

</details>


### [202] [Optimal Purity Estimation with Incoherent Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40248)
*Junseo Lee,Chirag Wadhwa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究在非相干测量下估计未知量子态纯度的副本复杂度，给出了新的非自适应算法、自适应估计器，并刻画了该问题的整体最优复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计未知量子态纯度是量子信息中的基本任务，乘性误差ε下的最优副本数在允许一般集体测量时已知为Θ(√d/ε²+d/ε)，但集体测量在实验中难以实现。因此需要刻画非相干测量下的副本复杂度。此前该设置下唯一非平凡结果是使用O(d/ε²+d²/ε)副本的非自适应算法，明显大于已知下界，存在多项式级差距。

Method: 作者提出：(1) 一种新的非自适应非相干测量算法；(2) 一种新的自适应估计器，用于高精度区域ε=o(1/d)；(3) 通过结合非自适应与自适应估计器，建立匹配的上下界来刻画问题复杂度。

Result: 新的非自适应算法使用O(d/ε²+d^{3/2}/ε)副本，改进了原算法第二项；并证明该副本数对任意非自适应算法是最优的。自适应估计器在高精度区域ε=o(1/d)进一步改进复杂度。两者结合给出非相干纯度估计的最优复杂度：Θ(min{d/ε²+d^{3/2}/ε, d²/ε+√d/ε²})。

Conclusion: 工作显著缩小了非相干纯度估计的上下界差距，证明了非自适应算法的最优性，并通过自适应方法在高精度区域达到更优复杂度，最终给出了该问题完整的副本复杂度刻画。

Abstract: In this work, we consider the fundamental task of estimating the purity of an unknown state to within $\textit{multiplicative}$ error $\varepsilon$. When one can perform general collective measurements, $Θ\left(\frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d}{\varepsilon}\right)$ copies are known to be necessary and sufficient for this problem [AISW20]. However, implementing collective measurements on such a large number of copies can be experimentally demanding, and we thus aim to characterize the copy complexity of this problem with incoherent measurements.
  In this setting, the only non-trivial result is a non-adaptive algorithm that uses $O\left(
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon}
  \right)$ copies [PTTW26], which is polynomially larger than the best-known lower bound. Our first result is a new algorithm performing non-adaptive incoherent measurements that succeeds using $O\left(
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}
  \right)$ copies, improving on the latter term in the copy complexity. Moreover, we show that for any algorithm restricted to non-adaptive measurements, the above copy complexity is optimal.
  We also develop a new adaptive estimator for the purity of a state that improves on the above complexity in the high-precision regime, i.e., for $\varepsilon = o(1/d)$. We also show that the non-adaptive and adaptive estimators together yield the optimal complexity for incoherent purity estimation; in particular, we show that the copy complexity of this problem is $$
  Θ\left(\min\left\{
  \frac{d}{\varepsilon^2} + \frac{d^{3/2}}{\varepsilon}, \frac{d^2}{\varepsilon} + \frac{\sqrt{d}}{\varepsilon^2}\right\}
  \right).
  $$

</details>


### [203] [An (almost) efficient classical algorithm for sampling from typical Gibbs states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40250)
*Saleh Naghdi,Eric R. Anschuetz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子 p-spin 模型的 Gibbs 采样问题,提出基于算法化随机局域化(ASL)的经典采样算法,可将量子优势的边界下推至自旋玻璃转变温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs 态制备被视为量子优势的潜在来源,但近期结果表明:在可高效量子制备 Gibbs 态的温度区间,经典准多项式算法也能计算局域期望值。然而在更强的"采样"模拟意义下,平均情形复杂度仍悬而未决,尤其对 Boltzmann 机量子学习与量子优越性提案所依赖的物理相关模型。本文即针对量子 p-spin 这一局域系统集合,探究 Gibbs 采样是否存在指数级量子优势。

Method: 本文采用算法化随机局域化(ASL)这一元算法。核心技术结果是一个广义收敛定理:将超立方体上任意分布的近似采样,归约为对相关"倾斜分布"均值的足够低加性误差估计。随后,作者将已有的、用于计算某类无序模型局域期望值的准多项式时间算法推广至量子 p-spin 模型,以计算这些"倾斜均值"。

Result: 作者给出了一个经典算法,可在全变差距离意义下、以任意多项式小的误差从量子 p-spin 模型的 Gibbs 态中采样。此外,通过非严格的物理论证,作者表明该均值估计算法可一直有效工作到自旋玻璃转变温度。

Conclusion: 由于高效量子算法在该自旋玻璃转变温度处同样失效,本文结果给出强证据:在低于该温度以下,量子算法无法对量子 p-spin 模型进行 Gibbs 采样,而此时经典算法仍是(几乎)高效的。因此,量子 p-spin 模型的 Gibbs 采样很可能不存在指数级量子优势。

Abstract: Gibbs state preparation has emerged as a potential avenue for quantum advantage in the study of physical systems. Although there is known advantage for computing local properties of worst-case Gibbs states, recent results suggest a different picture for average-case local systems: at temperatures where their Gibbs states can be efficiently prepared quantumly, quasi-polynomial time classical algorithms can also compute local expectation values. Yet this leaves open the possibility of an advantage under a stronger notion of simulation: sampling. Gibbs sampling underlies Boltzmann machine-based quantum learning algorithms and quantum supremacy proposals, but its classical average-case complexity for physically relevant models remains unresolved. Here, we give strong evidence against an exponential quantum advantage in Gibbs sampling for an ensemble of local systems known as the quantum $p$-spin model. We describe a classical algorithm for sampling from the Gibbs state to any polynomially small error in total variation distance. Our algorithm is based on a meta-algorithm known as algorithmic stochastic localization (ASL). Our main technical result is a generalized convergence theorem for ASL which reduces approximate sampling from any distribution on the hypercube to estimating the mean of a related "tilted distribution" to sufficiently low additive error. We then extend known quasi-polynomial time algorithms for computing local expectation values of a related disordered model to compute these "tilted means" for the quantum $p$-spin model. Finally, we give a non-rigorous physics argument that this mean-estimation algorithm works down to the spin glass transition temperature. As efficient quantum algorithms also fail at this transition, this suggests they cannot Gibbs sample the quantum $p$-spin model at temperatures below that for which classical algorithms are also (almost) efficient.

</details>


### [204] [Quantum oblique eigenprojection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40254)
*Alexander M. Dalzell,Yuan Su*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出量子算法可在块编码访问下实现一般方阵的斜特征投影，查询复杂度近线性于逆谱隙，覆盖数值范围、可对角化等常见条件，并应用于复特征值本征态制备、连续时间代数Riccati方程和Sylvester方程求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有量子特征投影算法主要针对正交（正规矩阵）投影，无法处理非正规矩阵产生的广义非正交本征子空间。作者希望将量子奇异值/特征值变换推广到非正交、非解析矩阵函数情形，扩展量子线性代数的适用范围。

Method: 给定输入矩阵的块编码，利用矩阵预解式的离散傅里叶变换，构造两侧块预处理（two-sided block preconditioning），在谱集条件下实现斜特征投影Π；复杂度对逆谱隙近线性，归一化因子接近‖Π‖。

Result: 算法查询复杂度近线性于1/gap，归一化因子接近‖Π‖，在数值范围或可对角化等常见假设下与已知正交投影结果匹配；并给出三类应用：(i)复特征值矩阵本征态制备，将Low-Su量子特征值变换推广到非实谱；(ii)连续时间代数Riccati方程，比Rodenas-Ruiz等此前求解器三次加速；(iii)常系数Sylvester方程，比Wang-Liu增广直接法二次改进。

Conclusion: 该工作表明量子计算机可高效实现斜特征投影，为在量子计算机上应用非解析矩阵函数开辟了有前景的路线，并直接加速多类矩阵方程求解任务。

Abstract: Every square matrix decomposes its underlying Hilbert space into generalized, nonorthogonal eigensubspaces. We show that a quantum computer can perform such an oblique eigenprojection $Π$ given block encoding access to the input matrix. Our approach has a query complexity nearly linear in the inverse gap and a normalization factor close to $\lVertΠ\rVert$ under a spectral-set condition on the input. This covers common assumptions on the numerical range or diagonalizability and matches known results for orthogonal eigenprojections. We achieve this with a two-sided block preconditioning that uses a discrete Fourier transform of the matrix resolvent. We describe applications to: (i) preparing eigenstates of matrices with complex eigenvalues, extending the quantum eigenvalue transformation algorithm of Low and Su beyond real spectra; (ii) solving continuous-time algebraic Riccati equations, cubically speeding up a prior solver of Rodenas-Ruiz, Zhao, and Lee; and (iii) solving ordinary Sylvester equations, quadratically improving a direct augmented method of Wang and Liu. Our result suggests a promising route to applying nonanalytic matrix functions on quantum computers.

</details>


### [205] [A General Theory of Multi-Resource Theories Involving Finite-Group Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40255)
*Yosuke Mitsuhashi,Hiroyasu Tajima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出有限群不对称性与多种量子资源理论组合的统一理论，核心是"对称信息的可及性"决定组合理论是否额外增加转换成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实量子场景通常同时施加多种约束，因此需要研究多资源理论；其中不对称性组合尤为自然，因为对称性是物理中的基本且普遍现象，而此前缺乏对有限群不对称性与广泛资源理论组合的统一处理。

Method: 以有限群不对称为切入点，统一研究其与多种资源理论的组合。关键观察是组合效果由对称信息在底层资源理论中的可及性决定，即转换所需信息能否从输入中提取并用于控制自由操作。据此分析渐近误差消失条件下的转换率，并考察时间平移协变的热操作所导致的额外兼容条件；同时研究对称性施加于基本操作而非仅施加于最终信道的设定。

Result: 若对称信息可以渐近消失误差提取，则组合理论的转换率等于两个组成理论转换率的较小者，即组合不产生额外成本。该条件适用于任意混合多体态上的LOCC、Clifford对称下多量子比特的魔术态转换以及Gibbs保持操作。热操作因时间平移协变只能获取有限对称信息，因而需要两对称性间额外的兼容条件。对基本操作施加对称性的情形，在适当假设下对LOCC和热操作建立了类似结果，并在对称性由Pauli算子表示时对魔术建立类似结果。此外，有限群对称性不降低每副本的渐近ergotropy，因此不引入新的完全被动态。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了刻画有限群不对称性与多类资源理论组合的统一框架，表明在许多重要资源理论中对称性组合在渐近意义下不增加成本，而热操作等时间平移协变场景则需额外兼容条件，并澄清了有限群对称性对ergotropy与完全被动态的影响。

Abstract: Resource theories provide a universal framework for quantifying properties of quantum states, such as entanglement, magic, athermality, and asymmetry, as resources under various constraints encountered in quantum science. Since realistic settings typically impose several constraints at once, it is important to study multi-resource theories that combine them. Among these, combinations with asymmetry are especially natural, as symmetry is fundamental and ubiquitous in physics. Here we develop a general theory that treats combinations of finite-group asymmetry with a broad range of resource theories in a unified manner. Our key observation is that the effect of combining the two constraints is governed by the accessibility of symmetry information under the underlying resource theory: whether the information needed for the conversion can be extracted from the input and used to control free operations. If this can be done with asymptotically vanishing error, we show that the conversion rate of the combined theory equals the smaller of the rates of the two constituent theories, so combining them incurs no additional cost. This condition holds for LOCC on arbitrary mixed multipartite states, magic-state conversion of multiqubit systems under Clifford symmetries, and Gibbs-preserving operations. Thermal operations, being time-translation covariant, can access only limited symmetry information, giving an additional compatibility condition between the two symmetries. We also analyze symmetry imposed on elementary operations rather than only on the resulting channel. We establish analogous results for LOCC and thermal operations under suitable assumptions, and for magic when the symmetry is represented by Pauli operators. Finally, we show that finite-group symmetry does not reduce the asymptotic ergotropy per copy and hence introduces no new completely passive states.

</details>


### [206] [Self-testing the toric code against classical communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40259)
*Gustavo Fróes,Amanda Wei,Carlos de Gois,Denis Rochette,Marc-Olivier Renou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在网络中允许设备间有限距离经典通信的宽松自测试框架，证明环面几何上分布的toric code码空间可噪声鲁棒地自测试，通信容忍度与网络直径成正比，并给出构造算法，将结果推广到surface code与cluster states（可鲁棒抵抗一轮最近邻经典通信）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有绝大多数device-independent自测试协议在各方能够交换消息时即失效，因此需要研究在量子网络中设备可能秘密进行有界距离经典通信时，如何仍保持认证的可靠性（soundness）。

Method: 将自测试置于量子网络的宽松设定：诚实协议中各方不通信，但不可信设备可在网络中有界距离内秘密交换经典消息；针对环形（torus）几何网络上分布的toric code，构造自测试协议并证明其噪声鲁棒性；同时提出一种算法化方法来构造此类协议，并推广至surface code和cluster states。

Result: 证明了toric code的码空间可在该设定下噪声鲁棒地自测试，其抵抗的通信距离与网络直径成正比；所提算法可构造此类协议；结果可扩展到surface code及cluster states，并能鲁棒抵抗一轮最近邻经典通信。

Conclusion: 在允许设备间有界经典通信的量子网络中，拓扑码（toric/surface code）与cluster states仍可实现鲁棒的device-independent自测试，突破了传统自测试对无通信假设的依赖。

Abstract: Device-independent self-testing certifies a multipartite quantum state from the correlations it produces alone, without assumptions on the inner workings of the devices. Most existing self-tests, however, break down as soon as the parties can exchange messages. We consider a relaxed setting in a quantum network, where in the honest version of the protocol, the parties never communicate, yet certification remains sound even in an untrusted case where the untrusted devices secretly exchange classical messages within a bounded distance in the network. We show that the code space of the toric code, distributed over a network with the geometry of a torus, can be self-tested in this setting in a noise-robust way, against communication up to distances proportional to the network diameter. We also develop an algorithmic method for constructing such protocols and extend our results to the surface code and cluster states, robust to one round of nearest-neighbor classical communication

</details>


### [207] [Quantum Algorithms for Minimum Generating Set](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40260)
*Bireswar Das,Udit Kumar,Kavita Samant,Dhara Thakkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出求解可解黑箱群最小生成集的量子多项式时间算法，并对每个非交换合成因子同构于固定 $S_d$ 子群的群类 $\Gamma_d$，设计计算主序列与最小生成集的量子算法，最后证明一般黑箱群的最小生成集问题属于 $\textrm{NP} \cap \textrm{coAM}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑箱群的最小生成集问题是计算群论的核心问题之一，经典方法在一般情形下缺乏高效算法。作者希望借助量子计算，在可解群以及受限合成因子的群类上突破多项式时间求解，并厘清一般黑箱群该问题的复杂度归属。

Method: 首先构造计算可解黑箱群最小生成集的量子多项式时间算法；随后引入群类 $\Gamma_d$，其中每个非交换合成因子同构于固定 $d$ 的对称群 $S_d$ 的某个子群。针对 $\Gamma_d$，设计量子算法计算阿贝尔因子群的直积分解并求解因子群的构造性成员问题，进而计算主序列，并基于主序列构造最小生成集算法；最后用复杂度类包含关系刻画一般黑箱群的最小生成集问题。

Result: 得到可解黑箱群最小生成集的量子多项式时间算法；对 $\Gamma_d$ 中的黑箱群，给出计算阿贝尔因子群直积分解、求解因子群构造性成员问题、计算主序列以及计算最小生成集的量子多项式时间算法；并证明一般黑箱群的最小生成集问题属于 $\textrm{NP} \cap \textrm{coAM}$。

Conclusion: 该工作将量子算法成功应用于黑箱群最小生成集及相关结构计算问题，在可解群与 $\Gamma_d$ 类群上实现了多项式时间求解，同时给出一般黑箱群最小生成集问题位于 $\textrm{NP} \cap \textrm{coAM}$ 的复杂度上界，为后续研究提供了算法与复杂度两方面的基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a polynomial-time quantum algorithm for computing a minimum-sized generating set of solvable black-box groups. Next, we consider the class $Γ_d$ of black-box groups, where every non-abelian composition factor is isomorphic to a subgroup of the symmetric group $S_d$ for a fixed $d$. We design polynomial-time quantum algorithms to compute the direct product decomposition of abelian factor groups and solve the constructive membership problem for factor groups of groups from $Γ_d$. With the help of these algorithms, we design a quantum algorithm for computing a chief series of black-box groups from $Γ_d$. Using the chief series, we construct a polynomial-time quantum algorithm for computing minimum generating sets of black-box groups from $Γ_d$. Finally, we show that the minimum generating set problem for general black-box groups is in $\textrm{NP} \cap \textrm{coAM}$.

</details>


### [208] [Quantum List Recovery and Decoding: Achievability and Limitations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40262)
*Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Xiaojuan Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了平衡折叠量子Reed-Solomon（FQRS）码与平衡随机CSS码的量子列表恢复（QLR）与量子列表解码（QLD）的组合上下界，给出了接近量子Singleton界时列表规模的精确渐近表达式及QLD半径权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对CSS码，分别处理X与Z扇区会使输出列表规模相乘，且不同经典候选在稳定子商化后可能坍缩（不再逻辑可区分），因此需要专门研究保持“稳定子可区分”的量子列表解码/恢复界。

Method: 针对FQRS码与平衡随机CSS码，构造并证明：联合X/Z候选列表的配对引理（保持单扇区系数、避免列表规模乘积损失）；基于经典投影-拼接（projection-and-patching）与稳定子可区分性的量子广义Singleton界（用于QLD逆界）；适配Chen-Zhang的折叠Reed-Solomon构造使候选保持稳定子可区分（用于QLR下界）；并证明随机CSS码的经典坏列表以高概率在稳定子商化后存活。

Result: 令量子码率R∈(0,1)，分量码率R1=(1+R)/2，半径ρ=(1-R)/2-γ趋近量子Singleton界（FQRS确定性成立，平衡随机CSS码以高概率成立），则L*_QLR=ℓ^{Θ(R1/γ)}，L*_QLD=Θ((1-R)/γ)（γ→0）。QLD渐近精确半径权衡为ρ*_L=(L/(L+1))·(1-R)/2，结论可推广到平均半径情形。

Conclusion: 本文建立了接近量子Singleton界时QLR与QLD的精确组合界，通过配对引理消除X/Z分离处理带来的列表规模乘积损失，并借助量子广义Singleton界与适配的FQRS构造给出匹配的上下界；结果表明量子列表解码半径由L/(L+1)因子精确刻画。

Abstract: Quantum list recovery (QLR) and quantum list decoding (QLD) seek short lists of logically distinct Pauli corrections consistent with a syndrome and prescribed error constraints. For CSS codes, two issues arise: treating the \(X\)- and \(Z\)-sectors separately can multiply their output list sizes, while distinct classical candidates can collapse after stabilizer quotienting.
  We study combinatorial upper and lower bounds for balanced folded quantum Reed--Solomon (FQRS) codes and balanced random CSS codes, both studied by Bergamaschi, Golowich, and Gunn (STOC 24). Let \(R\in(0,1)\) be the quantum rate and \(R_1=(1+R)/2\) the common component rate. As the radius \(ρ=(1-R)/2-γ\) approaches the quantum Singleton bound, we have, deterministically for FQRS codes and w.h.p. for balanced random CSS codes, \[
  L^\star_{\rm QLR}
  =
  \ell^{Θ(R_1/γ)},
  \qquad
  L^\star_{\rm QLD}
  =
  Θ\!\left(\frac{1-R}γ\right)
  \qquad (γ\downarrow0). \] The asymptotically exact QLD radius tradeoff is \[
  ρ_L^\star
  =
  \frac{L}{L+1}\frac{1-R}{2}. \] These conclusions extend to the average-radius setting.
  For achievability, building on the work of Brakensiek, Chen, Dhar, and Zhang (STOC 2026), we establish a pairing lemma for joint \(X/Z\) candidate lists that preserves the one-sector coefficient and avoids a product loss in list size. For the QLD converse, we prove a quantum generalized Singleton bound based on the classical projection-and-patching argument with stabilizer distinctness. For the QLR lower bounds, we adapt the folded Reed--Solomon construction of Chen and Zhang (STOC 2025) so that the candidates remain stabilizer distinct. For random CSS codes, we show classical bad lists survive the stabilizer quotient with high probability.

</details>


### [209] [Superlinear Quantum Query Lower Bounds for Subgraph Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40263)
*Amin Shiraz Gilani,Xingyu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出了子图检测问题（在邻接矩阵查询模型下）无条件下超线性量子查询复杂度下界，回答了长期悬而未决的问题。对固定团 K_r（r≥4）证明需 n^{λ_r-o(1)} 次查询，λ_4=19/18 且随 r 严格递增并趋近 2；对任意色数 c≥4 的固定连通图均给出超线性下界；并证明完全二部图 K_{r,r} 需 n^{β_r-o(1)} 次查询，β_{10}=181/180，说明色数并不能刻画该问题的量子查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子图检测的 H 副本是常数量级证书，非负权重 adversary method 无法证明超线性下界，因此该问题是否存在超线性量子下界长期未解。本文旨在给出首个无条件下超线性量子查询复杂度下界。

Method: 核心技术结果是：当输入比特独立采样时，从已知族中寻找全 1 证书的下界。证明结合 Zhandry 压缩 oracle（CRYPTO 2019）与随机植入证书的条件化，并改编 Belovs（FOCS 2026）的论证。困难实例由含大量目标子图副本且重叠受限的图构成：团使用 Gowers–Janzer（CPC 2021）构造，完全二部图使用随机构造。

Result: 对每个固定 r≥4，检测 K_r 需 n^{λ_r-o(1)} 次查询，λ_4=19/18，λ_r 随 r 严格递增且 λ_r≥2-4√(2/r)+O(1/r)；对色数 c≥4 的任意固定连通图 H 均有超线性下界，随 c 增大趋近二次：足够大 c 时需 n^{2-O(√(log log c/c))-o(1)} 次查询；检测 K_{r,r} 需 n^{β_r-o(1)} 次查询，β_{10}=181/180，β_r≥2-O(1/√r)。

Conclusion: 子图检测的量子查询复杂度存在超线性下界，该问题中色数不足以刻画复杂度；下界随色数增大趋近二次，并通过团与完全二部图两类实例揭示了指数与色数之间更精细的关系。

Abstract: Subgraph detection asks whether an $n$-vertex graph, accessed through queries to its adjacency matrix, contains a copy of a fixed graph $H$. We prove the first unconditional superlinear lower bounds on the bounded-error quantum query complexity of this problem, answering a longstanding open question. A copy of $H$ is a certificate of constant size, so the adversary method with nonnegative weights cannot prove superlinear lower bounds.
  For every fixed $r\ge 4$, detecting the clique $K_r$ requires $n^{λ_r-o(1)}$ queries, where $λ_4=19/18$, the exponents $λ_r$ increase strictly with $r$, and $λ_r\ge 2-4\sqrt{2/r}+O(1/r)$. More generally, we prove superlinear lower bounds for detecting every fixed connected graph $H$ with chromatic number $c\ge 4$. These bounds approach quadratic as $c$ grows: for sufficiently large $c$, detection requires $n^{2-O(\sqrt{\log\log c/c})-o(1)}$ queries. Chromatic number alone does not characterize the quantum query complexity of subgraph detection: we show that detecting the complete bipartite graph $K_{r,r}$ requires $n^{β_r-o(1)}$ queries, where $β_{10}=181/180$ and $β_r\ge 2-O(1/\sqrt{r})$.
  Our main technical result is a lower bound for finding an all-ones certificate from a known family when the input bits are sampled independently. Its proof combines Zhandry's compressed oracle (CRYPTO 2019) with conditioning on a randomly planted certificate, adapting an argument of Belovs (FOCS 2026). Our hard instances are built from graphs containing many copies of the desired subgraph with limited overlap. For cliques, we use a construction of Gowers and Janzer (CPC 2021); for complete bipartite graphs, we use a random construction.

</details>


### [210] [Two-Stage Quantum-Classical Distribution Network Reconfiguration via Cycle-Edge Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40264)
*Nowar Alashkar,Cade Kennedy,Phillip C. Lotshaw,Shaked Regev,Miguel Angel Lopez-Ruiz,Ananth Kaushik,Paul Smith,Claudio Girotto,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段量子-经典方法求解配电网重构（DNR）：先用基于循环-边编码的迭代LR-QAOA在可行子空间搜索，再用所得分布引导MISOCP求解，在六个33至417节点系统上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DNR是一个受拓扑与电气运行约束的组合优化问题，目标是寻找低损耗网络拓扑。经典方法在大规模网络上求解效率受限，而量子方法受量子比特预算约束，难以直接处理全空间。因此需要一种能在有限量子资源下兼具可行性与效率的求解框架。

Method: 提出两阶段量子-经典方法。第一阶段采用迭代线性斜坡量子交替算子拟设（LR-QAOA），在新颖的循环-边编码（cycle-edge encoding）所构造的拓扑可行子空间中进行搜索；该编码保证每个被表示的构型都是生成树（spanning tree），并可按量子比特预算调整子空间的大小与覆盖范围。第二阶段将得到的采样分布用于引导混合整数二阶锥规划（MISOCP）求解器。

Result: 在六个规模从33到417节点的配电网络上，通过仿真以及在囚禁离子量子硬件上执行进行评估。在全部六个测试系统上，由硬件导出分布引导的MISOCP求解器均能在中位求解时间内获得与已知最优解差距在1%以内的现有可行解，且中位求解时间短于对应的无引导求解器。

Conclusion: 循环-边编码有效保证了量子搜索空间内构型的拓扑可行性，并支持按比特预算灵活调节；量子采样分布作为先验信息可显著加速经典MISOCP求解，且该优势在真实量子硬件上依然成立，表明量子-经典混合框架对DNR具有实用潜力。

Abstract: Distribution network reconfiguration (DNR) is a combinatorial optimization problem that seeks a low-loss network topology subject to topological and electrical operating constraints. In this work, we propose a two-stage quantum-classical method for DNR. First, an iterative linear-ramp Quantum Alternating Operator Ansatz (LR-QAOA) searches topologically feasible subspaces constructed using a novel cycle-edge encoding. The encoding guarantees that every represented configuration is a spanning tree while allowing the size and coverage of each subspace to be adjusted to the available qubit budget. Second, the resulting sample distribution is used to guide a mixed-integer second-order-cone programming (MISOCP) solver. The method is evaluated on six distribution networks ranging from 33 to 417 buses through simulation and execution on trapped-ion quantum hardware. Across all six test systems, the MISOCP solver guided by hardware-derived distributions reaches an incumbent within 1\% of the best-known solution in less median solver time than the corresponding unguided solver.

</details>


### [211] [Testing quantum Gaussianity with constant sample complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40270)
*Mahtab Yaghubi Rad,Ricard Puig,Saksham Hassanandani,Luke Coffman,Jens Eisert,Carlos Bravo-Prieto,Antonio Anna Mele*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了检验费米子与玻色子高斯性的最优且鲁棒算法，样本复杂度与系统尺寸、粒子数及能量无关，并引入统一的"梯度流方法"作为分析核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯态是量子光学与多体物理中最重要的结构族之一，也是高效经典模拟的典型范式。然而，针对高斯性的最优检验算法此前一直未知，因此需要既最优又鲁棒的检验方案。

Method: 提出统一的"梯度流方法"：利用表示论，将定义对称性的近似满足转化为对拒绝概率梯度的定量控制，从而沿连续下降路径从输入态逼近目标族，其路径长度可界定两者的距离。具体协议使用与高斯性精确对称刻画相关的实验可行测量：费米子采用两副本上的Bell采样，玻色子采用三副本上的被动干涉与光子计数；容错检验器则需在更多但仍为常数的副本数上进行联合测量。

Result: 给出了费米子与玻色子高斯态检验的最优鲁棒算法，样本复杂度独立于系统尺寸、粒子数和能量；并进一步得到容错检验器，其所需副本数更大但仍为常数。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了高斯性最优检验的空白，所提梯度流方法可适用于所考虑的所有结构族，兼具最优性、鲁棒性与实验可行性。

Abstract: Efficiently testing whether a quantum state possesses a given structure is both a fundamental and a practical task in quantum information. Among the most important structured families are Gaussian states, which underpin quantum optics and many-body physics while defining paradigmatic regimes of efficient classical simulation. Despite the central role of Gaussian states, optimal tests of Gaussianity have remained unknown. Here we give optimal and robust algorithms for testing fermionic and bosonic Gaussian states, with sample complexity independent of system size, particle number, and energy. Remarkably, the protocols implement experimentally feasible measurements associated with known exact symmetry characterizations of Gaussianity, using Bell sampling on two copies for fermions and passive interferometry with photon counting on three copies for bosons. We further obtain tolerant testers requiring joint measurements on a larger, but still constant, number of copies. At the heart of our analysis is a unified approach---which we call the gradient-flow method---that applies across all structured families considered here and underlies our optimal testers: representation theory turns approximate satisfaction of the defining symmetry into quantitative control of the gradient of the rejection probability, allowing a continuous descent from the input state to the target family whose length bounds their distance.

</details>


### [212] [Conditioning-Free Non-Uniform Quantum Fourier and Chebyshev Transforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40277)
*Chaowen Guan,Akshit Katiyar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种高效的非均匀切比雪夫变换量子算法，基于改进的非均匀量子傅里叶变换（NUQFT），实现O(1)归一化、O(L)量子比特、Õ(L²)门复杂度的ε精度块编码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QFT-based量子切比雪夫变换无法处理在x∈[-1,1]上均匀采样、但在角度θ=arccos x上非均匀的节点。作者希望构建能够处理这类非均匀采样节点的切比雪夫变换量子算法，并消除已有NUQFT中误差界对几何相关参数κ的依赖。

Method: 1) 改进现有NUQFT，去除非均匀节点采样中的conditioning，使误差界不再依赖于几何参数κ；2) 利用切比雪夫变换矩阵可表示为两个Type-II非均匀DFT的平均值这一事实，用单个受控NUQFT电路实现；3) 提供显式oracle构造，包括此前仅作为假设的行访问oracle；4) 给出端到端实现及复杂度分析，涵盖成功概率与输出态误差。

Result: 得到的ε精度块编码具有O(1)归一化，使用O(L)量子比特和Õ(L²)门，其中L=log N+log(1/ε)。误差界独立于几何相关参数κ，并完成了包含成功概率与输出态误差的端到端复杂度分析。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了首个适用于非均匀采样节点的高效量子切比雪夫变换算法，通过去conditioning的NUQFT与单受控电路设计，实现了归一化常数与几何无关的误差界，并补全了此前缺失的oracle构造，为相关量子数值算法提供了完整实现框架。

Abstract: We present an efficient quantum algorithm for the non-uniform Chebyshev transform. It is defined as the projection of a function onto Chebyshev polynomials sampled at given nodes that are uniform in $x\in[-1,1]$, and hence non-uniform in the angle $θ=\arccos x$, a setting that QFT-based quantum Chebyshev transforms cannot handle. Our construction is based on an improvement of an existing Non-uniform Quantum Fourier Transform (NUQFT) whereby we remove the conditioning from non-uniform node sampling. Hence, error bounds are independent of the geometry-dependent parameter $κ$ of prior work. We use the fact that Chebyshev transform matrix is the average of two Type-II non-uniform DFTs, which we implement with a single controlled NUQFT circuit. We provide explicit oracle constructions, including the row-access oracle previously left as an assumption. The resulting $\varepsilon$-accurate block encoding has $O(1)$ normalization and uses $O(L)$ qubits and $\widetilde O(L^2)$ gates, where $L=\log N+\log(1/\varepsilon)$. We give an end-to-end implementation with complexity analysis, including the success probability and output-state error.

</details>


### [213] [Computational Bounds for $f$-Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40278)
*Oren Renard,Nicholas Spooner*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为 f-routing 量子位置验证协议中的显式函数证明无条件资源下界，证明一致攻击者若成功则函数 f 落入若干复杂度类，从而导出对多项式乃至拟多项式量子比特数安全的显式函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: f-routing 是量子位置验证的重要候选协议，但针对显式函数的安全性保证有限；已有工作依赖通信复杂度下界，而该下界本质上至多与输入长度线性相关，限制了可证明的安全范围。

Method: 作者绕过通信复杂度方法，针对一致生成的攻击策略建立无条件资源下界：设输入长度 n，攻击使用 q 个量子比特、描述长度 poly(q)、在每个输入上成功概率至少 1-ε，则分别对任意量子信道、Pauli-稀疏酉、Clifford+T 电路三类策略证明 f 的复杂度上界。技术路线是将策略成功转化为函数 f 的相应复杂度类归属。

Result: 对充分小常数 ε>0：(1) 任意量子信道策略下 f∈QSZK(poly(nq))；(2) 至多 s 个非零 Pauli 系数的显式 Pauli-稀疏酉下 f∈DTIME(poly(nqs))；(3) 至多 t 个 magic 门的 Clifford+T 电路下 f∈DTIME(poly(nq2^t))。

Conclusion: 利用时间与空间层次定理，得到对多项式乃至拟多项式量子比特 q 安全的显式函数；在限制对抗计算并增加诚实评估复杂度的代价下，这些界超过了 Bluhm、Christandl 与 Speelman (2022) 对内积函数 IP 的 q≤log n 界。

Abstract: The $f$-routing protocol is a leading candidate for quantum position verification (Kent, Munro, and Spiller, 2011), but security guarantees for explicit functions remain limited. We prove unconditional resource lower bounds for uniform attackers; our new techniques bypass communication-complexity bounds central to previous works, which are inherently at most linear in the input length.
  We show that, for input length $n$ and sufficiently small constant $ε>0$, a uniformly generated strategy using $q$ qubits and having description length $\mathrm{poly}(q)$, with success probability at least $1-ε$ on every input, implies the following computational bounds on $f$:
  1. If the strategies are arbitrary quantum channels, then $f\in\mathrm{QSZK}(\mathrm{poly}(nq))$, where $\mathrm{QSZK}(T)$ is the class of languages having quantum statistical zero knowledge proofs in which the verifier runs in time $T$ (and the simulator in time $\mathrm{poly}(T)$).
  2. If the strategies are explicit Pauli-sparse unitaries on $q$ qubits that have at most $s$ nonzero Pauli coefficients, then $f\in\mathrm{DTIME}(\mathrm{poly}(nqs))$.
  3. If the strategies are Clifford+T circuits using at most $t$ magic gates, then $f\in\mathrm{DTIME}(\mathrm{poly}(nq2^t))$.
  Time and space hierarchies then yield explicit functions secure against polynomial and even quasipolynomial qubits $q$ under our computational restrictions. These bounds exceed the $q\le\log n$ bound of Bluhm, Christandl, and Speelman (2022) for inner product function $f=\mathrm{IP}$, at the cost of restricting adversarial computation and increasing honest evaluation complexity.

</details>


### [214] [Classical simulation of coherent crosstalk in surface codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40279)
*Andrew S. Darmawan,Yelyzaveta Kolesnyk,Robert Koenig*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出多项式时间经典算法，可从相干近邻ZZ串扰下表面码态的综合征分布采样；但当单比特相干噪声与此类串扰同时存在时，若多项式层级不塌缩，则不存在高效经典采样算法（即使允许常数乘性误差）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表面码对单比特相干错误的经典模拟已有高效算法，但实际硬件中相干串扰（如ZZ耦合）普遍存在。作者希望明确：在相干噪声与串扰共存时，经典模拟是否仍可行，从而厘清表面码模拟复杂度的边界。

Method: 构造多项式时间经典算法，针对仅受相干近邻ZZ串扰污染的表面码态，从综合征分布采样；通过将相干噪声下的综合征分布与关联于非平面图的特定IQP线路输出分布建立联系，进行复杂度理论论证。

Result: 对仅含相干ZZ串扰的情形，给出高效经典采样算法；对单比特相干噪声与串扰并存的情形，证明高效经典采样存在复杂度理论障碍——除非多项式层级塌缩，否则无法以常数乘性误差近似采样。

Conclusion: 表面码在相干噪声下的经典可模拟性依赖于噪声类型：单独的单比特相干噪声或单独的ZZ串扰均可高效模拟，二者共存则导致IQP型困难，表明该混合噪声模型下不存在高效经典采样算法。

Abstract: We give a polynomial-time classical algorithm which samples from the syndrome distribution of a surface code state corrupted by coherent nearest-neighbor $ZZ$ crosstalk. Our algorithm complements the known efficient simulation algorithms for coherent single-qubit errors in the surface code. When both single-qubit coherent noise and coherent crosstalk are present, we find a complexity-theoretic obstruction to efficient simulation: unless the polynomial hierarchy collapses, there is no efficient classical algorithm for sampling from the syndrome distribution, even up to a constant multiplicative error. This is obtained by connecting the syndrome distribution under coherent noise to the output distribution of certain IQP circuits associated with a non-planar graph.

</details>


### [215] [Distinguishing Coherent Crosstalk from Calibration Drift via Pauli-Transfer Signatures and Quantum Edge Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40283)
*Syed Emad Uddin Shubha,Tasnuva Farheen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于残差泡利转移矩阵(PTM)的结构化验证与检测框架，证明局部幺正保迹信道的乘积不能混合权重1与权重2的泡利算符，且任意指定量子比特对的相互作用方向在恒等附近局部可观测；通过随机泡利测量构建反对称检测器，仿真中16384个随机设置在5%校准假阳性下达到λ_min≈0.13的检测阈值，并呈M^{-1/2}缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多租户量子处理器暴露脉冲级攻击面，相干串扰在平均门失效率相同时会伪装成良性校准漂移，传统平均失效率指标无法区分二者，因此需要一种结构性验证与检测方法来识别此类攻击。

Method: 基于残差泡利转移矩阵(PTM)构建框架：(1)证明局部幺正、保迹信道的乘积在任意强度或轴相干漂移下不能混合权重1与权重2泡利算符；(2)借助PTM-哈密顿量关系，证明任意指定量子比特对的九个相互作用系数到反对称交叉权重特征的一阶映射在缩放下为等距，从而各相互作用方向局部可观测；(3)展示大局部旋转下该特征被抵消，并证明全交叉权重范数在局部幺正复合下不变作为结构性对策；(4)对校准的小局部旋转，采用共享随机泡利测量支持基于反对称检测器的周期验证。

Result: 仿真中16384个随机设置在5%校准假阳性目标下给出检测阈值λ_min≈0.13，且随设置数M约呈M^{-1/2}缩放；进一步实验刻画了弛豫、退极化、原生ZZ涨落及时间依赖脉冲动力学下的检测表现；FRQI编码配合泡利图量子Hadamard边缘检测以数值精度复现经典结构边缘分数。

Conclusion: 残差PTM提供了一种结构性验证与检测手段，可区分相干串扰与良性校准漂移；反对称交叉权重特征在恒等附近对每个相互作用方向局部可观测，全交叉权重范数不变性可作为结构性对策，随机泡利测量支持周期验证，并在边缘检测任务中复现经典结果。

Abstract: Multi-tenant quantum processors expose a pulse-level attack surface in which coherent crosstalk can mimic benign calibration drift at matched average gate infidelity. We present a structural verification and detection framework based on the residual Pauli transfer matrix (PTM). We prove that products of local unital, trace-preserving channels cannot mix weight-1 and weight-2 Pauli operators, even under coherent drift of arbitrary strength or axis. Building on established PTM-to-Hamiltonian relations, we prove that, for any specified qubit pair in an arbitrarily large system, the first-order map from its nine interaction coefficients to the antisymmetric cross-weight feature is an isometry up to scale. Thus, every interaction direction is locally observable near the identity. We also exhibit cancellation of this feature under large local rotations and prove that the full cross-weight norm is invariant under local unitary composition, providing a structural countermeasure. For calibrated small local rotations, shared randomized-Pauli measurements support periodic verification using the evaluated antisymmetric detector. In simulation, $16{,}384$ randomized settings yield a detection threshold of $λ_{\min}\approx0.13$ at a $5\%$ calibrated false-positive target, with approximately $M^{-1/2}$ scaling in the number of settings $M$. Further experiments characterize detection under relaxation, depolarization, native ZZ fluctuations, and time-dependent pulse dynamics. Finally, an FRQI encoding with Pauli-graph quantum Hadamard edge detection reproduces the classical structural edge score to numerical precision.

</details>


### [216] [Verifiable Quantum Advantage and Computation via Quantum Circuit Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40289)
*Alexandru Gheorghiu,Aparna Gupte,Vojtěch Havlíček,Yunchao Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 利用量子不可区分混淆(qiO)构造可经典验证的量子优势协议与BQP计算的经典验证协议，并证明无辅助位酉电路qiO的最坏到平均归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为基于峰值随机电路采样的启发式量子优势方案提供严格密码学基础，并探索(量子)iO在无需额外计算硬度假设下的非平凡密码学应用，同时实现BQP计算的标准模型下公开可验证经典验证。

Method: 在qiO假设下，结合略强于BQP≠BPP的假设构造两消息、高效且公开可验证的量子优势协议；构造两个BQP经典验证协议：其一仅依赖qiO且私有可验证，其二额外假设后量子单向函数并公开可验证；证明无辅助位酉电路qiO的最坏到平均归约，扩展Canetti等的局部混合框架至量子类比假设。

Result: 得到高效公开可验证的两消息量子优势协议，为Aaronson-Zhang峰值随机电路采样方案提供严格密码学基础；获得仅依赖qiO的私有可验证BQP验证协议，以及基于qiO与后量子单向函数的首个标准模型下公开可验证BQP经典验证协议；证明无辅助位酉电路qiO的最坏到平均归约。

Conclusion: qiO可支撑可经典验证的量子优势及BQP经典验证，且结果在仅对无辅助位酉电路假设qiO时仍成立，并有最坏到平均归约作为支持证据。

Abstract: We construct protocols for classically verifiable quantum advantage and classical verification of $\mathsf{BQP}$ computations using \emph{quantum indistinguishability obfuscation} (qiO). Specifically, given qiO and assuming a slightly stronger version of $\mathsf{BQP}\neq\mathsf{BPP}$, we construct a two-message quantum-advantage protocol that is efficiently and publicly verifiable. Our result can be viewed as a rigorous cryptographic foundation for the heuristic quantum advantage proposals based on \emph{peaked random circuit sampling} of Aaronson and Zhang (arXiv:2404.14493).
  We also construct two simple protocols for classically verifying arbitrary $\mathsf{BQP}$ computations. The first protocol is privately verifiable and assumes only the existence of qiO. This gives a rare example of a nontrivial cryptographic application of (quantum) iO that does not make additional computational hardness assumptions. The second protocol additionally assumes post-quantum one-way functions and is \emph{publicly verifiable}. To our knowledge, this is the first publicly verifiable protocol for classical verification of $\mathsf{BQP}$ computations under computational assumptions in the standard model.
  We show that all our results hold when qiO is assumed only for ancilla-free unitary circuits. As evidence supporting this assumption, we prove a worst-to-average-case reduction for obfuscating such circuits. This reduction extends the local-mixing framework of Canetti, Chamon, Mucciolo and Ruckenstein (TCC 2024) under quantum analogues of their assumptions.

</details>


### [217] [The power of constant-depth quantum circuits of unbounded size](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40294)
*Sergii Strelchuk,Sathyawageeswar Subramanian,Máté Weisz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究无界扇入门量子电路能否在常数深度实现任意酉算符，给出置换、对角酉与任意纯态制备的常数深度精确构造，并将任意酉实现归约为无迹对合及基于端口隐形传态的近似方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典无界扇入电路在规模不受限时可用常数深度计算任意布尔函数。作者追问：若同时取消电路规模与辅助量子比特的限制，由任意单比特门和广义Toffoli门构成的量子电路是否也能在常数深度实现任意酉算符。

Method: 利用门集中的扇出构造任意计算基置换、对角酉算符与任意纯态制备的精确常数深度电路；将扇出替换为原门集上的精确电路以保持常数深度；通过复制指定正交基向量、提取标签、实现受限酉族等给出等价表述；用额外一个干净量子比特把任意酉实现归约为无迹酉对合；并采用端口隐形传态（输入维数d、端口数M≥d²-1）构造深度O(√d)的酉电路。

Result: 在含扇出的门集下，精确构造使用指数级多门与辅助比特，且所有辅助比特返回零；替换扇出后深度仍为常数，但规模上界可能变为双指数。任意酉的常数深度实现仍开放。端口隐形传态电路深度为O(√d)，与M无关，包含资源制备与端口选择，纠缠保真度至少为(1-(d²-1)/(2M))²；固定d时精度可任意提高而不增加深度。

Conclusion: 置换、对角酉与纯态制备可在常数深度精确实现，任意酉的常数深度实现尚未解决；该问题等价于基向量复制、标签提取及受限酉族实现，并可归约到无迹对合。自适应测量下门隐形传态深度与Clifford层级层数成正比。端口隐形传态在固定d下可任意逼近任意酉而不增加深度，但能否去除对d的依赖仍未定。

Abstract: Classical circuits with unbounded fan-in can compute any Boolean function in constant depth when their size is unrestricted. We ask whether removing the restrictions on circuit size and ancillary qubits also allows quantum circuits built from arbitrary single-qubit gates and generalised Toffoli gates to implement every unitary in constant depth.
  We give exact constant-depth constructions for arbitrary permutations of computational basis states, diagonal unitaries and the preparation of arbitrary pure states. These connect quantum state preparation to reversible classical computation and the preparation of probability distributions. With fanout in the gate set, they use exponentially many gates and ancillary qubits and return all ancillary qubits to zero. Replacing fanout by an exact circuit over the original gate set preserves constant depth, although the size bounds can become doubly exponential.
  The implementation of arbitrary unitaries in constant depth remains open. We give equivalent formulations in terms of copying the vectors of a specified orthonormal basis, extracting their labels and implementing restricted families of unitaries. We also reduce arbitrary unitary implementation to that of traceless unitary involutions using one additional clean qubit. With adaptive measurements, gate teleportation gives depth proportional to the level of a gate in the Clifford hierarchy.
  Towards arbitrary unitary implementation in constant depth, we use port-based teleportation: for input dimension $d$ and $M\geq d^2-1$ ports, we construct a unitary circuit of depth $O(\sqrt d)$, independent of $M$ and including resource preparation and port selection, with entanglement fidelity at least $(1-(d^2-1)/(2M))^2$. Thus, at fixed $d$, the approximation can be made arbitrarily accurate without increasing depth. Whether the dependence on $d$ can also be removed remains open.

</details>


### [218] [Need for Coherent Access in Constructing Quantum Cryptography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40301)
*Minki Hhan,Changhun Oh,Vaughn Sohn*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在经典可访问随机预言机模型下构造了量子安全单向函数（OWF）存在但超对数输出长度伪随机态（PRS）不存在的量子预言机，从而与已知的黑盒PRS构造形成对比，并指出PRS构造需要相干访问。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知存在从量子安全单向函数黑盒构造伪随机态生成器的方法，这似乎与OWF存在性不足以支撑超对数长度PRS的直觉相矛盾。作者希望厘清这一矛盾，明确访问模型（经典可访问 vs. 相干访问）在PRS构造中的关键作用。

Method: 构造量子预言机，其中包含经典可访问的随机预言机（即使量子算法也只能经典访问），并保持另一预言机分量不变。在此基础上证明：相对于该预言机，量子安全OWF存在，但超对数输出长度的PRS不存在；且该不可能性适用于任何经典可访问的经典预言机替代随机预言机。同时证明对数输出长度的PRFS在该预言机下存在。

Result: 相对于所构造的预言机，量子安全单向函数存在，但超对数输出长度的伪随机态不存在；对数输出长度的伪随机函数类态（PRFS）存在，从而给出经典可访问的对数长度PRFS与超对数长度PRS之间的预言机分离。

Conclusion: 该结果说明，从量子安全OWF构造PRS时必须使用对预言机的相干访问；且从对数长度到超对数长度的全黑盒PRS长度扩展，必须对底层短PRS使用相干访问。经典可访问性不足以支持此类构造。

Abstract: We construct quantum oracles relative to which quantum-secure one-way functions (OWFs) exist but pseudorandom states (PRSs) with superlogarithmic output length do not. At first glance, this appears to contradict the known black-box constructions of PRS generators from quantum-secure OWFs. The distinction lies in the access model to the oracles; our oracle separation uses \emph{classical-accessible} random oracles that can be accessed only classically even by quantum algorithms. In fact, our impossibility of PRSs applies to \emph{any} classically accessible classical oracle in place of the random oracle, while keeping the other oracle component unchanged, showing the need for coherent access in constructing PRSs.
  We further show that logarithmic output length pseudorandom function-like states (PRFSs) exist relative to our oracles, giving an oracle separation between classically accessible logarithmic length PRFSs and superlogarithmic length PRSs. This shows that fully black-box PRS length extension from logarithmic to superlogarithmic output length must use coherent access to the underlying short PRS.

</details>


### [219] [Solving Sparse SDPs in Sublinear Time: A Classical Algorithm Inspired by the Quantum OR Lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40302)
*Fernando G. S. L. Brandão,Alexander M. Dalzell,András Gilyén,Francisca Vasconcelos*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出首个在有限半径区间内、无需低秩假设的稀疏半定规划经典亚线性时间求解器，通过经典实现量子OR引理的样本复用机制，达到与量子算法相匹配的复杂度，并证明通用稀疏SDP求解不存在超二次量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前稀疏SDP的亚线性时间算法仅有量子算法（Brandão等2019、van Apeldoorn与Gilyén 2019），达到Õ(√n+√m)复杂度；而经典方法需Õ(mn)。量子加速的关键在于Quantum OR引理的样本复用机制，作者欲探究该机制能否被经典方法实现，从而给出经典亚线性求解器。

Method: 1) 提出同时估计稀疏哈密顿量Gibbs态下大量期望值的经典过程，结合随机化Lánczos滤波与高效采样估计器；2) 引入用于SDP求解的随机在线学习框架，改善对精度的依赖；3) 经典实现Quantum OR引理的样本复用，解耦Gibbs态制备与约束搜索成本。

Result: 设s为输入矩阵稀疏度，γ:=Rr/ε刻画原始(R)与对偶(r)半径及精度(ε)。当γ²≤min{m,n/s}时，求解器耗时Õ(nsγ^{4.5}+msγ²)；γ=O(1)时为Õ((n+m)s)，相对于O(mns)输入规模为亚线性。该结果在m与n上匹配已知下界（至对数因子）。

Conclusion: 该经典算法在维数m、n上匹配量子算法的下界，表明对于通用稀疏SDP求解不存在超二次量子优势；量子加速仅为“类Grover”平方级，且可被经典方法复现。

Abstract: We give the first sublinear-time classical solvers for sparse semidefinite programs in the bounded-radius regime, without low-rank assumptions or Frobenius norm dependence on the constraint matrices. For constant precision and bounded primal and dual radii, prior quantum algorithms of Brandão et al. (2019) and van Apeldoorn and Gilyén (2019) achieved $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{m})$ dependence on matrix dimension $n$ and constraint number $m$. Compared with the $\widetilde{O}(mn)$ runtime of existing classical methods, this suggests a quartic quantum speedup when $m \approx n$. Beyond a usual Grover speedup, this separation relies on the Quantum OR lemma, whose sample-reuse mechanism decouples the cost of Gibbs-state preparation from constraint search. We show that this reuse mechanism is classically realizable for sparse SDPs.
  Our main technical contribution is a classical procedure for simultaneously estimating many expectation values with respect to a sparse Hamiltonian's Gibbs state. This combines randomized Lánczos filtering with an efficient sampling-based estimator. We also introduce a stochastic online-learning framework for SDP solving, substantially improving accuracy-dependence over standard oracle-based MMWU approaches. Let $s$ denote the the input matrix sparsity and $γ:=Rr/\varepsilon$ capture dependence on the primal $(R)$ and dual $(r)$ radii as well as target accuracy $(\varepsilon)$. When $γ^2\leq\min\{m,n/s\}$, our solver runs in time $\widetilde{O}\left(nsγ^{4.5}+msγ^2\right)$. For $γ=O(1)$, this is $\widetilde{O}\left((n+m)s\right)$ and sublinear in the $O(mns)$ input size. Similar to the quantum algorithms, this matches known lower bounds with respect to $m$ and $n$, up to logarithmic factors. This implies that, with respect to dimensions $m$ and $n$, there is no super-quadratic quantum advantage for generic sparse SDP solving.

</details>


### [220] [A fully Gaussian quantum Stein's lemma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40307)
*Filippo Girardi,Jacopo Rizzo,Leah Turner,Gerardo Adesso,Ludovico Lami*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究多模玻色高斯态在任意集体高斯测量下的渐近非对称假设检验，为多对态给出Stein指数的单字母公式，包含一阶矩与二阶矩两项贡献；指出高斯测量相对熵具有真正的非可加性，但可加性破坏的唯一渐近效应是解耦一阶与二阶矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子信息中受限测量下量子态的渐近可区分性是核心而神秘的问题，尤其是高斯态在集体高斯测量下的Stein指数缺乏单字母刻画。

Method: 针对多模玻色高斯态与任意集体高斯测量，建立渐近非对称假设检验框架；通过新颖的对数行列式矩阵不等式给出二阶矩项可加性的充分条件。

Result: 对许多态对证明Stein指数单字母公式，明确一阶矩与二阶矩两项贡献；证明高斯测量相对熵存在真正非可加性，其唯一渐近效应是解耦一阶与二阶矩；并排除中心高斯态下集体高斯测量带来的任何可能优势。

Conclusion: 在广泛实际情形中给出高斯Stein指数的单字母表达，并证明中心高斯态下集体高斯测量无额外优势。

Abstract: A central yet mysterious problem in quantum information is the asymptotic distinguishability of quantum states under restricted measurements. Here we look at asymptotic asymmetric hypothesis testing for general multi-mode bosonic Gaussian states restricted to arbitrary collective Gaussian measurements. We establish a single-letter formula for the Stein exponent for many pairs of states, featuring two distinct contributions, corresponding to first and second moments, respectively. Remarkably, the Gaussian-measured relative entropy exhibits genuine non-additivity, but the only effect of additivity violations is to asymptotically decouple first and second moments. We prove this by establishing sufficient conditions for additivity of the second-moment term via novel log-determinant matrix inequalities. Our results not only identify a single-letter expression for the Gaussian Stein exponent in a wide range of practical situations, but also rule out any possible advantage from collective Gaussian measurements when considering centred Gaussian states.

</details>


### [221] [Decoupling of the QAOA into independent spin-boson systems and high-depth performance on pure and mixed spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40309)
*Sami Boulebnane,Abid Khan,Pragna Subrahmanya,Dylan Herman,Edward Farhi,Benjamin Villalonga,Ruslan Shaydulin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明在混合稠密自旋玻璃上计算QAOA能量时，自旋近似解耦为独立的spin-boson系统，无穷尺寸极限下解耦严格成立，从而给出可用张量网络低成本执行的递归算法，实现对高深度QAOA的优化与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QAOA被视为近期量子优势的有力候选，但其大规模研究能力受限。已有的精确递归公式虽可预测大规模自旋玻璃上的QAOA性能，计算成本却随QAOA层数指数增长，无法研究高深度这一有前景的区域。

Method: 作者提出广义spin-boson映射：对任意混合稠密自旋玻璃问题，计算QAOA能量时自旋近似解耦为独立spin-boson系统，在无穷尺寸极限下解耦精确成立。基于该映射导出计算QAOA能量的递归过程，并用张量网络以适中成本执行。

Result: 在先前技术无法处理的深度上，对纯自旋玻璃和混合自旋玻璃进行QAOA优化；观察到度数更高的问题需要更多层才能达到相近近似比，同时角度优化变得更困难。

Conclusion: spin-boson映射为QAOA的多体物理提供了自然框架，使大尺寸、高深度的QAOA能量评估成为可能；但解耦并不能实现QAOA的强模拟，仍需量子计算机采样对应预测能量的比特串。

Abstract: The Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) is regarded as a promising candidate for near-term quantum advantage in combinatorial optimization, yet our ability to study it at scale is limited. Exact recursive formulas have been introduced for predicting QAOA performance on large spin glasses, but the computational cost of evaluating them grows exponentially with the number of QAOA layers, preventing the study of QAOA in the promising high-depth regime. In this work, we show that for the task of computing QAOA energy on any mixed dense spin glass problem, spins approximately decouple into independent spin-boson systems. In the infinite-size limit, the decoupling becomes exact, establishing the spin-boson mapping as a natural framework for the many-body physics of QAOA. This generalized spin-boson mapping gives a recursive procedure for computing QAOA energies that can be executed at modest cost using tensor networks. As a numerical application, we optimize QAOA on pure and mixed spin glasses at depths intractable for prior techniques, observing that higher-degree problems require more layers for a comparable approximation ratio while angle optimization becomes more challenging. While decoupling enables the evaluations of QAOA energies at large size and depth, it does not enable strong simulation of QAOA; a quantum computer is required to sample the bitstring corresponding to the predicted energy.

</details>


### [222] [Planted Cliques and Quantum Symmetry-Adapted Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40310)
*Vojtech Havlicek,Jordan Docter,Subhash Khot*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究经典样本的两种量子编码（二进制相位态编码与对称适配测量）是否为植入团检测保留足够信息。二进制相位态编码需Ω(n^{1+2ε}ln²n)份样本才能常数优势检测，弱Schur采样仅依赖边数而失效，但保留表示标签与Specht寄存器后距离为1-o(1)；单个相干量子样本可实现高效区分器，在量子植入团硬度假设下给出与单经典样本的条件性计算分离。检测效率问题仍开放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 植入团问题具有显著的计算-统计间隙与经典硬度证据，是量子优势的候选问题。作者希望考察经典样本的量子编码是否保留足够信息以支持植入团检测，并探讨其算法效率潜力。

Method: 研究两种量子编码：一是自然的二进制相位态编码并允许任意联合测量，进行样本复杂度下界与上界分析；二是由Schur变换自然产生的对称适配测量，分析弱Schur采样结果分布及弱Schur采样后保留表示标签与Specht寄存器的性质。此外分析单个相干量子样本下的区分器构造，并给出条件性计算分离。

Result: 二进制相位态编码下常数优势检测需Ω(n^{1+2ε}ln²n)份样本，即使任意联合测量亦然；统计上约Õ(n²)份样本可超过对数团阈值。弱Schur采样结果分布仅通过边数依赖图，无法区分分布；但保留表示标签与Specht寄存器并丢弃重数后距离为1-o(1)，即使再丢弃标签也保持近完美可区分性。给出保留态的具体形式作为高效测量目标。单个相干量子样本可实现高效量子区分器，在量子植入团硬度假设下获得与单经典样本的条件性计算分离。

Conclusion: 结果为结构性及信息论性质；在猜想困难区间内由单个经典图实现高效检测仍为开放问题。

Abstract: The planted clique problem is a promising candidate for quantum advantage with a wide computational-statistical gap and substantial evidence for classical hardness. We study two quantum encodings of classical samples, a natural binary phase state encoding and symmetry-adapted measurements, and determine if they preserve enough information for planted-clique detection, as well as discuss their potential towards algorithmic efficiency. For the binary phase state encoding, we show that constant-advantage detection requires $Ω(n^{1+2\varepsilon}\ln^2 n)$ copies, even under arbitrary joint measurements. Measurements on $\tilde{O}(n^2)$ copies suffice statistically above the logarithmic clique threshold. The symmetry-adapted measurements on the full graph register arise naturally from the Schur transform. We show that the outcome distribution of weak Schur sampling depends on the sampled graph only through its edge count and fails to distinguish the distributions; whereas retaining the representation label and Specht register after discarding multiplicity preserves distance $1-o(1)$. Near-perfect distinguishability survives even if the label is also discarded. We calculate the retained states, providing concrete targets for efficient measurement. Finally, we show that one supplied coherent quantum sample enables an efficient quantum distinguisher, which yields a conditional computational separation from one classical sample under quantum planted-clique hardness. Our results are structural and information-theoretic; efficient detection from one classical graph in the conjectured hard regime remains open.

</details>


### [223] [Explicit Capacity-Achieving Quantum LDPC Codes List Decodable in Near-linear Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40313)
*William Gay,Fernando Granha Jeronimo,Abhi Shukul*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出达到列表译码容量且具备LDPC性质的显式量子码构造，译码半径趋近量子Singleton界且列表规模为常数，并给出近线性时间的列表译码算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典编码理论中，显式达到列表译码容量的码是长期目标；量子编码理论中，除"好码"外还亟需LDPC性质。两者结合——显式、达到列表译码容量且LDPC的量子码——此前未被实现，本文填补这一空白。

Method: 基于扩展图的量子AEL放大（Alon-Edmonds-Luby放大的量子类比），构造显式量子LDPC码；并将经典基于扩展图的弱正则性列表译码算法推广到合适的量子AEL实例上，得到高效译码算法。

Result: 给出首个显式量子LDPC码族，其列表译码半径趋近量子Singleton界，且列表规模为常数；同时提供近线性（正比于码长）时间的列表译码算法，性能趋近容量。

Conclusion: 显式量子LDPC码可在列表译码意义下达到容量，扩展图与量子AEL放大是实现LDPC性质的关键工具，经典弱正则性译码技术可成功迁移至量子情形。

Abstract: In classical coding theory, the quest for explicit codes achieving list decoding capacity has been an important driving force. While random codes are easily shown to achieve capacity, an explicit construction was only discovered decades later in the seminal work [Guruswami and Rudra, STOC 2006]. In quantum coding theory, it is highly desirable that a code family be LDPC. While good quantum codes were known for decades, obtaining the additional LDPC property was elusive. In fact, only very recently that good quantum LDPC codes were discovered in a breakthrough work [Panteleev and Kalachev, STOC 2022].
  In this context, a natural question is to ask for an explicit family of quantum codes achieving list decoding capacity while also possessing the important LDPC property. In this work, we provide (to the best of our knowledge) the first explicit constructions of quantum LDPC codes achieving list decoding capacity, namely, with a list decoding radius approaching the quantum Singleton bound with constant list sizes. Furthermore, we provide near-linear time (in the block-length) list decoding algorithms approaching capacity.
  Our explicit code construction are based on expander graphs via the quantum analogue of Alon-Edmonds-Luby (AEL) amplification [Bergamaschi, Golowich and Gunn, STOC 2024], and this enables the important LDPC property. Our efficient list decoding algorithms are obtained by generalizing the classical expander-based weak-regularity list decoding algorithms [Srivastava and Tulsiani, FOCS 2025] [Jeronimo and Singh, 2025] to suitable instantiations of quantum AEL.

</details>


### [224] [Quantum Sampling of Random Spanning Trees via Amortized Data Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40314)
*Yassine Hamoudi,Adrian Tanasa,Shrinidhi Teganahally Sridhara*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种量子算法，用亚线性查询次数在图上的生成树均匀叠加态（q-sample）采样，并证明其近似最优；同时给出计数生成树与寻找标记生成树的更快量子游走算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作多聚焦于生成经典样本（如Apers、Gao、Ji、Liu [ICALP 2025]的量子算法），而本文旨在生成生成树的均匀叠加态（q-sample），并在预处理与单次采样代价上超越经典最优算法（Anari、Liu、Vuong [FOCS 2022]，需 Õ(m) 预处理、每次采样 Õ(n) 操作）。

Method: 通过在一系列缓慢变化的马尔可夫链上进行量子游走采样；每条链为各向同化的 up-down 游走，能快速混合到输入图的生成树分布。关键组件是一个摊销数据结构，支持在整条序列中快速实现相应的量子游走算子，该结构维护随底层图演化的谱稀疏器与杠杆分数采样器。

Result: 对 n 点 m 边图，预处理 Õ(√(mn) + m^{1-δ})，之后每次 q-sample 代价 Õ(n^{1+2δ})（任意 δ）；或 k 个独立 q-sample 总代价 Õ(√(kmn))。并证明了对数因子内的匹配下界，表明算法本质最优；论文还给出更多权衡。

Conclusion: 所提量子算法在生成树 q-sample 上达到本质最优，并可导出更快的基于量子游走的生成树计数与寻找标记生成树算法。

Abstract: We present a quantum algorithm that generates a uniform superposition over the spanning trees of a graph -- also known as a q-sample -- using only a sub-linear number of queries to the graph. This goes beyond previous work that focuses on generating classical samples, including a recent quantum algorithm by Apers, Gao, Ji, and Liu [ICALP 2025]. For an $n$-vertex, $m$-edge graph, our algorithm admits a pre-processing step of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{mn} + m^{1-δ})$, after which each q-sample can be generated in $\tilde{O}(n^{1+2δ})$ for any $δ$. Alternatively, $k$ independent q-samples can be generated at a total cost of $\tilde{O}(\sqrt{kmn})$. We also prove a matching lower bound up to logarithmic factors, showing that our algorithm is essentially optimal. Further tradeoffs are given in the paper. In comparison, the optimal classical algorithms (Anari, Liu and Vuong [FOCS 2022]) need an $\tilde{O}(m)$ pre-processing step, after which each sample costs $\tilde{O}(n)$ operations. These results yield faster quantum walk-based algorithms for counting spanning trees or finding a marked one.
  Our result is obtained via quantum walk sampling over a sequence of slowly-changing Markov chains. Each chain is an isotropized up-down walk that mixes rapidly to the spanning tree distribution of the input graph. A key ingredient is an amortized data structure supporting fast implementation of the associated quantum walk operators throughout the sequence. This data structure maintains access to a spectral sparsifier and a leverage score sampler that evolve with the underlying graph.

</details>


### [225] [Local Automorphism-Aware Syndrome Compilation for General Quantum LDPC Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40319)
*Eugenio Durazo Rocha,Olai Å. Mostad,Hsuan-Yin Lin,Eirik Rosnes*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将CSS量子LDPC码的低深度校验提取建模为带量子奇偶约束的有序边染色问题，提出LocalASC方法，通过自同构子群将约束约化到边轨道变量并提升回全图，在满足度条件的双块CSS码与量子Tanner码上获得了深度最优调度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低深度校验提取是CSS量子LDPC码的关键，可形式化为有序边染色问题。但现有边染色只保证每层每个比特至多参与一个双比特门，无法保证X/Z校验测量的有效交错；且已知6层度下界在IBM双变量自行车码上不可达，需探究该不可达是否普遍。

Method: 提出LocalASC（局部自同构感知校验编译）：在Tanner图自同构子群下将约束系统约化为边轨道变量，并把每个可行轨道分配提升到全图；同时为阿贝尔群上的双块群代数CSS码构造平移子群，并给出方复形对称性扩展为Tanner图自同构的条件。

Result: 在最大校验权重等于Tanner图最大度的码实例中，LocalASC为若干奇分量权重（含不等奇权重）的双块CSS码找到深度最优校验提取调度，并为满足同度条件的若干量子Tanner码给出达到下界的调度；同时说明6层度下界在已发表权重6的IBM双变量自行车码上不可达，但在双块CSS码中并非普遍。

Conclusion: LocalASC能有效利用局部自同构获得深度最优的综合征提取调度，表明6层下界的不可达性并非普遍现象。

Abstract: Low-depth syndrome extraction for Calderbank-Shor-Steane (CSS) quantum low-density parity-check codes can be formulated as a proper ordered edge-coloring problem subject to quantum parity constraints. A proper edge-coloring of the CSS Tanner graph ensures that each data or ancilla qubit participates in at most one two-qubit gate per layer, but does not guarantee a valid interleaving of the X- and Z-check measurements as for every overlapping X/Z check pair, the number of shared data qubits on which the X interaction precedes the Z interaction must be even. The minimum number of colors in a proper ordered edge-coloring satisfying the quantum parity constraints equals the minimum two-qubit depth when each stabilizer check is measured with a single ancilla.
  We introduce local automorphism-aware syndrome compilation (LocalASC), which reduces the constraint system to edge-orbit variables under a subgroup of the Tanner graph automorphisms and lifts each feasible orbit assignment to the full graph. Although the $6$-layer degree lower bound is unattainable for the published weight-$6$ IBM bivariate bicycle codes, we show that this is not universal among two-block CSS codes. Among code instances for which the maximum check weight equals the maximum Tanner graph degree, LocalASC finds depth-optimal syndrome-extraction schedules for several two-block CSS codes with odd component weights, including instances with unequal odd weights. We also obtain lower-bound-saturating syndrome-extraction schedules for several quantum Tanner codes satisfying the same degree condition. To obtain the subgroups used by LocalASC without computing the full automorphism group of the Tanner graph, we construct translation subgroups for two-block group-algebra CSS codes over abelian groups. For quantum Tanner codes, we give conditions under which square-complex symmetries extend to Tanner graph automorphisms.

</details>


### [226] [Exponential quantum speedup for $\mathbb{F}_3^n$-Subset-Sum? Or, rigorous classical algorithms for Binary-Error LWE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40321)
*Robin Kothari,Tony Metger,Ryan O'Donnell,Noah Shutty,Kewen Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在Chen-Liu-Zhandry量子框架下，给出了F_3^n向量子集和问题的量子算法，对任意固定ε>0，仅需m=ε·n²个向量即可多项式时间求解，重新确立了指数级量子加速的可能性；关键技术是binary-error LWE的确定性经典算法及样本-时间权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向量子集和问题在m较小时越难求解。此前Chen-Liu-Zhandry (EUROCRYPT'22)给出m≈n²/2的量子算法，随后Kothari-O'Donnell-Wu (STOC'2026)给出m≈n²/3的经典算法，消除了该参数区间内指数级量子优势的希望。本文旨在重新证明量子加速的存在性。

Method: 沿用Chen-Liu-Zhandry框架设计量子算法。核心构件是一个针对binary-error Learning-with-Errors (LWE)问题的确定性经典算法，并为其严格建立此前仅由代数启发式预测的样本-时间权衡。

Result: 对任意固定ε>0，量子算法以m=ε·n²个向量在多项式时间内求解F_3子集和问题；并建立了在指数时间与多项式时间之间插值的完整样本-时间权衡。此外，对更大域上的向量子集和，显著改进了Kothari-O'Donnell-Wu (STOC'2026)的经典算法。

Conclusion: 本文表明在m=ε·n²的更少样本区间，量子算法可多项式时间求解F_3子集和，从而恢复指数级量子加速的可能性，并调和了此前量子与经典算法的界限。

Abstract: We study vector subset sum over $\mathbb{F}_3^n$: given $m$ random vectors from $\mathbb{F}_3^n$, find a nonempty subset that sums to zero; the smaller $m$, the more difficult it is to find such a subset. Chen, Liu, and Zhandry (EUROCRYPT'22) introduced an efficient quantum algorithm that solves this problem when $m\approx n^2/2$, where a naive classical algorithm would require exponential time. Subsequently, Kothari, O'Donnell, and Wu (STOC'2026) gave an efficient classical algorithm that only requires $m \approx n^2/3$ vectors, thus removing the hope for an exponential quantum advantage in this parameter regime.
  Using the framework of Chen, Liu, and Zhandry, we give quantum algorithms that require much fewer input vectors, renewing the possibility of an exponential quantum speedup: for any fixed $ε>0$, our quantum algorithm solves $\mathbb{F}_3$-subset sum in polynomial time with $m=ε\cdot n^2$ vectors. More generally, we establish a full sample--time tradeoff that interpolates between exponential and polynomial runtime.
  The main ingredient is a deterministic classical algorithm for the binary-error Learning-with-Errors problem, which is of independent cryptographic interest. For this, we rigorously establish a sample--time tradeoff that was predicted by earlier algebraic heuristics. For vector subset sums over larger fields, we also significantly improve classical algorithms in Kothari, O'Donnell, and Wu (STOC'2026).

</details>


### [227] [Computational Work Extraction: The Complexity of Catalysts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40323)
*Atul Singh Arora,Shantanav Chakraborty,Alexandru Cojocaru,Sreyas Saminathan,Uttam Singh*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子系统存在Θ(n)的ergotropy（可提取功），但任何高效过程提取的功可忽略；并在随机预言机模型（及基于量子安全伪随机函数的明文模型）下给出无条件的存在性分离与显式构造。核心工具是将ergotropy与催化计算的复杂性建立联系，通过量子空间受限算法的查询下界证明催化剂的"阈值效应"（λ个催化剂可解、cλ（c<1）不可解）。催化剂因此可在计算ergotropy中实现高效全量提取，并引出伪ergotropy概念，同时不改变信息论ergotropy。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典热力学中ergotropy衡量可从量子态提取的最大功，但"最大功可提取"与"高效过程实际能提取多少功"之间存在鸿沟。作者希望在计算复杂性视角下刻画这一差距：是否存在态具有大量ergotropy，却在所有高效（多项式时间）过程中只能提取可忽略的功？同时探索催化剂（catalyst）这一辅助系统在功提取中的作用，并厘清计算ergotropy与信息论ergotropy的区别。

Method: 1. 建立ergotropy与催化计算复杂性的联系：将功提取问题归约为催化辅助的态制备问题。2. 在随机预言机模型下构造关系问题与判定问题，证明其可用λ个催化剂高效求解，但不能用cλ（c<1）个催化剂求解。3. 证明量子空间受限算法的查询下界，从而得到催化剂数量的严格阈值下界。4. 用经典态与哈密顿量（含单比特项）构造显式分离实例。5. 借助量子安全伪随机函数将分离从随机预言机模型推广到明文模型。6. 引入并实例化伪ergotropy概念，类比伪随机性。7. 分析催化剂对信息论ergotropy无影响，并讨论经典ergotropy的对应结论及量子性证明协议对经典/量子催化ergotropy的分离。

Result: - 证明n比特系统可具有Θ(n) ergotropy，而所有高效过程提取的功可忽略（即使哈密顿量仅由单比特项构成）。- 在随机预言机模型下无条件建立存在性分离并给出显式构造；在量子安全伪随机函数假设下推广至明文模型。- 证明催化计算中催化剂数量的严格阈值：λ个催化剂可高效求解关系/判定问题，cλ（c<1）个则不可。- 发现催化剂在计算ergotropy中异常强大：存在一族哈密顿量与态，催化剂可实现Θ(n)全量ergotropy的高效提取，而非催化高效过程仅能提取可忽略功。- 引入并实例化伪ergotropy概念。- 证明催化剂不改变信息论ergotropy。- 构造主要基于经典态与哈密顿量，因此经典ergotropy有类似结论；并证明某些量子性证明协议可通用地区分经典与量子催化ergotropy。

Conclusion: 本文在计算复杂性框架下揭示了ergotropy的可提取性与高效可计算性之间的本质分离：大ergotropy未必意味着高效可提取。催化剂作为辅助资源在此分离中起关键作用，其数量存在严格阈值，并催生了伪ergotropy这一新概念。工作同时表明经典ergotropy具有类似现象，且催化ergotropy可作为区分经典与量子计算能力的工具，为热力学资源与计算复杂性之间架起桥梁。

Abstract: We prove maximal separations: $n$-qubit systems can have $Θ(n)$ ergotropy, while every efficient process extracts negligible work, even for Hamiltonians consisting of single-qubit terms. We establish an unconditional existential separation and give an explicit construction in the random oracle model. Assuming the existence of quantum-secure pseudorandom functions, this separation extends to the plain model.
  This work uncovers an important connection between ergotropy and the complexity of catalytic computation---computation where auxiliary qubits must be finally restored to their initial state. Relative to a random oracle, we establish relational and decision problems that: (i) can be solved efficiently with $λ$ catalysts; but (ii) cannot be solved by any algorithm with $cλ$ catalysts, for any $c<1$. We show this by proving query lower bounds for quantum-space bounded algorithms.
  As a consequence, for computational ergotropy, catalysts prove to be surprisingly powerful---there is a family of Hamiltonians and states for which catalysts enable efficient extraction of the full $Θ(n)$ ergotropy, while every efficient non-catalytic process extracts negligible work. Furthermore, catalysts also allow us to introduce and instantiate the notion of pseudoergotropy---analogous to pseudorandomness. On the other hand, we show catalysts do not change (information-theoretic) ergotropy.
  Finally, our work also sheds light on the classical aspect of the problem. First, most of our constructions rely on classical states and Hamiltonians and therefore imply analogous results for classical ergotropy. Second, we show that certain proof of quantumness protocols can be used to generically separate classical and quantum catalytic ergotropy.

</details>


### [228] [Quantum Enhanced Deep Reinforcement Learning for Distribution Network Reconfiguration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40327)
*Daniel Germain,Mason Blanchard,Audrey Versteegen,Nora Bauer,Jonathan Mei,Claudio Girotto,Paul Smith,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出深度强化学习框架用于静态配电网重构（DNR），在受控设置下系统比较不同Q网络架构对解质量与学习性能的影响，发现量子增强Q网络以更少参数获得更高奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能电网积累了丰富的运行数据，并部署了更多远程可控开关设备，为数据驱动的配电网重构创造了新机遇。DNR通过优化拓扑提升效率、降低网损并维持运行约束，但最优开关组合搜索是计算代价高昂的组合问题，因此需要研究网络架构对求解质量与学习性能的影响。

Method: 提出面向静态DNR的深度强化学习（DRL）框架。在受控的DRL实验设置中，仅改变Q网络架构，保持其他因素不变，以隔离各架构选择的贡献，并对比包括量子增强Q网络在内的多种架构。

Result: 量子增强Q网络相比经典对应架构，能够以更少的参数量获得更高的奖励。

Conclusion: 量子增强Q网络是组合型电力系统优化（如静态DNR）的有前景方向；同时表明Q网络架构选择对DRL求解质量与学习性能具有显著影响。

Abstract: Modern smart grids generate increasingly rich operational data and deploy greater numbers of remotely controlled switching assets than ever before, creating new opportunities for data-driven distribution network reconfiguration (DNR). DNR optimizes grid topology to improve efficiency, reduce power losses, and maintain operational constraints, but finding optimal switch configurations remains a computationally demanding combinatorial problem. In this paper, we propose a deep reinforcement learning (DRL) framework for static DNR to systematically investigate how the Q-network architecture affects both solution quality and learning performance. We evaluate multiple architectures within a controlled DRL framework, varying only the Q-network to isolate the contribution of each architectural choice. Our results show that quantum-enhanced Q-networks can achieve higher rewards with fewer parameters than their classical counterpart, suggesting that such architectures are a promising direction for combinatorial power systems optimization.

</details>


### [229] [Bath-assisted cooling without resets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40328)
*Xie-Hang Yu,Zherui Chen,Lin Lin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种单次相干浴接触的冷却机制，将输入熵转移至随后退耦的浴自由度中，使系统沿基态路径演化。对一类弱相互作用、有能隙、局域相互作用的费米子系统严格实现该机制：从任意混合态出发，在系统尺寸与逆全局迹范数误差的多对数物理时间内制备相互作用基态，仅需每个系统模式两个初始为空的浴模式以及一条共享脉冲，且无需知道微观参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统冷却需反复重置浴或持续耗散，效率与可行性受限；核心问题是：能否用单次相干浴接触、无需反复重置浴，就把任意混合态的熵转移出去并制备纯基态，同时保持系统哈密顿量全程开启且不修改。

Method: 设计非马尔可夫系统-浴动力学冷却机制：把输入信息转移到随后退耦的浴自由度，令系统与剩余浴沿基态路径演化，浴仅在末尾丢弃；对弱相互作用、有能隙、局域费米子系统给出严格构造，每个系统模式配两个初始空浴模式，所有在位系统-浴耦合共享一条脉冲，仅在最后一刻丢弃浴。

Result: 从任意混合态出发，协议在关于系统尺寸与逆全局迹范数误差均为多对数的总物理时间内制备相互作用基态；同一脉冲无需知道各容许系统的微观参数即可制备其各自基态；保证来自非马尔可夫系统-浴动力学，不存在有效Lindbladian描述。

Conclusion: 证明单次相干浴接触即可实现高效冷却，无需反复重置浴；该机制在一类有能隙局域费米子系统中严格成立，且依赖本质非马尔可夫的动力学，为无需微观参数、哈密顿量全程开启的通用基态制备提供新途径。

Abstract: Cooling an arbitrary mixed state to a pure ground state requires transferring its entropy to an environment. Can a single coherent bath contact accomplish this without repeated bath resets? We develop a new cooling mechanism that transfers the input information into bath degrees of freedom that subsequently decouple, while the system and the remaining bath follow a ground-state path. The bath is discarded only at the end, and the system Hamiltonian remains on and unmodified throughout. We rigorously realize this mechanism for a class of weakly interacting, gapped fermionic systems with local interactions. Starting from any mixed state, our protocol prepares the interacting ground state in total physical time polylogarithmic in the system size and inverse global trace-norm error. It uses two initially empty bath modes per system mode and a single pulse shared across all onsite system--bath couplings. The same pulse prepares each admissible system's own ground state without knowledge of its microscopic parameters. The guarantees follow directly from a non-Markovian system--bath dynamics without an effective Lindbladian description.

</details>


### [230] [Fast Quantum Algorithms for Learning Linear Threshold Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40331)
*Aleksandrs Krivcenko,Tuyen Nguyen,Ronald de Wolf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了线性阈值函数（LTF）的学习问题，并在三个不同设定下给出了量子算法，相比经典算法实现了显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性阈值函数是机器学习中的核心对象，用于线性判别正负样本。经典算法在学习LTF时需要大量查询，作者希望利用量子算法在查询复杂度和门复杂度上取得突破。

Method: 作者提出了三类量子学习算法：1. 在实数域查询模型下，使用O(log(n/ε))个成员查询和Õ(n)个其他门学习一般LTF，并分析高斯分布下的误差；2. 针对支持集大小k且权重相同的齐次LTF（即Majority函数），使用O(log k)个查询精确识别隐藏支持集；3. 在较弱的量子样本访问模型下（可通过酉算子U产生高斯测度下的量子样本），基于Jain等人的高效Hermite变换，使用O(n^{1/4}/√ε)次U和U†应用及Õ(n²/ε⁴)个其他门学习齐次LTF。

Result: 1. 实数域查询下，量子算法以O(log(n/ε))次成员查询学习LTF至欧氏误差ε，而经典算法需Ω(n log(1/ε))次查询；2. 对稀疏齐次LTF，查询复杂度从Belovs的O(k^{1/4})指数级改进为O(log k)；3. 在量子样本模型下，以O(n^{1/4}/√ε)次量子示例查询和Õ(n²/ε⁴)个其他门学习齐次LTF至误差ε。

Conclusion: 论文表明量子算法在多种访问模型下均能显著降低线性阈值函数学习的查询复杂度，尤其对稀疏情形实现了指数级加速，并在较弱的量子样本模型下依然能高效学习齐次LTF。

Abstract: Linear threshold functions are $f_{w,θ}(x)=\text{sign}(\langle x,w \rangle -θ)$, where the weight vector $w\in\mathbb{R}^n$ is a unit vector, $θ\in\mathbb R$ is a threshold, and typically $x\in\mathbb{R}^n$ or $x\in\{-1,1\}^n$. When $θ=0$, the LTF is called homogeneous, and we write $f_w:=f_{w,0}$. Such functions are among the most important objects in machine learning, since they serve to linearly discriminate positive and negative examples. We give three positive results about learning LTFs: 1. Suppose we can make real-domain queries, meaning we can compute $f_{w,θ}(x)$ at any $x\in\mathbb{R}^n$ of our choice. We give a quantum algorithm that learns $f_{w,θ}$ up to Euclidean error~$ε$ using $O(\log(n/ε))$ membership queries and $\widetilde{O}(n)$ other gates. Then we have also learned $f_{w,θ}$ up to error $O(ε)$ when $x$ is Gaussian. Classical algorithms need $Ω(n\log(1/ε))$ queries. 2. A homogeneous LTF $f_w$ on domain $\{-1,1\}^n$ where $w$ has only $k$ nonzero entries of the same value, is the Majority function on the support of $w$. Belovs gave a bounded-error quantum algorithm that identifies the hidden support exactly (and hence learns $f_w$) using $O(k^{1/4})$ queries. We give an exponential improvement, using $O(\log k)$ queries. 3. Suppose we have a unitary $U$ that can produce (discretized) \emph{quantum examples} under Gaussian measure, corresponding to $\int_x \sqrt{γ_n(x)}|x\rangle |f_w(x)\rangle dx$. This is a weaker access model than membership queries. We give a quantum algorithm based on the efficient \emph{Hermite transform} of Jain et al.\ to learn homogeneous LTFs~$f_w$ with error~$ε$ under the Gaussian distribution, using $O(n^{1/4}/\sqrtε)$ applications of $U$ and $U^\dagger$ and $\widetilde{O}(n^2/ε^4)$ other gates.

</details>


### [231] [Tight Universal Bounds on Quantum Data Hiding with Multipartite Werner States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40332)
*Oren Akresh,Jacob Beckey,Felix Leditzky*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文确定了Werner态量子数据隐藏方案的最优安全性依赖，证明任意n-qudit Werner态对在PPT测量下的区分偏差为O(n²/d)，并给出达到该标度的显式构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eggeling与Werner提出的基于Werner态的多体隐藏方案，其统一安全性保证对粒子数和局域维度的最优依赖关系在二十多年间一直未解决。本文旨在解决这一悬而未决的问题。

Method: 将区分偏差归结为偏转置算子的迹范数，并利用混合Schur-Weyl对偶性进行分析，借鉴了为基于端口的量子隐形传态所发展的表示论方法；同时构造了仅使用非自适应局域测量的显式Werner态对。

Result: 证明任意n-qudit Werner态对在每个二分划下PPT测量效应的区分偏差为O(n²/d)；显式构造达到该标度，确立了最坏情况最优性，并表明PPT松弛保持了在粒子数与局域维度上的最优依赖。在固定安全性下，将可认证隐藏区域从n=O(d^{1/4})扩展到n=O(√d)。

Conclusion: 该界表明用自适应单拷贝测量检验任何非平凡酉不变性质需要Ω(√d)个拷贝，从而对任何在集体测量下具有维度无关样本复杂度的性质给出分离；展示了表示论方法在数据隐藏与量子性质检验中的实用价值。

Abstract: Quantum data hiding concerns pairs of states which are highly distinguishable with global measurements, yet nearly indistinguishable when restricted to local operations and classical communication. More than two decades after Eggeling and Werner introduced a multipartite hiding scheme based on Werner states, the optimal dependence of its uniform security guarantee on the number of parties and local dimension remained unknown. We resolve this problem by showing that the distinguishing bias of any pair of $n$-qudit Werner states is $O(n^2/d)$ under measurements whose effects remain positive under partial transposition (PPT) across every bipartition. An explicit pair attains this scaling using only nonadaptive local measurements, establishing worst-case optimality and showing that the PPT relaxation preserves the optimal dependence on both the number of parties and the local dimension. At fixed security, this extends the certified hiding regime from $n=O(d^{1/4})$ to $n=O(\sqrt d)$. Beyond data hiding, the same bound implies that testing any nontrivial unitarily invariant property with adaptive single-copy measurements requires $Ω(\sqrt d)$ copies, yielding separations for any property with dimension-independent sample complexity under collective measurements. Our proof reduces the distinguishing bias to trace norms of partially transposed operators and analyzes them using mixed Schur-Weyl duality, demonstrating the utility of representation theoretic methods developed for port-based teleportation to data hiding and quantum property testing.

</details>


### [232] [Local Relaxation Hierarchies for Quantum Ground State Energies: Convergence Guarantees and Message Passing Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40336)
*Sheng-Ku Lin,Ricardo Rivera Cardoso,Roberto Bondesan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出局部凸松弛层级与高效可并行消息传递算法，用于估计量子多体系统基态能量下界，并给出精确性与收敛性的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸松弛层级能在经典计算机上以多项式时间计算量子多体系统基态能量的下界，但传统求解器计算代价高、效率保证稀缺，难以扩展到大规模系统和精确近似。

Method: 1) 构建局部松弛层级，并设计高效、高度可并行的消息传递算法以估计松弛基态能量；2) 基于成对约化密度矩阵的局部一致性，构造第一层松弛层级；3) 提出基于一致区间的另一层级；4) 基于次梯度法与Nesterov型加速梯度下降（作用于熵平滑目标），给出两种消息传递算法变体，在有界度图上分别以O(n/ε²)和O(n/ε)时间运行。

Result: 1) 证明了层级第一层（基于成对约化密度矩阵局部一致性）对树上的对易哈密顿量是精确的；2) 证明基于一致区间的层级在链上对可分哈密顿量的弱扰动情形下，随区间大小指数收敛到基态能量，从而提供高效经典算法；3) 两种消息传递算法在有界度图上对任意固定层级分别达到O(n/ε²)与O(n/ε)时间复杂度（假设最优消息范数为O(1)，实验中观察到该条件成立）；4) 在多种量子哈密顿量、晶格几何与松弛层级上进行基准测试，验证理论预测并展示优于标准凸优化求解器的潜力。开源库见github.com/rick1924/gse-message-passing。

Conclusion: 局部松弛层级结合消息传递算法为量子多体基态能量下界估计提供了理论上具有精确性/收敛性保证、实践中高效且可并行的新途径，具备超越标准凸优化求解器的潜力。

Abstract: Convex relaxation hierarchies provide lower bounds to the ground state energy of quantum many-body systems that can be computed in polynomial time on a classical computer, at any fixed hierarchy level. However, scaling these methods to large systems and accurate approximations remains challenging due to the computational cost of traditional solvers and the scarcity of efficiency guarantees.
